from openpyxl import load_workbook
import time
import pyodbc
import win32com.client as win32
import tkinter as tk

# ======================================================
# CABLE MAKER - FRIENDLY ERROR POPUP
# Paste BELOW the existing imports in Cable Maker.
# ======================================================
import sys
import traceback
from tkinter import ttk

DB_TRANSACTION_OPEN = False
DB_TRANSACTION_DONE = False
E3_COMPONENT_CREATED = False


def describe_cable_error(error):
    raw = str(error).strip()
    msg = raw.lower()

    if "model check" in msg:
        return ("Excel Model Check Failed",
                "The workbook did not pass its model check in cell F20.",
                "Fix the workbook checks, recalculate and save Excel, then try again.")

    if "layout count" in msg or "part count" in msg:
        return ("Connector Count Mismatch", raw,
                "Check connector parts in B3:B14 against positions in W4:W11 and Z4:Z11.")

    if "duplicate connector" in msg:
        return ("Duplicate Connector", raw,
                "Each connector in the W/Z layout needs a unique designation.")

    if "pin " in msg and "not found on" in msg:
        return ("Connector Pin Not Found", raw,
                "Verify the Excel pin number and the actual physical pin in the E3 connector library.")

    if "connector not found" in msg:
        return ("Connector Part Not Found", raw,
                "Check the part number and its E3 version. If multiple versions exist, set CONNECTOR_VERSION_OVERRIDES.")

    if "could not create component" in msg:
        return ("Cable Assembly Could Not Be Created", raw,
                "Check whether the name/version is already used and whether the cable template exists.")

    if "componentdata" in msg or "active component id" in msg:
        return ("E3 Component Lookup Failed", raw,
                "Check whether E3 saved the new component and whether the database is accessible.")

    if "wire definitions require" in msg:
        return ("Wire Definitions Do Not Match", raw,
                "Check B15/B16, wire section quantities and the L/N/O/Q pinout table.")


    if isinstance(error, IndexError):
        return ("Missing Pinout Rows", "There are fewer usable pinout rows than the wire definitions require.",
                "Check wire section quantities and both sides of the Excel pinout table.")

    if isinstance(error, KeyError):
        return ("Unknown Connector", f"Connector reference not found: {raw}",
                "Check that every connector in L/Q is also listed in W/Z.")

    if isinstance(error, (ValueError, TypeError)):
        return ("Invalid Workbook Value", raw or "A required value is missing or invalid.",
                "Check B15/B16, wire quantities and pin assignments. See Technical Details for the line.")

    if isinstance(error, FileNotFoundError):
        return ("Excel File Not Found", raw,
                "Select an existing workbook and check that it has not been moved.")

    if isinstance(error, PermissionError):
        return ("File Access Denied", raw,
                "Check the file permissions or whether another program has locked the file.\n"
                "Make sure to close the file. If it is on a network drive or onedrive\n"
                "it will make you close it before running.")

    if isinstance(error, pyodbc.Error):
        return ("Database Error", "The SQL Server operation failed.",
                "Check network/database access and your permissions. See Technical Details for SQL's message.")

    if "com_error" in type(error).__name__.lower():
        return ("Zuken E3 Error", "E3/DBE could not complete the requested operation.",
                "Check E3 and DBE status. See Technical Details for the COM error code.")

    return ("Cable Maker Error", raw or "An unexpected error interrupted Cable Maker.",
            "Review the workbook and technical details. Cable Maker has stopped.")


def show_cable_error(error, details, db_note=""):
    title, what, fix = describe_cable_error(error)
    root = tk.Tk()
    root.title("Cable Maker - Error")
    root.geometry("560x340")
    root.resizable(False, False)
    root.attributes("-topmost", True)

    frame = ttk.Frame(root, padding=20)
    frame.pack(fill="both", expand=True)

    ttk.Label(frame, text=title, font=("Segoe UI", 16, "bold"),
              foreground="#B42318").pack(anchor="w", pady=(0, 14))
    ttk.Label(frame, text="What happened:",
              font=("Segoe UI", 10, "bold")).pack(anchor="w")
    ttk.Label(frame, text=what, wraplength=510,
              justify="left").pack(anchor="w", pady=(3, 11))
    ttk.Label(frame, text="Suggested fix:",
              font=("Segoe UI", 10, "bold")).pack(anchor="w")
    ttk.Label(frame, text=fix, wraplength=510,
              justify="left").pack(anchor="w", pady=(3, 10))

    if db_note:
        ttk.Label(frame, text=db_note, wraplength=510,
                  foreground="#92600E").pack(anchor="w", pady=(0, 8))

    buttons = ttk.Frame(frame)
    buttons.pack(side="bottom", fill="x")

    details_frame = ttk.Frame(frame)
    error_text = tk.Text(details_frame, font=("Consolas", 9),
                         wrap="word", height=10)
    scrollbar = ttk.Scrollbar(details_frame, command=error_text.yview)
    error_text.config(yscrollcommand=scrollbar.set)
    scrollbar.pack(side="right", fill="y")
    error_text.pack(side="left", fill="both", expand=True)
    error_text.insert("1.0", details)
    error_text.config(state="disabled")

    expanded = False

    def toggle():
        nonlocal expanded
        expanded = not expanded
        if expanded:
            root.geometry("560x570")
            details_frame.pack(fill="both", expand=True,
                               pady=(8, 12), before=buttons)
            detail_button.config(text="Hide Technical Details")
        else:
            details_frame.pack_forget()
            root.geometry("560x340")
            detail_button.config(text="Show Technical Details")

    def copy_error():
        root.clipboard_clear()
        root.clipboard_append(details)
        root.update()

    detail_button = ttk.Button(buttons, text="Show Technical Details", command=toggle)
    detail_button.pack(side="left")
    ttk.Button(buttons, text="Copy Error", command=copy_error).pack(side="left", padx=8)
    ttk.Button(buttons, text="OK", command=root.destroy).pack(side="right")
    root.protocol("WM_DELETE_WINDOW", root.destroy)
    root.mainloop()


def cable_error_handler(error_type, error, tb):
    if issubclass(error_type, (KeyboardInterrupt, SystemExit)):
        sys.__excepthook__(error_type, error, tb)
        return

    details = "".join(traceback.format_exception(error_type, error, tb))
    print(details, file=sys.stderr)
    note = ""

    connection = globals().get("conn")
    if connection is not None and DB_TRANSACTION_OPEN and not DB_TRANSACTION_DONE:
        try:
            connection.rollback()
            note = "Uncommitted SQL changes were rolled back."
        except Exception as rollback_error:
            note = "WARNING: SQL rollback could not be confirmed."
            details += f"\nRollback error: {rollback_error!r}\n"
        try:
            connection.close()
        except Exception:
            pass

    if E3_COMPONENT_CREATED:
        note += " An E3 component created earlier may still exist."

    try:
        show_cable_error(error, details, note.strip())
    except Exception:
        sys.__excepthook__(error_type, error, tb)


sys.excepthook = cable_error_handler

# ================================================
# HELPER FXN
# ================================================
def make_gui(body):
    root = tk.Tk()
    root.title("Commit changes to database")

    choice = {"value": None}

    def pressed(value):
        choice["value"] = value
        root.quit()

    # Frame with scrollbar
    frame = tk.Frame(root)
    frame.pack(padx=10, pady=10)

    scrollbar = tk.Scrollbar(frame)
    scrollbar.pack(side=tk.RIGHT, fill=tk.Y)

    text = tk.Text(frame, height=20, width=80, wrap="word",
                   yscrollcommand=scrollbar.set)
    text.pack(side=tk.LEFT, fill=tk.BOTH)

    scrollbar.config(command=text.yview)

    text.insert("1.0", body)
    text.config(state="disabled")

    tk.Button(root, text="COMMIT", width=50,
              command=lambda: pressed("YES")).pack(pady=5)
    tk.Button(root, text="ROLLBACK", width=50,
              command=lambda: pressed("NO")).pack(pady=5)

    root.mainloop()
    return choice["value"]



def ask_save_path(default_full_path=""):
    import os
    import tkinter as tk
    from tkinter import filedialog

    default_folder = os.path.dirname(default_full_path)

    # Create Tk root
    root = tk.Tk()
    root.withdraw()
    root.attributes("-topmost", True)
    root.update()

    try:
        # Let user PICK an existing Excel file
        selected_path = filedialog.askopenfilename(
            initialdir=default_folder,
            filetypes=[("Excel Files", "*.xlsx"), ("All Files", "*.*")]
        )
    except Exception:
        return None

    # Cancel or X
    if not selected_path:
        root.destroy()
        return None
    root.destroy()
    # Return ONLY the file name (not full path)
    return os.path.abspath(selected_path)


# ================================================
# PARSE EXCEL
# ================================================
output_path = ask_save_path()
wb = load_workbook(output_path, data_only=True)
ws = wb.active

ASSY_NAME = ws["B1"].value
VERSION = ws["B2"].value

# ======================================================
# INCLUDE P IN CONNECTOR NAMES? (MERGED AB3:AC3)
#
# AB3 = NO (case-insensitive):  %.2, %.9B
# AB3 = YES, blank, or anything else:  %.P2, %.P9B
# Only an explicit NO disables the P prefix.
# ======================================================
INCLUDE_P_PREFIX = str(ws["AB3"].value or "").strip().upper() != "NO"
print(
    "CONNECTOR P PREFIX:",
    "ENABLED (%.P2)" if INCLUDE_P_PREFIX else "DISABLED (%.2)"
)

# Connector PART NUMBERS: up to 12 in B3:B14.
CONNECTOR_PARTS = [ws[f"B{r}"].value for r in range(3, 15)]
CONNECTOR_PARTS = [part for part in CONNECTOR_PARTS if part is not None and str(part).strip() != ""]


def clean_connector_designation(value):
    if value is None:
        return None

    # Keep Excel numbers clean: 9.0 -> "9"
    if isinstance(value, float) and value.is_integer():
        value = int(value)

    text = str(value).strip().upper().replace(" ", "")

    if text == "":
        return None

    # Accept values such as 9, 9B, P9, P9B, W1.P9B, P9B#...
    if "." in text:
        text = text.split(".")[-1]

    if "#" in text:
        text = text.split("#", 1)[0]

    if text.startswith("P"):
        text = text[1:]

    return text


# Connector DESIGNATIONS come from the connector layout window.
# Up to 8 layout positions may be on either side (12 connectors total). Blank layout cells are ignored.
LEFT_CONNECTOR_LAYOUT = [clean_connector_designation(ws[f"W{r}"].value) for r in range(4, 12)]
RIGHT_CONNECTOR_LAYOUT = [clean_connector_designation(ws[f"Z{r}"].value) for r in range(4, 12)]

LEFT_CONNECTOR_LAYOUT = [x for x in LEFT_CONNECTOR_LAYOUT if x is not None]
RIGHT_CONNECTOR_LAYOUT = [x for x in RIGHT_CONNECTOR_LAYOUT if x is not None]
CONNECTOR_LAYOUT = LEFT_CONNECTOR_LAYOUT + RIGHT_CONNECTOR_LAYOUT
print(CONNECTOR_LAYOUT)
if len(CONNECTOR_LAYOUT) != len(CONNECTOR_PARTS):
    raise Exception(
        f"Connector layout count ({len(CONNECTOR_LAYOUT)}) does not match connector part count ({len(CONNECTOR_PARTS)}). "
        f"Check B3:B14 and W4:W11 / Z4:Z11."
    )

if len(set(CONNECTOR_LAYOUT)) != len(CONNECTOR_LAYOUT):
    raise Exception(f"Duplicate connector designation found in layout: {CONNECTOR_LAYOUT}")

# Key everything by the actual connector designation from the layout.
# COPY remains numeric because E3's assembly connection tables use numeric COPY ids.
CONNECTORS = {}
CONNECTOR_COPIES = {}
for copy_no, (designation, part) in enumerate(zip(CONNECTOR_LAYOUT, CONNECTOR_PARTS), start=1):
    CONNECTORS[designation] = part
    CONNECTOR_COPIES[designation] = copy_no

print("LEFT CONNECTOR LAYOUT:", LEFT_CONNECTOR_LAYOUT)
print("RIGHT CONNECTOR LAYOUT:", RIGHT_CONNECTOR_LAYOUT)
print("CONNECTOR PART MAP:", CONNECTORS)
print("CONNECTOR COPY MAP:", CONNECTOR_COPIES)
print("CONNECTOR ASSIGNMENT ORDER (B3:B14 -> W4:W11 then Z4:Z11):")
for designation in CONNECTOR_LAYOUT:
    print(f"  {designation} -> {CONNECTORS[designation]} (COPY {CONNECTOR_COPIES[designation]})")


NO_WIRES = int(ws["B15"].value)
NO_SECTIONS = int(ws["B16"].value)

# Model check moved down with the additional connector rows.
MODEL_CHECK = ws["F20"].value
print(MODEL_CHECK)

if MODEL_CHECK != "Pass":
    raise Exception("Model check is failing, fix it")
print(output_path)


wire_definitions = []  
for r in range(18, 18 + NO_SECTIONS):
    wire_type = ws[r][1].value
    wire_no = ws[r][3].value
    wires = ws[r][2].value

    entry = {
        "type": str(wire_type),
        "value": int(wire_no),
        "wires": int(wires),
    }
    if entry["type"] == "spare":
        continue
    wire_definitions.append(entry)


# New sheet pinout mapping:
#   LEFT connector  = column L
#   LEFT pin        = column N
#   RIGHT pin       = column O
#   RIGHT connector = column Q
#
# Connector cells may only be populated at the beginning of a connector
# block, so forward-fill the most recent connector exactly as the new sheet
# layout requires.

def connector_number(value):
    # Keep the old function name so nothing else has to change, but return
    # the actual connector designation as a STRING instead of forcing int().
    # Examples: 9 -> "9", 9B -> "9B", P9B -> "9B", W1.P9B -> "9B".
    return clean_connector_designation(value)


pinouts = []
current_connector1 = None
current_connector2 = None

for r in range(5, 5 + NO_WIRES):
    connector1_value = ws[f"L{r}"].value
    pin1 = ws[f"N{r}"].value
    pin2 = ws[f"O{r}"].value
    connector2_value = ws[f"Q{r}"].value

    if connector1_value is not None:
        current_connector1 = connector_number(connector1_value)

    if connector2_value is not None:
        current_connector2 = connector_number(connector2_value)

    if current_connector1 is None or current_connector2 is None:
        continue

    if pin1 is None or pin2 is None:
        continue

    pinouts.append([current_connector1, pin1, current_connector2, pin2])

print(pinouts)
wb.close()

# ================================================
# PARSE DATA
# ================================================
WIRES = []
idx = 0
# Connector COPY ids are 1..N, so wires begin immediately after them.
assy_no = max(CONNECTOR_COPIES.values()) + 1
for entry in wire_definitions:
    wire_type = entry["type"]
    count = entry["value"]
    pins_per_wire = entry["wires"]

    for i in range(count):
        for j in range(pins_per_wire):
            connector1, pin, connector2, mating_pin = pinouts[idx]
            WIRES.append((wire_type, "1", connector1,  pin, connector2, mating_pin, assy_no, pins_per_wire, j + 1))
            idx += 1

        assy_no += 1

print(wire_definitions, '\n')
print(WIRES, '\n')

print(CONNECTORS)
# ================================================
# CREATE PART IN DBE EDITOR
# ================================================
CONNECTION_STRING = (
    "DRIVER={SQL Server};"
    "SERVER=sql-zuken-prd;"
    "DATABASE=Zuken_E3Library;"
    "Trusted_Connection=yes;"
)

app = win32.gencache.EnsureDispatch("CT.DBEApplication")

app.PutMessage("Creating through DBE APPLICATION...")
result = app.NewComponent(ASSY_NAME, VERSION, "TEMPLATE_CABLE_ASSY", "1", 0)
app.PutMessage("app.NewComponent():", result)
if result != 0:
    app.PutMessage("\nCOMPONENT NEEDS A DIFFERENT NAME, THIS ONE ALREADY USED IN DATABASE SOMEHWERE")
    raise Exception("\nCOMPONENT NEEDS A DIFFERENT NAME, THIS ONE ALREADY USED IN DATABASE SOMEHWERE")

time.sleep(1)


# ================================================
# GET LPNTR
# ================================================
cid = app.GetActiveComponentId()
print("Active ID:", cid)

if cid > 0:
    comp = app.CreateDbeComponentObject()
    comp.SetId(cid)

    print("Name:", comp.GetName())
    print("Version:", comp.GetVersion())
    print("Type:", comp.GetComponentType())
    print("Save:", comp.Save())

time.sleep(2)

conn = pyodbc.connect(CONNECTION_STRING, autocommit=False)
cur = conn.cursor()

CURRENT_NAME = comp.GetName()
CURRENT_VERSION = comp.GetVersion()

cur.execute("""
SELECT ID, ENTRY, VERSION, LPNTR
FROM dbo.ComponentData
WHERE ENTRY = ?
AND VERSION = ?
""", CURRENT_NAME, CURRENT_VERSION)

row = cur.fetchone()

print("\nDATABASE ROW:")
print(tuple(row) if row else None)
print('LPNTR: ', row[-1])
print('DB_ID: ', row[0])
DB_ID = row[0]
LPNTR = row[-1]


# ============================================================
# HELPERS
# ============================================================
def add_member(copy_no, part, version, member_name="%.#"):
    cur.execute("""
    INSERT INTO dbo.Assembly
    (LPNTR, COPY, FLAGS, NAME, SUBENTRY, SUBVERSION)
    VALUES (?, ?, 2, ?, ?, ?)
    """, LPNTR, copy_no, member_name, part, version)

    cur.execute("""
    INSERT INTO dbo.GatePinRelation
    (ENTRY, GateNumber, PinNumber, Representation, NodeNumber)
    VALUES (?, ?, 0, 0, 0)
    """, LPNTR, copy_no)


def connect_wire(wire_copy, end1_copy, pin1, end2_copy, pin2):
    print(wire_copy, end1_copy, pin1, end2_copy, pin2)
    cur.execute("""
    INSERT INTO dbo.WIRTYP_CONNECT_INFOS
    (LPNTR, FLAGS, COPY, NODENO, COPYEND1, PINCOPYEND1, COPYEND2, PINCOPYEND2)
    VALUES (?, 0, ?, 4, ?, ?, ?, ?)
    """, LPNTR, wire_copy, end1_copy, pin1, end2_copy, pin2)

def connect_wire(wire_copy, node_no, end1_copy, pin1, end2_copy, pin2):
    print("Wire:", wire_copy, "Node:", node_no, "End1:", end1_copy,  "Pin1:", pin1,   "End2:", end2_copy, "Pin2:", pin2)

    cur.execute("""
    INSERT INTO dbo.WIRTYP_CONNECT_INFOS
    (LPNTR, FLAGS, COPY, NODENO, COPYEND1, PINCOPYEND1, COPYEND2, PINCOPYEND2)
    VALUES (?, 0, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
    """,
    LPNTR,
    wire_copy,  node_no, end1_copy, pin1, end2_copy, pin2)

def mark_component_updated():
    cur.execute("""
    UPDATE dbo.ComponentData
    SET LASTUPDT = GETDATE()
    WHERE ID = ?
    """, DB_ID)

def remove_template_member(part_name):
    cur.execute("""
    SELECT COPY
    FROM dbo.Assembly
    WHERE LPNTR = ?
    AND SUBENTRY = ?
    """, LPNTR, part_name)

    rows = cur.fetchall()

    if not rows:
        print(f"Template member not found: {part_name}")
        return

    for row in rows:
        copy_no = row[0]

        print(f"Removing template member COPY {copy_no}: {part_name}")

        # Remove any wire connections involving this member
        cur.execute("""
        DELETE FROM dbo.WIRTYP_CONNECT_INFOS
        WHERE LPNTR = ?
        AND (COPY = ? OR COPYEND1 = ? OR COPYEND2 = ?)
        """, LPNTR, copy_no, copy_no, copy_no)

        # Remove corresponding gate
        cur.execute("""
        DELETE FROM dbo.GatePinRelation
        WHERE ENTRY = ?
        AND GateNumber = ?
        """, LPNTR, copy_no)

        # Remove member from assembly
        cur.execute("""
        DELETE FROM dbo.Assembly
        WHERE LPNTR = ?
        AND COPY = ?
        """, LPNTR, copy_no)

    app.PutMessage("Template member removed.")

def get_pin_copy(connector_name, connector_version, physical_pin):
    cur.execute("""
    SELECT LPNTR
    FROM dbo.ComponentData
    WHERE ENTRY = ?
    AND VERSION = CONVERT(NVARCHAR(50), ?)
    """, connector_name, connector_version)

    row = cur.fetchone()

    if not row:
        raise RuntimeError(f"Connector not found: {connector_name}")

    connector_lpntr = row[0]

    cur.execute("""
    SELECT COPY
    FROM dbo.Pin
    WHERE ENTRY = ?
    AND PHYSPIN = ?
    """, connector_lpntr, str(physical_pin))

    row = cur.fetchone()

    if not row:
        raise RuntimeError(f"Pin {physical_pin} not found on {connector_name}")

    return row[0]

# Resolve connector versions from the E3 library instead of assuming "1".
# Explicitly select A for the connector whose revision is known.
CONNECTOR_VERSION_OVERRIDES = {
    "Molex_Recept_42_2x21": "A",
}


def get_connector_version(connector_name):
    cur.execute("""
        SELECT DISTINCT VERSION
        FROM dbo.ComponentData
        WHERE ENTRY = ?
    """, connector_name)
    versions = [str(row[0]).strip() for row in cur.fetchall() if row[0] is not None]

    if not versions:
        raise RuntimeError(f"Connector not found: {connector_name}")

    preferred = CONNECTOR_VERSION_OVERRIDES.get(str(connector_name))
    if preferred is not None:
        for version in versions:
            if version.upper() == str(preferred).upper():
                return version
        raise RuntimeError(
            f"Version {preferred} not found for {connector_name}. "
            f"Available versions: {', '.join(versions)}"
        )

    if len(versions) == 1:
        return versions[0]

    # Preserve the original script's version-1 default if several exist.
    if "1" in versions:
        return "1"

    raise RuntimeError(
        f"Multiple versions exist for connector {connector_name}: "
        f"{', '.join(versions)}. Add the desired version to "
        "CONNECTOR_VERSION_OVERRIDES."
    )


# Resolve once so assembly membership and pin lookups use the SAME version.
CONNECTOR_VERSIONS = {
    designation: get_connector_version(CONNECTORS[designation])
    for designation in CONNECTOR_LAYOUT
}
print("CONNECTOR VERSION MAP:", CONNECTOR_VERSIONS)

# ============================================================
# CONNECTORS
# ============================================================
remove_template_member("TEMPLATE_COMPONENT_DONT_DELETE")

for connector_designation in CONNECTOR_LAYOUT:
    connector_part = CONNECTORS[connector_designation]
    connector_copy = CONNECTOR_COPIES[connector_designation]

    app.PutMessage(
        f"\nAdding Connector {connector_designation} "
        f"(COPY {connector_copy}): {connector_part}"
    )

    # COPY remains numeric, preserving the original pin/wire mapping.
    # Only an explicit "NO" in AB3 removes the P prefix.
    if INCLUDE_P_PREFIX:
        member_name = f"%.P{connector_designation}"
    else:
        member_name = f"%.{connector_designation}"

    print("CONNECTOR MEMBER NAME:", member_name)

    add_member(
        connector_copy,
        connector_part,
        CONNECTOR_VERSIONS[connector_designation],
        member_name
    )


# ============================================================
# WIRES + PIN ASSIGNMENTS
# ============================================================
added_wire_copies = set()

for wire_data in WIRES:
    (wire_part, wire_version, connector1, pin1, connector2, pin2, wire_copy, no_wires, conductor_no) = wire_data

    # Add physical wire/cable only once
    if wire_copy not in added_wire_copies:

        app.PutMessage(f"Adding wire COPY {wire_copy}: {wire_part} ({no_wires} conductor(s))")
        add_member(wire_copy, wire_part, wire_version)
        added_wire_copies.add(wire_copy)

    # Get connector part numbers and E3 numeric COPY ids from the actual
    # connector designations read from the cable table/layout.
    connector1_part = CONNECTORS[connector1]
    connector2_part = CONNECTORS[connector2]
    connector1_copy = CONNECTOR_COPIES[connector1]
    connector2_copy = CONNECTOR_COPIES[connector2]

    # Convert physical pins to Zuken pin COPY numbers
    pin1_copy = get_pin_copy(connector1_part, CONNECTOR_VERSIONS[connector1], pin1)
    pin2_copy = get_pin_copy(connector2_part, CONNECTOR_VERSIONS[connector2], pin2)

    # Determine internal conductor node
    if no_wires == 1:
        node_no = 4
    else:
        node_no = 4 + (conductor_no - 1)

    app.PutMessage(
        f"Wire COPY {wire_copy}, Conductor {conductor_no}: {wire_part} | "
        f"Connector {connector1} (COPY {connector1_copy}) Pin {pin1} -> "
        f"Connector {connector2} (COPY {connector2_copy}) Pin {pin2} | Node {node_no}"
    )
    connect_wire(
        wire_copy,
        node_no,
        connector1_copy,
        pin1_copy,
        connector2_copy,
        pin2_copy,
    )

mark_component_updated()


# ============================================================
# ASK USER TO CONFIRM
# ============================================================
app.PutMessage("\nASSEMBLY:")

cur.execute("""
SELECT COPY, SUBENTRY, SUBVERSION
FROM dbo.Assembly
WHERE LPNTR = ?
ORDER BY COPY
""", LPNTR)

for row in cur.fetchall():
    app.PutMessage(str(tuple(row)))


app.PutMessage("\nSee script prompt to committ changes to database.")

cur.execute("""
SELECT COPY, COPYEND1, PINCOPYEND1, COPYEND2, PINCOPYEND2
FROM dbo.WIRTYP_CONNECT_INFOS
WHERE LPNTR = ?
ORDER BY COPY
""", LPNTR)


# ============================================================
# SIMPLE GUI
# ============================================================
rows = cur.fetchall()
to_commit = "Do you want to add these connections to database?\n"
to_commit += "CONNECTIONS:\n"

to_commit += "\n".join(
    f"Assembly number: {row[0]}, End 1 number: {row[1]}, End 1 pin: {row[2]}, End 2 number: {row[3]}, End 2 pin: {row[4]}"
    for row in rows
)

answer = make_gui(to_commit)

if answer == "YES":
    conn.commit()
    app.PutMessage("Committed.")
else:
    conn.rollback()
    app.PutMessage("Rolled back.")

# ============================================================
# CLEANUP
# ============================================================
if answer == "YES":
    app.PutMessage("From the component viewer -> right click -> delete -> reopen / edit new assembly to see changes.")

cur.close()
conn.close()

del comp
del app

# =======================================================
# USER-FRIENDLY ERROR HANDLING
# =======================================================
import traceback

def friendly_error_message(error):
    msg = str(error)
    lower = msg.lower()

    if "no pins found" in lower:
        return (
            "Connector Pins Not Found",
            "The selected connector's pins could not be found in E3.",
            "Verify that the connector exists in the E3 library "
            "and matches the Excel cable layout."
        )

    if "could not find e3 connector" in lower or "could not locate the split schematic view" in lower:
        return (
            "Connector View Not Found",
            "A connector or its schematic view could not be located.",
            "Check that the correct cable is open in E3 and "
            "that its connector views have been placed."
        )

    if "cable table references connector" in lower:
        return (
            "Connector not defined correctly",
            "There is an error with something in the spreadsheet",
            "Make sure that the connectors defined in W3-Z11 "
            "actually appear somewhere in columns L/Q "
            " - Maybe the wrong number was used?"
        )


    if "could not resize" in lower or "final length changed" in lower:
        return (
            "Shield Resizing Failed",
            "E3 could not resize a shield to the required length.",
            "Check the available space on the schematic and "
            "verify that Shield_Demo is available."
        )

    if "could not load library" in lower:
        return (
            "Symbol Not Found",
            "A required library symbol could not be loaded.",
            "Verify that the shield or twist symbol exists "
            "in the E3 library."
        )

    if "d15" in lower or "must contain yes or no" in lower or "connector layout count" in lower:
        return (
            "Excel Configuration Error",
            msg,
            "Review the indicated Excel cell or layout setting "
            "and correct its value."
        )

    if isinstance(error, FileNotFoundError):
        return (
            "File Not Found",
            "The selected file could not be located.",
            "Make sure the Excel workbook has not been moved, "
            "renamed, or deleted."
        )

    if isinstance(error, PermissionError):
        return (
            "File Access Error",
            "The script does not have permission to access a file.",
            "Check that the file is accessible and that the file "
            "is CLOSED. - Files on network drive or on Onedrive"
            " need to be closed or permission will be denied."
        )

    if isinstance(error, KeyError):
        return (
            "Missing Data",
            f"Required information could not be found: {msg}",
            "Check the Excel pin assignments and connector "
            "definitions for missing or incorrect values."
        )

    if "com_error" in type(error).__name__.lower():
        return (
            "Zuken E3 Communication Error",
            "The script encountered an error while communicating with E3.",
            "Make sure E3 is running with a blank sheet open. "
            " Could also be a wifi thing. "
            "Check Technical Details for the specific E3 error."
        )

    if "pyodbc" in type(error).__module__.lower():
        return (
            "Database Error",
            "The cable library database operation failed.",
            "Check the database connection and availability."
            " Also make sure to check your wifi connection"
            " try expanding a folder for something in zuken"
            " like cable_cnp and see if that throws error"
            " if so - its a wifi thing."
        )

    return (
        "Cable Placer Error",
        "The operation could not be completed.",
        "Review the technical details below to identify "
        "the specific problem."
    )


def show_error_dialog(error, parent=None, details=None):
    title, description, suggestion = friendly_error_message(error)

    if details is None:
        details = "".join(
            traceback.format_exception(
                type(error),
                error,
                error.__traceback__
            )
        )

    owns_root = parent is None

    if owns_root:
        window = tk.Tk()
    else:
        window = tk.Toplevel(parent)

    window.title("Cable Placer - Error")
    window.geometry("520x340")
    window.resizable(False, False)

    try:
        window.attributes("-topmost", True)
    except Exception:
        pass

    frame = ttk.Frame(window, padding=22)
    frame.pack(fill="both", expand=True)

    ttk.Label(
        frame,
        text=title,
        font=("Segoe UI", 16, "bold"),
        foreground="#C0392B"
    ).pack(anchor="w", pady=(0, 15))

    ttk.Label(
        frame,
        text="What happened:",
        font=("Segoe UI", 10, "bold")
    ).pack(anchor="w")

    ttk.Label(
        frame,
        text=description,
        wraplength=465,
        justify="left"
    ).pack(anchor="w", pady=(4, 15))

    ttk.Label(
        frame,
        text="Suggested fix:",
        font=("Segoe UI", 10, "bold")
    ).pack(anchor="w")

    ttk.Label(
        frame,
        text=suggestion,
        wraplength=465,
        justify="left"
    ).pack(anchor="w", pady=(4, 15))

    detail_frame = ttk.Frame(frame)

    detail_text = tk.Text(
        detail_frame,
        height=7,
        wrap="word",
        font=("Consolas", 9)
    )
    detail_text.pack(fill="both", expand=True)
    detail_text.insert("1.0", details)
    detail_text.config(state="disabled")

    showing_details = False

    def toggle_details():
        nonlocal showing_details

        showing_details = not showing_details

        if showing_details:
            window.geometry("520x550")
            detail_frame.pack(fill="both", expand=True, pady=(5, 10))
            details_button.config(text="Hide Technical Details")
        else:
            detail_frame.pack_forget()
            window.geometry("520x340")
            details_button.config(text="Show Technical Details")

    def copy_details():
        window.clipboard_clear()
        window.clipboard_append(details)
        window.update()

    def close_window():
        window.destroy()

    button_frame = ttk.Frame(frame)
    button_frame.pack(side="bottom", fill="x", pady=(10, 0))

    details_button = ttk.Button(
        button_frame,
        text="Show Technical Details",
        command=toggle_details
    )
    details_button.pack(side="left")

    ttk.Button(
        button_frame,
        text="Copy Error",
        command=copy_details
    ).pack(side="left", padx=8)

    ttk.Button(
        button_frame,
        text="OK",
        command=close_window
    ).pack(side="right")

    window.protocol("WM_DELETE_WINDOW", close_window)

    if owns_root:
        window.mainloop()
    else:
        window.transient(parent)
        window.grab_set()
        parent.wait_window(window)


# =======================================================
# HANDLE UNCAUGHT SCRIPT ERRORS
# =======================================================
def cable_exception_handler(exc_type, exc_value, exc_tb):
    if issubclass(exc_type, (KeyboardInterrupt, SystemExit)):
        sys.__excepthook__(exc_type, exc_value, exc_tb)
        return

    details = "".join(
        traceback.format_exception(
            exc_type, exc_value, exc_tb
        )
    )

    print(details, file=sys.stderr)

    try:
        show_error_dialog(exc_value, details=details)
    except Exception:
        sys.__excepthook__(exc_type, exc_value, exc_tb)


sys.excepthook = cable_exception_handler

# -------------------------------------------------------
# STARTUP MODE
# -------------------------------------------------------
def choose_start_mode():
    """
    Choose whether this run should:
      1. Build/place the cable and then optionally place symbols,
      2. Add V9 shields/twists to the cable already open in E3, or
      3. Run only the existing formatter.

    Symbols-only mode does NOT create/load/place the cable component.
    Formatter-only mode does not require an Excel workbook.
    """
    root = tk.Tk()
    root.title("Cable Placer")
    root.geometry("600x540")
    root.resizable(False, False)

    style = ttk.Style()
    try:
        style.theme_use("vista")
    except:
        pass

    style.configure("ModeTitle.TLabel", font=("Segoe UI", 18, "bold"))
    style.configure("ModeText.TLabel", font=("Segoe UI", 10))
    style.configure("ModeButton.TButton", font=("Segoe UI", 11, "bold"), padding=(18, 11))

    result = {"mode": None}

    def select_mode(mode):
        result["mode"] = mode
        root.destroy()

    frame = ttk.Frame(root, padding=25)
    frame.pack(fill="both", expand=True)

    ttk.Label(
        frame,
        text="Cable Placer",
        style="ModeTitle.TLabel"
    ).pack(anchor="w")

    ttk.Label(
        frame,
        text="Choose what you want this run to do.",
        style="ModeText.TLabel"
    ).pack(anchor="w", pady=(4, 22))

    ttk.Button(
        frame,
        text="Build / Place Cable",
        style="ModeButton.TButton",
        command=lambda: select_mode("FULL")
    ).pack(fill="x", pady=(0, 12))

    ttk.Label(
        frame,
        text=(
            "Creates/loads and places the cable, formats the connector views, "
            "then pauses for manual connection lines before placing shields/twists "
            "when symbols are defined."
        ),
        justify="left",
        wraplength=535,
        style="ModeText.TLabel"
    ).pack(anchor="w", pady=(0, 24))

    ttk.Button(
        frame,
        text="Add Symbols to Open Cable",
        style="ModeButton.TButton",
        command=lambda: select_mode("SYMBOLS_ONLY")
    ).pack(fill="x", pady=(0, 12))

    ttk.Label(
        frame,
        text=(
            "Uses the cable already open in E3 and places"
            "shield/twist. It does not load or place the cable."
        ),
        justify="left",
        wraplength=535,
        style="ModeText.TLabel"
    ).pack(anchor="w")

    ttk.Button(
        frame,
        text="Simple Formatter Run",
        style="ModeButton.TButton",
        command=lambda: select_mode("FORMATTER_ONLY")
    ).pack(fill="x", pady=(24, 12))

    ttk.Label(
        frame,
        text=(
            "Runs only the existing Zuken Symbol Formatter on the cable already "
            "open in E3. No Excel file is requested and no cable or symbols "
            "are placed."
        ),
        justify="left",
        wraplength=535,
        style="ModeText.TLabel"
    ).pack(anchor="w")

    ttk.Button(
        frame,
        text="Cancel",
        command=lambda: select_mode(None)
    ).pack(pady=(22, 0))

    root.protocol("WM_DELETE_WINDOW", lambda: select_mode(None))
    root.mainloop()

    return result["mode"]


# -------------------------------------------------------
# GUI SETUP
# -------------------------------------------------------
def create_gui():
    from tkinter import ttk
    import threading

    root = tk.Tk()
    root.title("Zuken Symbol Formatter")
    root.geometry("560x500")
    root.resizable(False, False)

    style = ttk.Style()
    try:
        style.theme_use("vista")
    except:
        pass

    style.configure("Title.TLabel", font=("Segoe UI", 18, "bold"))
    style.configure("Subtitle.TLabel", font=("Segoe UI", 10))
    style.configure("Step.TLabel", font=("Segoe UI", 10))
    style.configure("Status.TLabel", font=("Segoe UI", 10, "bold"))
    style.configure("Run.TButton", font=("Segoe UI", 11, "bold"), padding=(20, 10))
    style.configure("Secondary.TButton", font=("Segoe UI", 10), padding=(15, 8))

    main_frame = ttk.Frame(root, padding=25)
    main_frame.pack(fill="both", expand=True)

    ttk.Label(
        main_frame,
        text="Zuken Symbol Formatter",
        style="Title.TLabel"
    ).pack(anchor="w")

    ttk.Label(
        main_frame,
        text="Configure and format generated connector symbols.",
        style="Subtitle.TLabel"
    ).pack(anchor="w", pady=(2, 20))

    instruction_frame = ttk.LabelFrame(
        main_frame,
        text="Before Running",
        padding=15
    )
    instruction_frame.pack(fill="x", pady=(0, 20))

    instructions = (
        "1. Open Device Properties in Zuken.\n\n"
        "2. Enable:\n"
        "   Assembly  →  Additional Part ID\n\n"
        "3. Return here and click Run Formatter."
    )

    ttk.Label(
        instruction_frame,
        text=instructions,
        justify="left",
        style="Step.TLabel"
    ).pack(anchor="w")

    status_frame = ttk.LabelFrame(
        main_frame,
        text="Status",
        padding=12
    )
    status_frame.pack(fill="x", pady=(0, 20))

    status_label = ttk.Label(
        status_frame,
        text="Ready",
        style="Status.TLabel"
    )
    status_label.pack(anchor="w")

    # -------------------------------------------------------
    # FORMATTER FINISHED SUCCESSFULLY
    # -------------------------------------------------------
    def finished():
        root.config(cursor="")

        # Hide the formatter window immediately.
        root.withdraw()

        # Show completion popup.
        try:
            messagebox.showinfo(
                "Complete",
                "Formatting complete.",
                parent=root
            )
        finally:
            # Fully close formatter GUI after OK.
            root.destroy()

    # -------------------------------------------------------
    # FORMATTER ERROR
    # -------------------------------------------------------
    def failed(error, details):
        status_label.config(text="Formatting failed.")
        run_button.config(state="normal")
        run_again_button.config(state="normal")
        root.config(cursor="")

        show_error_dialog(
            error,
            parent=root,
            details=details
        )

    # -------------------------------------------------------
    # BACKGROUND FORMATTER WORKER
    # -------------------------------------------------------

    def worker():
        error = None
        details = None
        com_initialized = False

        try:
            pythoncom.CoInitialize()
            com_initialized = True

            run_ct_code()

        except Exception as e:
            error = e
            details = traceback.format_exc()
            print(details)

        finally:
            if com_initialized:
                pythoncom.CoUninitialize()

        if error is None:
            root.after(0, finished)
        else:
            root.after(
                0,
                lambda err=error, trace=details: failed(err, trace)
            )
    # -------------------------------------------------------
    # START FORMATTER
    # -------------------------------------------------------
    def run_formatter():
        status_label.config(text="Running...")
        run_button.config(state="disabled")
        run_again_button.config(state="disabled")
        root.config(cursor="watch")

        threading.Thread(
            target=worker,
            daemon=True
        ).start()

    # -------------------------------------------------------
    # CLOSE
    # -------------------------------------------------------
    def close_app():
        root.destroy()

    # -------------------------------------------------------
    # BUTTONS
    # -------------------------------------------------------
    button_frame = ttk.Frame(main_frame)
    button_frame.pack(fill="x")

    run_button = ttk.Button(
        button_frame,
        text="Run Formatter",
        style="Run.TButton",
        command=run_formatter
    )
    run_button.pack(fill="x")

    secondary_frame = ttk.Frame(main_frame)
    secondary_frame.pack(pady=(12, 0))

    run_again_button = ttk.Button(
        secondary_frame,
        text="Run Again",
        style="Secondary.TButton",
        command=run_formatter
    )
    run_again_button.pack(side="left", padx=(0, 8))

    ttk.Button(
        secondary_frame,
        text="Close",
        style="Secondary.TButton",
        command=close_app
    ).pack(side="left")

    ttk.Label(
        main_frame,
        text="E3 Symbol Automation",
        font=("Segoe UI", 8)
    ).pack(side="bottom", anchor="e", pady=(15, 0))

    root.protocol("WM_DELETE_WINDOW", close_app)

    root.mainloop()

# -------------------------------------------------------
# CONNECTION-LINE CHECKPOINT
#
# This window is only shown when the selected workbook contains
# at least one shield or twist definition.
# -------------------------------------------------------
def wait_for_connection_lines():
    root = tk.Tk()
    root.title("Connect Cable Lines")
    root.geometry("560x360")
    root.resizable(False, False)

    style = ttk.Style()
    try:
        style.theme_use("vista")
    except:
        pass

    style.configure("ConnectTitle.TLabel", font=("Segoe UI", 18, "bold"))
    style.configure("ConnectStep.TLabel", font=("Segoe UI", 10))
    style.configure(
        "ConnectButton.TButton",
        font=("Segoe UI", 11, "bold"),
        padding=(20, 10)
    )

    result = {"continue": False}

    def continue_to_symbols():
        result["continue"] = True
        root.destroy()

    def cancel():
        result["continue"] = False
        root.destroy()

    frame = ttk.Frame(root, padding=25)
    frame.pack(fill="both", expand=True)

    ttk.Label(
        frame,
        text="Place Symbols",
        style="ConnectTitle.TLabel"
    ).pack(anchor="w")

    ttk.Label(
        frame,
        text="CONNECT ALL PINS AND CONNECTORS BEFORE PLACING SYMBOLS!",
        style="ConnectStep.TLabel"
    ).pack(anchor="w", pady=(4, 18))

    instructions = (
        "Click to Place Shields / Twists."
    )

    ttk.Label(
        frame,
        text=instructions,
        justify="left",
        style="ConnectStep.TLabel"
    ).pack(anchor="w", fill="x")

    button_frame = ttk.Frame(frame)
    button_frame.pack(fill="x", pady=(25, 0))

    ttk.Button(
        button_frame,
        text="Place Shields / Twists",
        style="ConnectButton.TButton",
        command=continue_to_symbols
    ).pack(fill="x")

    ttk.Button(
        button_frame,
        text="Cancel",
        command=cancel
    ).pack(pady=(10, 0))

    root.protocol("WM_DELETE_WINDOW", cancel)
    root.mainloop()

    return result["continue"]


def run_ct_code():
    try:
        app = win32.gencache.EnsureDispatch('CT.Application')
        prj = app.CreateJobObject()
        text = prj.CreateTextObject()
        dev = prj.CreateDeviceObject()
        sym = prj.CreateSymbolObject()

        # =====================================================
        # SCHEMATIC-VIEW ADDITIONAL PART ID
        #
        # Keep exactly ONE Additional Part ID across ALL split
        # schematic pin views.
        #
        # The text is attached to the overall top-left main pin
        # symbol, but moved to the horizontal midpoint between the
        # LEFT and RIGHT main pin columns and slightly above the
        # highest main pin.
        #
        # This prevents one Additional Part ID from appearing on
        # both the left and right sides.
        # =====================================================
        all_schematic_symbol_locations = {}

        for scan_dev_id in new_dev_ids:
            dev.SetId(scan_dev_id)

            scan_symbol_info = dev.GetSymbolIds(None)
            scan_symbol_ids = [
                x for x in scan_symbol_info[1]
                if x is not None
            ]

            for scan_symbol_id in scan_symbol_ids:
                sym.SetId(scan_symbol_id)

                xloc = [0.0]
                yloc = [0.0]
                grid = [""]
                column = [""]
                row = [""]

                try:
                    location = sym.GetSchemaLocation(
                        xloc,
                        yloc,
                        grid,
                        column,
                        row
                    )

                    if isinstance(location, tuple) and len(location) >= 3:
                        all_schematic_symbol_locations[scan_symbol_id] = (
                            float(location[1]),
                            float(location[2])
                        )
                except Exception:
                    pass

        schematic_additional_part_symbol_id = None
        schematic_additional_part_x = None
        schematic_additional_part_y = None

        # PINN_LOCS contains the actual main pin-column X locations
        # generated by the cable placer.  Spare pins use SPARE_X and
        # therefore are not included here.
        main_pin_x_values = sorted(set(
            round(float(location[1]), 6)
            for location in PINN_LOCS
        ))

        if all_schematic_symbol_locations and main_pin_x_values:
            left_main_x = min(main_pin_x_values)
            right_main_x = max(main_pin_x_values)

            # Center the single label between the two cable sides.
            schematic_additional_part_x = (
                left_main_x + right_main_x
            ) / 2.0

            main_symbol_ids = [
                symbol_id
                for symbol_id, location
                in all_schematic_symbol_locations.items()
                if (
                    abs(location[0] - left_main_x) <= 0.001
                    or abs(location[0] - right_main_x) <= 0.001
                )
            ]

            if main_symbol_ids:
                top_main_y = max(
                    all_schematic_symbol_locations[symbol_id][1]
                    for symbol_id in main_symbol_ids
                )

                # Keep the text just above the top pin.
                schematic_additional_part_y = top_main_y +0.15

                # Use the top-most symbol on the LEFT side as the single
                # owner of the type-1000 text object.
                left_symbol_ids = [
                    symbol_id
                    for symbol_id in main_symbol_ids
                    if abs(
                        all_schematic_symbol_locations[symbol_id][0]
                        - left_main_x
                    ) <= 0.001
                ]

                if left_symbol_ids:
                    schematic_additional_part_symbol_id = max(
                        left_symbol_ids,
                        key=lambda symbol_id:
                            all_schematic_symbol_locations[symbol_id][1]
                    )
                else:
                    schematic_additional_part_symbol_id = max(
                        main_symbol_ids,
                        key=lambda symbol_id:
                            all_schematic_symbol_locations[symbol_id][1]
                    )

                print(
                    "SINGLE SCHEMATIC ADDITIONAL PART ID OWNER:",
                    schematic_additional_part_symbol_id
                )
                print(
                    "SCHEMATIC ADDITIONAL PART ID CENTER:",
                    schematic_additional_part_x,
                    schematic_additional_part_y
                )

        # =====================================================
        # LOOP THROUGH EACH NEW DEVICE VIEW
        # =====================================================

        for new_dev_id in new_dev_ids:
            connector = NEW_DEV_CONNECTORS.get(new_dev_id)

            print("\n======================================")
            print("NEW VIEW ID:", new_dev_id)
            print("CONNECTOR:", connector)
            print("======================================")

            if connector is None:
                print("WARNING: no connector mapping for view", new_dev_id)
                continue

            dev.SetId(new_dev_id)
            symbol_info = dev.GetSymbolIds(None)
            symbol_ids = [x for x in symbol_info[1] if x is not None]
            print("NEW VIEW SYMBOL IDS:", symbol_ids)

            for symbol_id in symbol_ids:
                sym.SetId(symbol_id)
                print("new view symbol:",  symbol_id, sym.GetName())
                texinfo = sym.GetTextIds(None)
                text_ids = [x for x in texinfo[1] if x is not None]

                for t_id in text_ids:
                    text.SetId(t_id)
                    text_type = text.GetType()

                    print(
                        "PIN SYMBOL:",
                        sym.GetName(),
                        "| text:",
                        text.GetText(),
                        "| type:",
                        text_type
                    )

                    if text_type == 5:
                        text.SetVisibility(0)

                    elif text_type == 1000:
                        if (
                            symbol_id
                            == schematic_additional_part_symbol_id
                            and schematic_additional_part_x is not None
                            and schematic_additional_part_y is not None
                        ):
                            app.PutMessage(
                                "Configuring single centered schematic "
                                "Additional Part ID"
                            )

                            text.SetVisibility(1)
                            text.SetStyle(1)
                            text.SetHeight(0.2)
                            text.SetColour(0)
                            text.SetMode(1)
                            text.SetAlignment(2)
                            text.SetSchemaLocation(
                                schematic_additional_part_x,
                                schematic_additional_part_y
                            )
                        else:
                            # Every other split-view Additional Part ID,
                            # including the previous top-right one, is hidden.
                            text.SetVisibility(0)

                    if text_type == 12:
                        text.SetStyle(1)

            connector_id = CONNECTORS.get(connector)
            print("CONNECTOR:",  connector, "ID:",  connector_id)

            if connector_id is None:
                print("WARNING: connector ID not found for", connector)
                continue

            dev.SetId(connector_id)
            print("Formatting bulk connector:", dev.GetName())


            assydevsymbolinfo = dev.GetSymbolIds(None)
            assydevsymbolids = [x for x in assydevsymbolinfo[1] if x is not None]
            print("BULK SYMBOL IDS:", assydevsymbolids)

            for assydevsymbol_id in assydevsymbolids:
                sym.SetId(assydevsymbol_id)
                print("BULK SYMBOL:", assydevsymbol_id, "| name:", sym.GetName())
                assydevsymboltextinfo = sym.GetTextIds(None)
                assydevsymboltextids = [x for x in assydevsymboltextinfo[1] if x is not None]

                for assydevsymboltext_id in assydevsymboltextids:
                    text.SetId(assydevsymboltext_id)
                    print("name:", sym.GetName(), "| text:", text.GetText(), "| type:", text.GetType())
                    if text.GetType() == 5:
                        text.SetVisibility(0)
                    if text.GetType() == 12:
                        text.SetStyle(1)
                    if (text.GetType() == 1000 and sym.GetName() == "J1"):
                        app.PutMessage("Configuring text")
                        text.SetVisibility(1)
                        text.SetStyle(1)
                        text.SetHeight(0.2)
                        text.SetColour(0)
                        text.SetMode(1)
                        text.SetAlignment(2)
                        # A formatter-only pass must NEVER shift an already
                        # positioned top-level (bulk J1) Additional Part ID.
                        # The previous +1.5 / +0.15 adjustment was relative
                        # to its current location, so each formatter run
                        # moved the label farther away.  Preserve its actual
                        # E3 location in FORMATTER_ONLY; keep the original
                        # first-placement behavior in the full cable workflow.
                        if START_MODE != "FORMATTER_ONLY":
                            xloc = [0.0]
                            yloc = [0.0]
                            grid = [""]
                            column = [""]
                            row = [""]
                            result = text.GetSchemaLocation(xloc, yloc, grid, column, row)
                            new_x = result[1] + 1.5
                            new_y = result[2] + 0.15
                            text.SetSchemaLocation(new_x, new_y)
                        else:
                            print(
                                "FORMATTER ONLY: keeping top-level Additional "
                                "Part ID at its existing schematic location"
                            )

                    elif text.GetType() == 1000:
                        text.SetVisibility(0)

        del text
        del sym
        del dev
        del prj
        del app
        print("E3 formatter completed successfully.")

    except Exception as e:
        print(f"E3 formattor error: {e}")
        raise


def ask_save_path(default_full_path=""):
    import os
    import tkinter as tk
    from tkinter import filedialog

    default_folder = os.path.dirname(default_full_path)

    # Create Tk root
    root = tk.Tk()
    root.withdraw()
    root.attributes("-topmost", True)
    root.update()

    try:
        # Let user PICK an existing Excel file
        selected_path = filedialog.askopenfilename(
            initialdir=default_folder,
            filetypes=[("Excel Files", "*.xlsx"), ("All Files", "*.*")]
        )
    except Exception:
        return None

    # Cancel or X
    if not selected_path:
        root.destroy()
        return None
    root.destroy()
    # Return ONLY the file name (not full path)
    return os.path.abspath(selected_path)

def generate_ordered_pin_ids(dict_to_order, dest):
    return {str(pin): dict_to_order[str(pin)] for pin in dest}


def get_connector_pin_ids(dev, device_id, connector_name="", prefer_all=False):
    """
    Return the actual project pin IDs for a connector/device view.

    Base connector devices are queried with GetPinIds first because those are
    the physical connector pins.  Created schematic views are queried with
    GetAllPinIds first because their view pins are what PlacePins needs.

    The alternate method is used only as a fallback.  This keeps the old
    behavior available while fixing connectors for which GetAllPinIds returns
    an empty list on the base device.
    """
    dev.SetId(device_id)

    if prefer_all:
        method_names = ("GetAllPinIds", "GetPinIds")
    else:
        method_names = ("GetPinIds", "GetAllPinIds")

    for method_name in method_names:
        method = getattr(dev, method_name, None)
        if method is None:
            continue

        # E3 COM methods can differ between generated wrappers, so support
        # both the explicit output-array form and the no-argument form.
        for use_none in (True, False):
            try:
                result = method(None) if use_none else method()
            except Exception as error:
                print(
                    "PIN ENUMERATION FAILED:",
                    connector_name,
                    "DEVICE:", device_id,
                    "METHOD:", method_name,
                    "ERROR:", repr(error)
                )
                continue

            pin_ids = extract_e3_ids(result)

            print(
                "PIN ENUMERATION:",
                connector_name,
                "DEVICE:", device_id,
                "METHOD:", method_name,
                "RAW:", repr(result),
                "PIN IDS:", pin_ids
            )

            if pin_ids:
                return pin_ids

    return []


def connector_designation_from_device_name(value):
    """
    Convert an E3 connector name/reference to the workbook layout designation.

    Current cable-maker output uses W1.<designation>, e.g. W1.9B.  The older
    W1.9B / P9B form is still accepted for numeric designations.
    """
    return normalize_connector_token(value)


def generate_placement_locations(Pins, layout, pin_spacing=0.20):
    TOP_OF_PAGE = 32.9
    BOTTOM_OF_PAGE = 0.0
    LEFT_OF_PAGE = 0.0
    RIGHT_OF_PAGE = 42.9

    PIN_OFFSET_FROM_CONN = 3.0

    X_START = 1.0
    Y_START = 32.5
    main_conn_locs = [] # conn, x, y, flip

    # Center the shorter connector column vertically against the longer one.
    # Example:
    #   LEFT  = [P1, P3, P4]
    #   RIGHT = [P2]
    # gives P2 the same Y as P3.
    active_rows = [
        [conn for conn in row if conn is not None]
        for row in layout
    ]

    max_connector_count = max(
        (len(row) for row in active_rows),
        default=0
    )

    for i, row in enumerate(active_rows):
        vertical_offset = (max_connector_count - len(row)) / 2.0

        for j, conn in enumerate(row):
            flip = i > 0
            y_index = j + vertical_offset

            main_conn_locs.append([
                conn,
                X_START + (i * 3.0),
                Y_START - (y_index * 1.0),
                flip
            ])

    y_pin_start = 32.9
    for r in main_conn_locs:
        if y_pin_start > r[2]:
            y_pin_start = r[2]
    y_pin_start -= PIN_OFFSET_FROM_CONN

    print(Pins)
    print(layout)

    pin_locs = [] # conn, xstart, ystart, flip

    for dictionary in Pins:
        # Actual connector designation from the E3 device name.
        # Examples: W1.1 -> "1", W1.9B -> "9B".
        conn_key = list(dictionary.keys())[0]
        conn = connector_designation_from_device_name(conn_key)

        # number of pins = all keys except the "W1.<designation>"
        count = len(dictionary) - 1

        top = True
        flip = 0
        xstart = 0
        ystart = 0

        # find this connector's placement data
        for r in main_conn_locs:
            if str(r[0]) == conn:
                xstart = r[1]
                ystart = r[2]
                flip = r[3]

        # determine if connector is below another connector
        for r in main_conn_locs:
            if r[3] == flip:       # same flip side (top or bottom)
                if ystart < r[2]:  # this connector is below another
                    top = False

        if top:
            # normal top placement
            ystart = y_pin_start
        else:
            # Below another connector, use the same D15-selected vertical
            # wire spacing as the actual placed schematic pin views.

            pins_above = 0
            conns_above = 0

            # iterate over all pin dictionaries ABOVE our current connector
            for r in Pins:
                r_conn_key = list(r.keys())[0]
                r_conn = connector_designation_from_device_name(r_conn_key)

                if r_conn == conn:
                    break  # stop when reaching our connector

                # check if the connector is above AND same flip
                for c in main_conn_locs:
                    if str(c[0]) == r_conn and c[3] == flip:
                        if c[2] > ystart:
                            pins_above += (len(r) - 1)
                            conns_above += 1

            ystart = y_pin_start - (pins_above * pin_spacing) - (conns_above * 0.5)

        pin_locs.append([conn, xstart, ystart, flip])

    return main_conn_locs, pin_locs, y_pin_start + 2

def generate_aligned_pin_y(pinouts, y_start, pin_spacing=0.20, connector_gap=0.5):
    pin_y = {}

    current_y = y_start

    previous_c1 = None
    previous_c2 = None

    for row_index, (c1, p1, c2, p2) in enumerate(pinouts):
        if c1 is None or c2 is None:
            continue

        # Add a gap whenever we move into a new connector group
        # on EITHER side of the cable.
        if previous_c1 is not None:
            connector_changed = (
                c1 != previous_c1
                or c2 != previous_c2
            )

            if connector_changed:
                current_y -= connector_gap

        # Both ends of this wire get EXACTLY the same Y.
        pin_y[(str(c1), str(p1))] = current_y
        pin_y[(str(c2), str(p2))] = current_y

        current_y -= pin_spacing

        previous_c1 = c1
        previous_c2 = c2

    return pin_y

def get_spare_pins(pins, pinouts):
    spares = {}

    # Build quick connector -> pin dictionary lookup
    pin_lookup = {}

    for pindict in pins:
        connector = list(pindict.keys())[0]
        pin_lookup[connector] = pindict

    for c1, p1, c2, p2 in pinouts:

        # ==========================================
        # SPARE ON SIDE 1
        # ==========================================
        if c1 is not None and p1 is not None and c2 is None:
            connector = f"W1.{c1}"

            if connector not in spares:
                spares[connector] = {}

            if connector in pin_lookup:
                pin_id = pin_lookup[connector].get(str(p1))

                if pin_id is not None:
                    spares[connector][str(p1)] = pin_id

        # ==========================================
        # SPARE ON SIDE 2
        # ==========================================
        elif c2 is not None and p2 is not None and c1 is None:
            connector = f"W1.{c2}"

            if connector not in spares:
                spares[connector] = {}

            if connector in pin_lookup:
                pin_id = pin_lookup[connector].get(str(p2))

                if pin_id is not None:
                    spares[connector][str(p2)] = pin_id

    return spares

#
# This file is intentionally based directly on the script the user
# confirmed resized/placed Shield_Demo correctly.
#
# Do NOT change these working E3 behaviors:
#   - outer shields are attempted FIRST
#   - inner shields are attempted before twists
#   - each shield attempt gets a fresh CreateSymbolObject()
#   - Shield_Demo uses Load -> Place -> immediate SetDisplayLength
#   - SetId/GetDisplayLength happen only AFTER that immediate stretch attempt
#
# Outer shields are now split at every connector change: P1, P3 and P4
# each receive a separate physical shield, even when Excel marks continue.
# ======================================================================

#====================================================================================================================================
# FILE PICKER

def ask_excel_path():
    root = tk.Tk()
    root.withdraw()
    root.attributes("-topmost", True)
    root.update()
    selected_path = filedialog.askopenfilename(title="Select Cable Symbol Placement Excel File", filetypes=[("Excel Files", "*.xlsx"), ("Excel Macro Files", "*.xlsm"), ("All Files", "*.*")])
    root.destroy()

    if not selected_path:
        return None

    return os.path.abspath(selected_path)


#====================================================================================================================================
# VALUE HELPERS
#====================================================================================================================================
def clean_excel_value(value):
    if value is None:
        return ""

    if isinstance(value, int):
        return str(value)

    if isinstance(value, float):
        if value.is_integer():
            return str(int(value))

        return str(value).strip()

    return str(value).strip()


def normalize_pin_name(value):
    return clean_excel_value(value).upper()


def normalize_connector_token(value):
    """
    Normalize an Excel/E3 connector reference to the layout designation only.

    Examples:
        3       -> "3"
        P3      -> "3"
        W1.3    -> "3"
        W1.P3   -> "3"
        9B      -> "9B"
        P9B     -> "9B"
        W1.9B   -> "9B"

    A leading P is removed only for the old P<number> form.  Names such as
    PMOD are left alone.
    """
    text = clean_excel_value(value).upper().replace(" ", "")

    if text == "":
        return ""

    root_prefix = CONNECTOR_ROOT_NAME.upper() + "."

    if text.startswith(root_prefix):
        text = text[len(root_prefix):]

    if "#" in text:
        text = text.split("#", 1)[0]

    match = re.fullmatch(r"P?0*(\d+)([A-Z]*)", text)

    if match:
        number = int(match.group(1))
        suffix = match.group(2)
        return f"{number}{suffix}"

    return text


def connector_base_name(connector_token):
    connector = normalize_connector_token(connector_token)

    if connector == "":
        return ""

    # Cable-maker assembly members are named %.<designation>, so E3 exposes
    # them as W1.<designation> (for example W1.3 and W1.9B), NOT W1.P3.
    return f"{CONNECTOR_ROOT_NAME}.{connector}"


def cell_has_bar(value):
    return "|" in clean_excel_value(value)


#====================================================================================================================================
# YES / NO SETTING
#====================================================================================================================================
def parse_yes_no(value, setting_name):
    text = clean_excel_value(value).upper()

    if text == "":
        print("WARNING:", setting_name, "IS BLANK - DEFAULTING TO YES")
        return True

    if text in ["YES", "Y", "TRUE", "1"]:
        return True

    if text in ["NO", "N", "FALSE", "0"]:
        return False

    raise Exception(f"{setting_name} must contain YES or NO. Current value: {value}")


#====================================================================================================================================
# READ CABLE PIN SPACING (D15)
#====================================================================================================================================
def read_pin_spacing_setting(ws):
    selected = clean_excel_value(ws[PIN_SPACING_MODE_CELL].value)
    normalized = " ".join(selected.upper().split())

    if not normalized:
        print(
            "D15 is blank; defaulting to Default Zuken Spacing (0.20)."
        )
        normalized = "DEFAULT ZUKEN SPACING"

    if normalized == "DEFAULT ZUKEN SPACING":
        return "Default Zuken Spacing", DEFAULT_ZUKEN_PIN_SPACING

    if normalized == "INCREASED SPACING":
        return "Increased Spacing", INCREASED_PIN_SPACING

    raise ValueError(
        f"Cell {PIN_SPACING_MODE_CELL} must be 'Default Zuken Spacing' "
        f"or 'Increased Spacing'. Found: {selected!r}"
    )


#====================================================================================================================================
# READ TWIST OFFSET SETTINGS
#
# J2:K2 merged -> J2
# R2:S2 merged -> R2
#====================================================================================================================================
def read_twist_offset_settings(ws):
    left_enabled = parse_yes_no(ws[LEFT_TWIST_OFFSET_CELL].value, "LEFT TWIST OFFSET J2:K2")
    right_enabled = parse_yes_no(ws[RIGHT_TWIST_OFFSET_CELL].value, "RIGHT TWIST OFFSET R2:S2")

    settings = {
        "LEFT": left_enabled,
        "RIGHT": right_enabled
    }

    return settings


#====================================================================================================================================
# FIND LAST DATA ROW
#====================================================================================================================================
def get_last_data_row(ws):
    columns = [LEFT_OUTER_SHIELD_COL, LEFT_INNER_SHIELD_COL, LEFT_TWIST_A_COL, LEFT_TWIST_B_COL, LEFT_CONNECTOR_COL, PIN_1_COL, PIN_2_COL, RIGHT_CONNECTOR_COL, RIGHT_TWIST_A_COL, RIGHT_TWIST_B_COL, RIGHT_INNER_SHIELD_COL, RIGHT_OUTER_SHIELD_COL]
    scan_end = min(max(ws.max_row, DATA_FIRST_ROW), DATA_FIRST_ROW + MAX_SCAN_ROWS)
    last_row = DATA_FIRST_ROW - 1

    for row in range(DATA_FIRST_ROW, scan_end + 1):
        for col in columns:
            value = clean_excel_value(ws[f"{col}{row}"].value)

            if value != "":
                last_row = row
                break

    return last_row


#====================================================================================================================================
# READ LEFT OR RIGHT SIDE
#====================================================================================================================================
def read_side_rows(ws, side, last_row):
    if side == "LEFT":
        connector_col = LEFT_CONNECTOR_COL
        pin_col = PIN_1_COL
        twist_a_col = LEFT_TWIST_A_COL
        twist_b_col = LEFT_TWIST_B_COL
        inner_col = LEFT_INNER_SHIELD_COL
        outer_col = LEFT_OUTER_SHIELD_COL
    else:
        connector_col = RIGHT_CONNECTOR_COL
        pin_col = PIN_2_COL
        twist_a_col = RIGHT_TWIST_A_COL
        twist_b_col = RIGHT_TWIST_B_COL
        inner_col = RIGHT_INNER_SHIELD_COL
        outer_col = RIGHT_OUTER_SHIELD_COL

    rows = []
    current_connector = ""

    for excel_row in range(DATA_FIRST_ROW, last_row + 1):
        connector_value = clean_excel_value(ws[f"{connector_col}{excel_row}"].value)
        pin_name = normalize_pin_name(ws[f"{pin_col}{excel_row}"].value)

        if connector_value != "":
            current_connector = normalize_connector_token(connector_value)

        if pin_name == "":
            continue

        row_info = {
            "side": side,
            "excel_row": excel_row,
            "connector": current_connector,
            "pin": pin_name,
            "twist_a": cell_has_bar(ws[f"{twist_a_col}{excel_row}"].value),
            "twist_b": cell_has_bar(ws[f"{twist_b_col}{excel_row}"].value),
            "inner_shield": cell_has_bar(ws[f"{inner_col}{excel_row}"].value),
            "outer_shield": cell_has_bar(ws[f"{outer_col}{excel_row}"].value)
        }

        rows.append(row_info)

    return rows


#====================================================================================================================================
# SPLIT INTO CONNECTOR SECTIONS
#====================================================================================================================================
def split_into_sections(rows):
    sections = []
    current = []

    for row in rows:
        if not current:
            current = [row]
            continue

        previous = current[-1]
        connector_changed = row["connector"] != previous["connector"]
        row_gap = row["excel_row"] != previous["excel_row"] + 1

        if connector_changed or row_gap:
            sections.append(current)
            current = [row]
        else:
            current.append(row)

    if current:
        sections.append(current)

    return sections


#====================================================================================================================================
# CONTIGUOUS MARKED RANGES
#====================================================================================================================================
def true_ranges(rows, key):
    ranges = []
    start = None

    for index, row in enumerate(rows):
        marked = bool(row[key])

        if marked and start is None:
            start = index

        if not marked and start is not None:
            ranges.append((start, index - 1))
            start = None

    if start is not None:
        ranges.append((start, len(rows) - 1))

    return ranges


def range_pins(rows, start, end):
    pins = []

    for index in range(start, end + 1):
        pins.append(rows[index]["pin"])

    return pins


def range_excel_rows(rows, start, end):
    excel_rows = []

    for index in range(start, end + 1):
        excel_rows.append(rows[index]["excel_row"])

    return excel_rows



#====================================================================================================================================
# BUILD TWIST / INNER-SHIELD JOBS FOR ONE CONNECTOR SECTION
#
# INNER SHIELD RULE:
#   Inner SHLD does NOT create a standalone shield.
#   It only places Shield_Demo around a twist run when every wire in
#   that twist run is marked in the inner-SHLD column.
#
# OUTER SHIELDS ARE BUILT SEPARATELY AND SPLIT AT CONNECTOR CHANGES,
# EVEN WHEN OUTER-SHIELD MARKS CONTINUE (EXAMPLE: P1 -> P3 -> P4).
#====================================================================================================================================
def build_section_jobs(rows):
    jobs = []
    warnings = []

    if not rows:
        return jobs, warnings

    side = rows[0]["side"]
    connector = rows[0]["connector"]

    if connector == "":
        warnings.append(f"{side}: rows {rows[0]['excel_row']}-{rows[-1]['excel_row']} have pins but no connector")
        return jobs, warnings

    conflicts = []

    for row in rows:
        if row["twist_a"] and row["twist_b"]:
            conflicts.append(row["excel_row"])

    if conflicts:
        message = f"{side} {connector}: same wire marked in both twist columns on Excel rows {conflicts}"

        if STRICT_TWIST_VALIDATION:
            raise Exception(message)

        warnings.append(message)

    twist_jobs = []

    for key, twist_column in [("twist_a", "A"), ("twist_b", "B")]:
        ranges = true_ranges(rows, key)

        for start, end in ranges:
            pins = range_pins(rows, start, end)
            excel_rows = range_excel_rows(rows, start, end)

            if len(pins) < 2:
                warnings.append(
                    f"{side} {connector}: twist {twist_column} only contains one wire "
                    f"on Excel row {excel_rows[0]}"
                )
                continue

            job = {
                "type": "twist",
                "side": side,
                "connector": connector,
                "twist_column": twist_column,
                "start": start,
                "end": end,
                "pins": pins,
                "excel_rows": excel_rows,
                "symbol_name": TWIST_SYMBOL_NAME
            }

            twist_jobs.append(job)
            jobs.append(job)

    # Inner SHLD only wraps an existing twist. It never makes its own span.
    for twist_job in twist_jobs:
        indexes = list(range(twist_job["start"], twist_job["end"] + 1))
        inner_marks = [bool(rows[index]["inner_shield"]) for index in indexes]

        if all(inner_marks):
            jobs.append({
                "type": "shield_inner",
                "side": side,
                "connector": connector,
                "twist_column": twist_job["twist_column"],
                "pins": list(twist_job["pins"]),
                "excel_rows": list(twist_job["excel_rows"]),
                "symbol_name": SHIELD_SYMBOL_NAME
            })
        elif any(inner_marks):
            warnings.append(
                f"{side} {connector}: inner SHLD partially covers twist "
                f"{twist_job['twist_column']} on Excel rows {twist_job['excel_rows']}; "
                f"inner shield ignored because the full twist must be marked"
            )

    return jobs, warnings


#====================================================================================================================================
# BUILD ONE OUTER-SHIELD JOB PER CONTIGUOUS RUN ON ONE CONNECTOR
#
# A connector change ends the current shield, even when both rows are
# marked in Excel.  Blank Excel row gaps and unmarked rows also end it.
# Each new connector gets its own Shield_Demo and its own calculated span.
#====================================================================================================================================
def build_outer_shield_jobs(rows):
    jobs = []

    if not rows:
        return jobs

    side = rows[0]["side"]
    start = None

    def append_job(start_index, end_index):
        covered_rows = rows[start_index:end_index + 1]

        connector = covered_rows[0]["connector"]
        if any(row["connector"] != connector for row in covered_rows):
            raise ValueError("Outer shield job must contain exactly one connector")

        jobs.append({
            "type": "shield_outer",
            "side": side,
            "connector": connector,
            "connectors": [connector],
            "rows": covered_rows,
            "pins": [row["pin"] for row in covered_rows],
            "excel_rows": [row["excel_row"] for row in covered_rows],
            "twist_column": None,
            "symbol_name": SHIELD_SYMBOL_NAME
        })

    for index, row in enumerate(rows):
        marked = bool(row["outer_shield"])

        if start is not None:
            previous = rows[index - 1]
            row_gap = row["excel_row"] != previous["excel_row"] + 1
            connector_changed = row["connector"] != previous["connector"]

            if row_gap or connector_changed or not marked:
                append_job(start, index - 1)
                start = None

        if marked and start is None:
            start = index

    if start is not None:
        append_job(start, len(rows) - 1)

    return jobs


#====================================================================================================================================
# E3 ID EXTRACTOR
#====================================================================================================================================
def extract_e3_ids(result):
    if result is None:
        return []

    if isinstance(result, int):
        if result > 0:
            return [result]

        return []

    if isinstance(result, list):
        output = []
