        for value in result:
            if value is not None:
                try:
                    if int(value) > 0:
                        output.append(int(value))
                except Exception:
                    pass

        return output

    if not isinstance(result, tuple):
        return []

    if len(result) == 0:
        return []

    if len(result) >= 2:
        values = result[1]

        if values is None:
            return []

        if isinstance(values, int):
            if values > 0:
                return [values]

            return []

        try:
            return [int(value) for value in values if value is not None and int(value) > 0]
        except Exception:
            pass

    output = []

    for value in result:
        try:
            if int(value) > 0:
                output.append(int(value))
        except Exception:
            pass

    return output


#====================================================================================================================================
# GET ALL BASE DEVICES
#====================================================================================================================================
def get_all_devices(prj, dev):
    info = prj.GetAllDeviceIds(None)
    ids = extract_e3_ids(info)
    records = []

    for device_id in ids:
        dev.SetId(device_id)

        try:
            name = clean_excel_value(dev.GetName())
        except Exception:
            continue

        records.append({"device_id": device_id, "name": name})

    return records


#====================================================================================================================================
# READ PIN SCHEMATIC LOCATION
#====================================================================================================================================
def get_pin_location(pin, pin_id):
    pin.SetId(pin_id)

    xloc = [0.0]
    yloc = [0.0]
    grid = [""]
    column = [""]
    row = [""]

    try:
        result = pin.GetSchemaLocation(xloc, yloc, grid, column, row)
    except Exception:
        return None

    if not isinstance(result, tuple):
        return None

    if len(result) < 3:
        return None

    try:
        sheet_id = int(result[0])
        x = float(result[1])
        y = float(result[2])
    except Exception:
        return None

    if sheet_id <= 0:
        return None

    return {"sheet_id": sheet_id, "x": x, "y": y}


#====================================================================================================================================
# READ SYMBOL SCHEMATIC LOCATION
#====================================================================================================================================
def get_symbol_location(sym, symbol_id):
    sym.SetId(symbol_id)

    xloc = [0.0]
    yloc = [0.0]
    grid = [""]
    column = [""]
    row = [""]

    try:
        result = sym.GetSchemaLocation(xloc, yloc, grid, column, row)
    except Exception:
        return None

    if not isinstance(result, tuple):
        return None

    if len(result) < 3:
        return None

    try:
        sheet_id = int(result[0])
        x = float(result[1])
        y = float(result[2])
    except Exception:
        return None

    if sheet_id <= 0:
        return None

    return {"sheet_id": sheet_id, "x": x, "y": y}


#====================================================================================================================================
# LOAD DEVICE PIN MAP
#====================================================================================================================================
def load_device_pin_map(dev, pin, device_id):
    # Use the SAME view-pin enumeration fallback as the working placer.
    # Some E3 views expose pins through GetPinIds rather than GetAllPinIds.
    pin_ids = get_connector_pin_ids(
        dev, device_id, connector_name=f"overlay candidate {device_id}",
        prefer_all=True
    )
    pin_map = {}

    for pin_id in pin_ids:
        pin.SetId(pin_id)

        try:
            pin_name = normalize_pin_name(pin.GetName())
        except Exception:
            continue

        location = get_pin_location(pin, pin_id)

        if pin_name not in pin_map:
            pin_map[pin_name] = []

        if location is not None:
            record = {"pin_id": pin_id, "sheet_id": location["sheet_id"], "x": location["x"], "y": location["y"]}
            pin_map[pin_name].append(record)

    return pin_map


#====================================================================================================================================
# FIND BASE CONNECTOR
#====================================================================================================================================
def find_base_connector_devices(connector_token, all_devices, dev):
    """Find a connector with the same naming rules as the working placer.

    After E3 creates schematic views, a connector may be exposed as W1.P1,
    W1.01, or W1.1#1 instead of the literal W1.1.  The old overlay only
    accepted an exact name, which wrongly rejected those valid connectors.
    Never match a similarly numbered connector belonging to another cable.
    """
    expected_name = connector_base_name(connector_token)
    expected_upper = expected_name.upper()
    expected_designation = normalize_connector_token(connector_token)
    root_name = CONNECTOR_ROOT_NAME.upper()
    root_prefix = root_name + "."
    matches = []
    seen_ids = set()

    def add_match(record, reason):
        device_id = record["device_id"]
        if device_id not in seen_ids:
            matches.append(record)
            seen_ids.add(device_id)
            print("OVERLAY CONNECTOR MATCH:", expected_name,
                  "->", record["name"], "DEVICE ID:", device_id,
                  "MATCH TYPE:", reason)

    # Prefer the exact canonical name, but recognize the aliases already
    # supported by the main cable placer (P-prefix, leading zeroes, #suffix).
    for record in all_devices:
        device_name = clean_excel_value(record["name"])
        name_upper = device_name.upper()
        if name_upper == expected_upper:
            add_match(record, "EXACT")
        elif (name_upper.startswith(root_prefix)
              and normalize_connector_token(device_name) == expected_designation):
            add_match(record, "NORMALIZED")

    # E3 can report an assembly member's LOCAL name (1/P1) rather than
    # W1.1.  Accept those only if its actual parent assembly is W1.
    if not matches:
        for record in all_devices:
            device_name = clean_excel_value(record["name"])
            if normalize_connector_token(device_name) != expected_designation:
                continue
            if device_name.upper().startswith(root_prefix):
                continue
            try:
                dev.SetId(record["device_id"])
                assembly_id = int(dev.GetAssemblyId())
                if assembly_id <= 0:
                    continue
                dev.SetId(assembly_id)
                if clean_excel_value(dev.GetName()).upper() == root_name:
                    add_match(record, "ASSEMBLY MEMBER")
            except Exception as error:
                print("OVERLAY LOCAL-NAME MATCH FAILED:", device_name, repr(error))

    # On some E3 versions the assembly children aren't included in
    # GetAllDeviceIds.  Explicitly enumerate children of the W1 assembly.
    if not matches:
        for root_record in all_devices:
            if clean_excel_value(root_record["name"]).upper() != root_name:
                continue
            for child_id in get_child_device_ids(dev, root_record["device_id"]):
                try:
                    dev.SetId(child_id)
                    child_name = clean_excel_value(dev.GetName())
                except Exception:
                    continue
                if normalize_connector_token(child_name) == expected_designation:
                    add_match({"device_id": child_id, "name": child_name},
                              "W1 ASSEMBLY CHILD")

    if not matches:
        cable_names = [record["name"] for record in all_devices
                       if clean_excel_value(record["name"]).upper().startswith(root_prefix)]
        print("OVERLAY LOOKUP FAILED FOR:", expected_name)
        print("AVAILABLE", root_name, "CONNECTORS:", cable_names)

    return matches


#====================================================================================================================================
# GET VIEW IDS
#====================================================================================================================================
def get_device_view_ids(dev, base_device_id):
    dev.SetId(base_device_id)

    if not hasattr(dev, "GetViewIds"):
        print("GetViewIds IS NOT AVAILABLE ON THIS E3 DEVICE OBJECT")
        return []

    try:
        result = dev.GetViewIds(None)
    except Exception as error:
        print("GetViewIds FAILED FOR BASE ID:", base_device_id, "ERROR:", repr(error))
        return []

    view_ids = extract_e3_ids(result)

    print("BASE DEVICE ID:", base_device_id, "VIEW IDS:", view_ids)

    return view_ids


#====================================================================================================================================
# GET CHILD DEVICE IDS
#====================================================================================================================================
def get_child_device_ids(dev, base_device_id):
    dev.SetId(base_device_id)

    if not hasattr(dev, "GetDeviceIds"):
        return []

    try:
        result = dev.GetDeviceIds(None)
    except Exception:
        return []

    return extract_e3_ids(result)


#====================================================================================================================================
# GET SAME NAME ASSEMBLY DEVICES
#====================================================================================================================================
def get_same_name_assembly_devices(dev, base_device_id, expected_name):
    found_ids = []

    dev.SetId(base_device_id)

    try:
        assembly_id = dev.GetAssemblyId()
    except Exception:
        return found_ids

    if assembly_id is None:
        return found_ids

    try:
        assembly_id = int(assembly_id)
    except Exception:
        return found_ids

    if assembly_id <= 0:
        return found_ids

    dev.SetId(assembly_id)

    try:
        device_info = dev.GetDeviceIds(None)
    except Exception:
        return found_ids

    device_ids = extract_e3_ids(device_info)

    for device_id in device_ids:
        dev.SetId(device_id)

        try:
            name = clean_excel_value(dev.GetName())
        except Exception:
            continue

        if (normalize_connector_token(name) == normalize_connector_token(expected_name)
                and (name.upper().startswith(CONNECTOR_ROOT_NAME.upper() + ".")
                     or name.upper() == expected_name.upper())):
            found_ids.append(device_id)

    return found_ids


#====================================================================================================================================
# COLLECT CONNECTOR CANDIDATES
#====================================================================================================================================
def collect_connector_candidate_ids(connector_token, all_devices, dev):
    expected_name = connector_base_name(connector_token)
    base_records = find_base_connector_devices(connector_token, all_devices, dev)

    if not base_records:
        root_prefix = CONNECTOR_ROOT_NAME.upper() + "."
        visible_names = [record["name"] for record in all_devices
                         if clean_excel_value(record["name"]).upper().startswith(root_prefix)]
        available = ", ".join(visible_names[:15]) if visible_names else "none enumerated"
        raise Exception(
            f"Could not find E3 connector/view for {expected_name}. "
            f"E3 currently reports these {CONNECTOR_ROOT_NAME} device names: {available}. "
            "Check that the selected workbook matches the open E3 cable "
            "and that its connector pin views have been placed."
        )

    candidate_sources = {}

    for record in base_records:
        base_id = record["device_id"]

        candidate_sources[base_id] = "BASE"

        view_ids = get_device_view_ids(dev, base_id)

        for view_id in view_ids:
            candidate_sources[view_id] = "VIEW"

        child_ids = get_child_device_ids(dev, base_id)

        for child_id in child_ids:
            if child_id not in candidate_sources:
                candidate_sources[child_id] = "CHILD"

        assembly_ids = get_same_name_assembly_devices(dev, base_id, expected_name)

        for assembly_device_id in assembly_ids:
            if assembly_device_id not in candidate_sources:
                candidate_sources[assembly_device_id] = "ASSEMBLY"

    return candidate_sources


#====================================================================================================================================
# ANALYZE CANDIDATE VIEW
#====================================================================================================================================
def analyze_candidate(required_pins, pin_map):
    sheets = {}

    for pin_name in required_pins:
        locations = pin_map.get(pin_name, [])

        for location in locations:
            sheet_id = location["sheet_id"]

            if sheet_id not in sheets:
                sheets[sheet_id] = {"pins": set(), "xs": [], "ys": []}

            sheets[sheet_id]["pins"].add(pin_name)
            sheets[sheet_id]["xs"].append(location["x"])
            sheets[sheet_id]["ys"].append(location["y"])

    if not sheets:
        return None

    results = []

    for sheet_id, data in sheets.items():
        placed_count = len(data["pins"])
        unique_x_values = set(round(value, COORDINATE_ROUND_DIGITS) for value in data["xs"])
        unique_y_values = set(round(value, COORDINATE_ROUND_DIGITS) for value in data["ys"])
        unique_x_count = len(unique_x_values)
        unique_y_count = len(unique_y_values)
        x_span = max(data["xs"]) - min(data["xs"]) if data["xs"] else 0.0
        y_span = max(data["ys"]) - min(data["ys"]) if data["ys"] else 0.0
        has_real_y_spread = y_span >= SCHEMATIC_MIN_Y_SPAN and unique_y_count > 1
        result = {"sheet_id": sheet_id, "placed_count": placed_count, "unique_x_count": unique_x_count, "unique_y_count": unique_y_count, "x_span": x_span, "y_span": y_span, "has_real_y_spread": has_real_y_spread}

        results.append(result)

    results.sort(key=lambda item: (item["has_real_y_spread"], item["placed_count"], item["unique_y_count"], item["y_span"]), reverse=True)

    return results[0]


#====================================================================================================================================
# RESOLVE REAL SCHEMATIC VIEW
#====================================================================================================================================
def resolve_connector_schematic_view(connector_token, required_pins, all_devices, dev, pin, cache):
    expected_name = connector_base_name(connector_token)
    candidate_sources = collect_connector_candidate_ids(connector_token, all_devices, dev)
    candidates = []

    print("")
    print("============================================================")
    print("E3 CANDIDATES FOR:", expected_name)
    print("============================================================")

    for candidate_id, source in candidate_sources.items():
        dev.SetId(candidate_id)

        try:
            candidate_name = clean_excel_value(dev.GetName())
        except Exception:
            candidate_name = "UNKNOWN"

        if candidate_id not in cache:
            try:
                cache[candidate_id] = load_device_pin_map(dev, pin, candidate_id)
            except Exception as error:
                print("CANDIDATE ID:", candidate_id, "SOURCE:", source, "NAME:", candidate_name, "PIN MAP FAILED:", repr(error))
                continue

        pin_map = cache[candidate_id]
        analysis = analyze_candidate(required_pins, pin_map)

        if analysis is None:
            print("CANDIDATE ID:", candidate_id, "SOURCE:", source, "NAME:", candidate_name, "NO PLACED REQUIRED PINS")
            continue

        print("CANDIDATE ID:", candidate_id, "SOURCE:", source, "NAME:", candidate_name, "SHEET:", analysis["sheet_id"], "PLACED:", analysis["placed_count"], "UNIQUE X:", analysis["unique_x_count"], "UNIQUE Y:", analysis["unique_y_count"], "X SPAN:", analysis["x_span"], "Y SPAN:", analysis["y_span"])

        candidate = {"device_id": candidate_id, "name": candidate_name, "source": source, "pin_map": pin_map, "analysis": analysis}

        candidates.append(candidate)

    if not candidates:
        raise Exception(f"No usable E3 device/view candidates found for {expected_name}")

    candidates.sort(key=lambda item: (item["analysis"]["has_real_y_spread"], item["analysis"]["placed_count"], item["analysis"]["unique_y_count"], item["analysis"]["y_span"]), reverse=True)

    best = candidates[0]

    if len(required_pins) == 1:
        # A connector may have just ONE wire within its own outer shield.
        # One pin cannot establish vertical spread, so select a placed
        # schematic VIEW over an assembly/base-device candidate when possible.
        # Keep the original multi-pin view validation unchanged.
        view_priority = {"VIEW": 3, "CHILD": 2, "ASSEMBLY": 1, "BASE": 0}
        candidates.sort(
            key=lambda item: (
                view_priority.get(item["source"], 0),
                item["analysis"]["placed_count"],
            ),
            reverse=True,
        )
        best = candidates[0]
        print("SINGLE-PIN SHIELD VIEW: using placed-pin sheet without requiring Y spread")
    elif not best["analysis"]["has_real_y_spread"]:
        raise Exception(f"Could not locate the split schematic view for {expected_name}")

    sheet_id = best["analysis"]["sheet_id"]
    missing_pins = []

    for pin_name in required_pins:
        locations = best["pin_map"].get(pin_name, [])
        found_on_sheet = False

        for location in locations:
            if location["sheet_id"] == sheet_id:
                found_on_sheet = True
                break

        if not found_on_sheet:
            missing_pins.append(pin_name)

    if missing_pins:
        raise Exception(f"Selected schematic view for {expected_name} is missing pins on sheet {sheet_id}: {missing_pins}")

    print("")
    print("SELECTED SCHEMATIC VIEW ID:", best["device_id"])
    print("SELECTED SOURCE:", best["source"])
    print("SELECTED NAME:", best["name"])
    print("SELECTED SHEET:", sheet_id)
    print("SELECTED PLACED PIN COUNT:", best["analysis"]["placed_count"])
    print("SELECTED UNIQUE Y COUNT:", best["analysis"]["unique_y_count"])
    print("SELECTED Y SPAN:", best["analysis"]["y_span"])
    print("============================================================")

    record = {"device_id": best["device_id"], "name": best["name"]}

    return record, best["pin_map"], sheet_id


#====================================================================================================================================
# PIN LOCATION ON SELECTED SHEET
#====================================================================================================================================
def pin_location_on_sheet(pin_map, pin_name, sheet_id):
    locations = pin_map.get(pin_name, [])

    for location in locations:
        if location["sheet_id"] == sheet_id:
            return location

    return None


#====================================================================================================================================
# PRINT PIN LOCATIONS
#====================================================================================================================================
def print_section_pin_locations(required_pins, pin_map, sheet_id):
    print("")
    print("ACTUAL SCHEMATIC PIN LOCATIONS")

    for pin_name in required_pins:
        location = pin_location_on_sheet(pin_map, pin_name, sheet_id)

        if location is None:
            print("PIN:", pin_name, "LOCATION: NOT FOUND")
            continue

        print("PIN:", pin_name, "X:", location["x"], "Y:", location["y"])


#====================================================================================================================================
# JOB X OFFSET
#====================================================================================================================================
def job_offset(job, twist_offset_settings):
    offset_enabled = twist_offset_settings[job["side"]]

    if job["type"] == "twist":
        if not offset_enabled:
            return OFFSET_NO_TWIST_LINE

        if job["twist_column"] == "A":
            return OFFSET_YES_TWIST_A

        return OFFSET_YES_TWIST_B

    if job["type"] == "shield_inner":
        if job["twist_column"] is None:
            return INNER_SHIELD_ONLY_OFFSET

        if not offset_enabled:
            return OFFSET_NO_TWIST_LINE

        if job["twist_column"] == "A":
            return OFFSET_YES_TWIST_A

        return OFFSET_YES_TWIST_B

    if not offset_enabled:
        return OFFSET_NO_OUTER_SHIELD

    return OFFSET_YES_OUTER_SHIELD


#====================================================================================================================================
# DRAW ORDER
#
# ACTUAL WORKING ORDER INSIDE EACH CONNECTOR SECTION:
# Inner shields first, then twists.
# Connector-specific outer shields are processed before inner shields/twists.
#====================================================================================================================================
def job_priority(job):
    # This matches the earlier working dynamic-placement implementation:
    # shields are created before twists.
    if job["type"] == "shield_outer":
        return 0

    if job["type"] == "shield_inner":
        return 1

    return 2


#====================================================================================================================================
# CALCULATE FINAL SYMBOL PLACEMENT
#====================================================================================================================================
def calculate_placement(job, pin_map, sheet_id, twist_offset_settings):
    locations = []

    for pin_name in job["pins"]:
        location = pin_location_on_sheet(pin_map, pin_name, sheet_id)

        if location is None:
            raise Exception(f"{job['side']} {job['connector']}: pin {pin_name} has no schematic location on sheet {sheet_id}")

        locations.append(location)

    x_values = [location["x"] for location in locations]
    y_values = [location["y"] for location in locations]

    anchor_x = statistics.median(x_values)
    bottom_y = min(y_values)
    top_y = max(y_values)

    if top_y - bottom_y < SCHEMATIC_MIN_Y_SPAN and len(job["pins"]) > 1:
        raise Exception(f"{job['side']} {job['connector']} {job['type']}: pins {job['pins']} all have effectively the same Y coordinate")

    direction = 1.0 if job["side"] == "LEFT" else -1.0
    offset = job_offset(job, twist_offset_settings)
    x = anchor_x + direction * offset

    if job["type"] == "twist":
        y = bottom_y - TWIST_BOTTOM_OVERLAP
        length = None
    else:
        y = bottom_y - SHIELD_BOTTOM_OVERLAP
        length = top_y - bottom_y + SHIELD_BOTTOM_OVERLAP + SHIELD_TOP_OVERLAP

        if length < MIN_SYMBOL_LENGTH:
            length = MIN_SYMBOL_LENGTH

    placement = {
        "sheet_id": sheet_id,
        "x": x,
        "y": y,
        "length": length,
        "anchor_x": anchor_x,
        "bottom_y": bottom_y,
        "top_y": top_y,
        "direction": direction,
        "offset_enabled": twist_offset_settings[job["side"]]
    }

    return placement



#====================================================================================================================================
# CALCULATE ONE CONNECTOR'S OUTER SHIELD PLACEMENT
#
# The job builder guarantees every row belongs to the same connector.
# Resolve that connector's actual placed pins and size Shield_Demo only
# from its first to its last marked wire.  The existing E3 sizing and
# placement workflow below is intentionally unchanged.
#====================================================================================================================================
def calculate_outer_shield_placement(job, all_devices, dev, pin, cache, twist_offset_settings):
    resolved_by_connector = {}
    sheet_ids = set()
    locations = []

    rows_by_connector = {}

    for row in job["rows"]:
        connector = row["connector"]

        if connector == "":
            raise Exception(
                f"{job['side']} outer shield: Excel row {row['excel_row']} has no connector"
            )

        rows_by_connector.setdefault(connector, []).append(row)

    for connector, connector_rows in rows_by_connector.items():
        required_pins = list(dict.fromkeys(row["pin"] for row in connector_rows))

        device_record, pin_map, sheet_id = resolve_connector_schematic_view(
            connector,
            required_pins,
            all_devices,
            dev,
            pin,
            cache
        )

        resolved_by_connector[connector] = {
            "device_record": device_record,
            "pin_map": pin_map,
            "sheet_id": sheet_id
        }

        sheet_ids.add(sheet_id)

    if len(sheet_ids) != 1:
        details = ", ".join(
            f"{connector}=sheet {data['sheet_id']}"
            for connector, data in resolved_by_connector.items()
        )
        raise Exception(
            f"{job['side']} outer shield spans connectors on different E3 sheets: {details}"
        )

    sheet_id = next(iter(sheet_ids))

    for row in job["rows"]:
        connector = row["connector"]
        pin_name = row["pin"]
        pin_map = resolved_by_connector[connector]["pin_map"]

        location = pin_location_on_sheet(pin_map, pin_name, sheet_id)

        if location is None:
            raise Exception(
                f"{job['side']} outer shield: {connector} pin {pin_name} "
                f"has no schematic location on sheet {sheet_id}"
            )

        locations.append(location)

    if not locations:
        raise Exception(f"{job['side']} outer shield has no resolved schematic locations")

    x_values = [location["x"] for location in locations]
    y_values = [location["y"] for location in locations]

    anchor_x = statistics.median(x_values)
    bottom_y = min(y_values)
    top_y = max(y_values)

    direction = 1.0 if job["side"] == "LEFT" else -1.0

    if twist_offset_settings[job["side"]]:
        offset = OFFSET_YES_OUTER_SHIELD
    else:
        offset = OFFSET_NO_OUTER_SHIELD

    x = anchor_x + direction * offset
    y = bottom_y - SHIELD_BOTTOM_OVERLAP
    length = top_y - bottom_y + SHIELD_BOTTOM_OVERLAP + SHIELD_TOP_OVERLAP

    if length < MIN_SYMBOL_LENGTH:
        length = MIN_SYMBOL_LENGTH

    return {
        "sheet_id": sheet_id,
        "x": x,
        "y": y,
        "length": length,
        "anchor_x": anchor_x,
        "bottom_y": bottom_y,
        "top_y": top_y,
        "direction": direction,
        "offset_enabled": twist_offset_settings[job["side"]]
    }


#====================================================================================================================================
# SAFE DISPLAY LENGTH READ
#====================================================================================================================================
def get_display_length_safe(sym, symbol_id):
    sym.SetId(symbol_id)

    try:
        return float(sym.GetDisplayLength())
    except Exception:
        return None


#====================================================================================================================================
# TRY TO SIZE SHIELD AT CURRENT LOCATION
#====================================================================================================================================
def try_size_shield(sym, symbol_id, requested_length):
    # EXACT KNOWN-GOOD E3 SEQUENCE:
    #   Place -> SetId(placed id) -> GetDisplayLength ->
    #   SetDisplayLength -> SetId(placed id) -> GetDisplayLength
    sym.SetId(symbol_id)

    try:
        before = float(sym.GetDisplayLength())
    except Exception:
        before = None

    print("SHIELD LENGTH BEFORE:", before)

    try:
        stretch_result = sym.SetDisplayLength(float(requested_length))
    except Exception as error:
        stretch_result = None
        print("SHIELD STRETCH EXCEPTION:", repr(error))

    print("SHIELD STRETCH RESULT:", stretch_result)

    # Re-bind exactly like the standalone test that successfully resized.
    sym.SetId(symbol_id)
    time.sleep(STRETCH_DELAY_SECONDS)

    try:
        after = float(sym.GetDisplayLength())
    except Exception:
        after = None

    print("SHIELD REQUESTED LENGTH:", requested_length)
    print("SHIELD LENGTH AFTER:", after)

    return after is not None and abs(after - float(requested_length)) <= 0.001


def shield_stage_y_values(requested_length):
    # Shield_Demo grows upward from its insertion point.
    # 10.0 at Y=22.0 was proven to resize successfully in the earlier test.
    # For longer shields, lower the staging Y enough to keep the full symbol on-sheet.
    safe_y = min(
        SHIELD_STAGE_DEFAULT_Y,
        SHIELD_STAGE_TOP_LIMIT - float(requested_length) - SHIELD_STAGE_MARGIN
    )
    safe_y = max(0.75, safe_y)

    candidates = [
        safe_y,
        max(0.75, safe_y - 0.50),
        min(SHIELD_STAGE_DEFAULT_Y, safe_y + 0.50),
        max(0.75, safe_y - 1.00),
        min(SHIELD_STAGE_DEFAULT_Y, safe_y + 1.00),
    ]

    output = []
    for value in candidates:
        value = round(float(value), 3)
        if value not in output:
            output.append(value)

    return output


#====================================================================================================================================
# PLACE TWIST
#
# NO STRETCH.
#
# E3/database decides display length automatically.
#====================================================================================================================================
def place_twist(sym, job, placement):
    load_result = sym.Load(job["symbol_name"], SYMBOL_VERSION)

    print("TWIST LOAD RESULT:", load_result)

    if load_result == 0:
        raise Exception(f"Could not load library twist {job['symbol_name']}")

    print("TWIST TARGET X:", placement["x"])
    print("TWIST TARGET Y:", placement["y"])

    placed_id = sym.Place(placement["sheet_id"], placement["x"], placement["y"], 0, 1.0, 0)

    print("TWIST PLACE RESULT:", placed_id)

    if placed_id == 0:
        raise Exception(f"Could not place library twist {job['symbol_name']} at X={placement['x']} Y={placement['y']}")

    time.sleep(PLACEMENT_DELAY_SECONDS)

    sym.SetId(placed_id)

    try:
        placed_name = sym.GetName()
    except Exception:
        placed_name = "UNKNOWN"

    try:
        symbol_type = sym.GetSymbolType()
    except Exception:
        symbol_type = "UNKNOWN"

    try:
        automatic_length = float(sym.GetDisplayLength())
    except Exception:
        automatic_length = None

    print("TWIST PLACED ID:", placed_id)
    print("TWIST PLACED NAME:", placed_name)
    print("TWIST SYMBOL TYPE:", symbol_type)
    print("TWIST AUTOMATIC DISPLAY LENGTH:", automatic_length)
    print("TWIST LEFT AT E3 DATABASE AUTO LENGTH")

    return placed_id


# ======================================================================
# SHIELD PLACEMENT BACKPORT
# ======================================================================
# E3 symbol Load / Place / immediate SetDisplayLength and stage->move were
# restored from zuken_cable_overlay_v9_exact_working_core.py.
# Changes retained elsewhere: D15 0.20/0.25 (blank -> 0.20), no-offset
# outside twist position, connector aliases / pin view discovery, GUI.
# Extremely tall shields are staged only at valid on-page Y coordinates.
# Failed automatic stretches report failure instead of silently leaving an
# incorrectly-sized (native-length) shield.
# ======================================================================

#====================================================================================================================================
# PLACE SHIELD
#
# PROCESS:
#
# 1. Load Shield_Demo.
# 2. Search staging coordinates until the symbol can be placed.
# 3. Try SetDisplayLength at that staging position.
# 4. If resize fails, move SAME shield to another staging location.
# 5. Once correct size is reached, move SAME shield to final cable X/Y.
#
# No duplicate shields are intentionally created while searching.
#====================================================================================================================================
def place_shield(prj, job, placement):
    """
    Place a REAL Shield_Demo symbol and resize it with SetDisplayLength.

    CRITICAL E3 BEHAVIOR:
    The earlier working test called SetDisplayLength IMMEDIATELY after Place()
    on the SAME symbol object returned from Load/Place.

    Do NOT call SetId() first.
    Do NOT call GetDisplayLength() first.
    Do NOT use PlaceDynamic().

    Calling SetId before SetDisplayLength was the important difference in the
    later broken versions.
    """
    requested_length = float(placement["length"])
    final_x = float(placement["x"])
    final_y = float(placement["y"])
    sheet_id = int(placement["sheet_id"])

    print("")
    print("SHIELD FINAL X:", final_x)
    print("SHIELD FINAL Y:", final_y)
    print("SHIELD REQUESTED LENGTH:", requested_length)
    print("SHIELD LIBRARY SYMBOL:", job["symbol_name"])
    print("SHIELD MODE: Load -> Place -> IMMEDIATE SetDisplayLength")
    print("SHIELD NOTE: NO SetId / GetDisplayLength BEFORE STRETCH")

    # Restored from the earlier working V9 core: final position first, then
    # fresh objects at on-sheet staging coordinates, moving the SAME sized
    # symbol to its final location. Never leave an unstretched substitute.
    attempts = [("FINAL", final_x, final_y)]

    # Keep the proven 22.0 staging height for shorter shields.  For large
    # shields, lower the origin to fit the FULL stretched length on-sheet.
    # Do not try off-page heights; those attempts can fail indefinitely in E3.
    min_stage_y = 0.25
    stage_top = SHIELD_STAGE_TOP_LIMIT - SHIELD_STAGE_MARGIN
    max_stage_y = stage_top - requested_length

    if max_stage_y >= min_stage_y:
        stage_base_y = min(SHIELD_STAGE_DEFAULT_Y, max_stage_y)
        stage_y_values = [
            stage_base_y,
            stage_base_y - 0.5,
            stage_base_y + 0.5,
            stage_base_y - 1.0,
            stage_base_y + 1.0,
            min_stage_y,
        ]
        seen_stage_y = []
        for candidate_y in stage_y_values:
            candidate_y = round(float(candidate_y), 6)
            if not (min_stage_y <= candidate_y <= max_stage_y + 1e-6):
                continue
            if candidate_y not in seen_stage_y:
                seen_stage_y.append(candidate_y)

        for stage_y in seen_stage_y:
            for stage_x in SHIELD_STAGE_X_VALUES:
                attempts.append(("STAGE", float(stage_x), float(stage_y)))
    else:
        print(
            "SHIELD IS TOO TALL FOR ON-SHEET STAGING:", requested_length,
            "(highest allowed staging origin:", round(max_stage_y, 3), ")"
        )
        print("FINAL POSITION WILL STILL BE ATTEMPTED")

    for attempt_number, (mode, x, y) in enumerate(attempts, start=1):
        print("")
        print("SHIELD ATTEMPT:", attempt_number, "MODE:", mode, "X:", x, "Y:", y)

        # Fresh object for each attempt.
        shield_sym = prj.CreateSymbolObject()

        load_result = shield_sym.Load(job["symbol_name"], SYMBOL_VERSION)
        print("SHIELD LOAD RESULT:", load_result)

        if load_result == 0:
            try:
                del shield_sym
            except Exception:
                pass
            continue

        placed_id = shield_sym.Place(sheet_id, x, y, 0, 1.0, 0)
        print("SHIELD PLACE RESULT:", placed_id)

        if placed_id == 0:
            try:
                del shield_sym
            except Exception:
                pass
            time.sleep(PLACEMENT_DELAY_SECONDS)
            continue

        # ================================================================
        # THIS IS THE KEY FIX.
        #
        # The old working test did:
        #     Load(...)
        #     Place(...)
        #     SetDisplayLength(...)
        #
        # It did NOT SetId() between Place and SetDisplayLength.
        # It did NOT read GetDisplayLength() before SetDisplayLength.
        # ================================================================
        try:
            stretch_result = shield_sym.SetDisplayLength(requested_length)
        except Exception as error:
            stretch_result = None
            print("SHIELD IMMEDIATE STRETCH EXCEPTION:", repr(error))

        print("SHIELD IMMEDIATE STRETCH RESULT:", stretch_result)

        time.sleep(STRETCH_DELAY_SECONDS)

        try:
            immediate_length = float(shield_sym.GetDisplayLength())
        except Exception:
            immediate_length = None

        print("SHIELD IMMEDIATE LENGTH AFTER:", immediate_length)

        immediate_success = (
            immediate_length is not None
            and abs(immediate_length - requested_length) <= 0.001
        )

        # Only NOW bind by ID, after the resize attempt is already complete.
        try:
            set_id_result = shield_sym.SetId(placed_id)
            print("SHIELD POST-STRETCH SetId RESULT:", set_id_result)
        except Exception as error:
            print("SHIELD POST-STRETCH SetId EXCEPTION:", repr(error))

        try:
            verified_length = float(shield_sym.GetDisplayLength())
        except Exception:
            verified_length = immediate_length

        print("SHIELD VERIFIED LENGTH:", verified_length)

        verified_success = (
            verified_length is not None
            and abs(verified_length - requested_length) <= 0.001
        )

        if immediate_success or verified_success:
            print("SHIELD RESIZE SUCCESS")

            final_id = placed_id

            # If resized in staging, move that SAME real shield to final X/Y.
            if mode == "STAGE":
                time.sleep(MOVE_DELAY_SECONDS)

                try:
                    shield_sym.SetId(placed_id)
                except Exception:
                    pass

                move_result = shield_sym.Place(
                    sheet_id,
                    final_x,
                    final_y,
                    0,
                    1.0,
                    0
                )

                print("SHIELD FINAL MOVE RESULT:", move_result)

                if move_result == 0:
                    raise Exception(
                        f"{job['symbol_name']} resized correctly but could not "
                        f"move to final X={final_x} Y={final_y}"
                    )

                final_id = move_result
                time.sleep(PLACEMENT_DELAY_SECONDS)

            # Final verification.
            try:
                shield_sym.SetId(final_id)
            except Exception:
                pass

            try:
                final_length = float(shield_sym.GetDisplayLength())
            except Exception:
                final_length = None

            try:
                final_name = shield_sym.GetName()
            except Exception:
                final_name = "UNKNOWN"

            try:
                final_type = shield_sym.GetSymbolType()
            except Exception:
                final_type = "UNKNOWN"

            try:
                final_is_shield = shield_sym.IsShield()
            except Exception:
                final_is_shield = "UNKNOWN"

            final_location = get_symbol_location(shield_sym, final_id)

            print("SHIELD FINAL ID:", final_id)
            print("SHIELD FINAL NAME:", final_name)
            print("SHIELD FINAL SYMBOL TYPE:", final_type)
            print("SHIELD FINAL IS SHIELD:", final_is_shield)
            print("SHIELD FINAL DISPLAY LENGTH:", final_length)

            if final_location is not None:
                print("SHIELD FINAL SHEET:", final_location["sheet_id"])
                print("SHIELD FINAL ACTUAL X:", final_location["x"])
                print("SHIELD FINAL ACTUAL Y:", final_location["y"])

            if final_length is None or abs(final_length - requested_length) > 0.001:
                raise Exception(
                    f"{job['symbol_name']} resized successfully but final length "
                    f"changed. Expected {requested_length}, actual {final_length}"
                )

            print("SHIELD REAL SYMBOL + LENGTH VERIFIED")
            return final_id

        print("SHIELD RESIZE FAILED FOR THIS ATTEMPT")

        # Remove the failed trial before trying another position.
        try:
            shield_sym.SetId(placed_id)
            delete_result = shield_sym.Delete()
            print("FAILED SHIELD DELETE RESULT:", delete_result)
        except Exception as error:
            print("FAILED SHIELD DELETE EXCEPTION:", repr(error))

        try:
            del shield_sym
        except Exception:
            pass

        time.sleep(PLACEMENT_DELAY_SECONDS)

    raise Exception(
        f"{job['symbol_name']} could not resize to {requested_length}. "
        f"All attempts used Load -> Place -> immediate SetDisplayLength "
        f"without SetId before the stretch."
    )


#====================================================================================================================================
# PLACE ONE JOB
#====================================================================================================================================
def place_job(prj, sym, job, placement):
    print("")
    print("------------------------------------------------------------")
    print("TYPE:", job["type"])
    print("SIDE:", job["side"])
    print("TWIST OFFSET ENABLED:", placement["offset_enabled"])
    print("CONNECTOR:", job["connector"])
    print("TWIST COLUMN:", job.get("twist_column"))
    print("PINS:", job["pins"])
    print("EXCEL ROWS:", job["excel_rows"])
    print("LIBRARY SYMBOL:", job["symbol_name"])
    print("SHEET:", placement["sheet_id"])
    print("CONNECTOR PIN X:", placement["anchor_x"])
    print("FINAL SYMBOL X:", placement["x"])
    print("BOTTOM WIRE Y:", placement["bottom_y"])
    print("TOP WIRE Y:", placement["top_y"])
    print("FINAL SYMBOL Y:", placement["y"])

    if placement["length"] is None:
        print("REQUESTED SYMBOL LENGTH: AUTO")
    else:
        print("REQUESTED SYMBOL LENGTH:", placement["length"])

    if DRY_RUN:
        print("DRY RUN - NOT PLACED")
        return None

    if job["type"] == "twist":
        return place_twist(sym, job, placement)

    return place_shield(prj, job, placement)


#====================================================================================================================================
# MAIN
#====================================================================================================================================
def run_v9_symbol_overlay(excel_path):
    if excel_path is None:
        raise Exception("No Excel file selected")

    workbook = load_workbook(excel_path, data_only=True, read_only=False)

    if TARGET_SHEET_NAME is None:
        ws = workbook.active
    else:
        if TARGET_SHEET_NAME not in workbook.sheetnames:
            raise Exception(f"Worksheet {TARGET_SHEET_NAME} was not found")

        ws = workbook[TARGET_SHEET_NAME]

    sheet_device_reference = clean_excel_value(ws[DEVICE_NAME_CELL].value)
    twist_offset_settings = read_twist_offset_settings(ws)
    last_row = get_last_data_row(ws)

    if last_row < DATA_FIRST_ROW:
        raise Exception("No cable overlay data found")

    print("")
    print("============================================================")
    print("ZUKEN CABLE SHIELD / TWIST OVERLAY")
    print("============================================================")
    print("EXCEL:", excel_path)
    print("WORKSHEET:", ws.title)
    print("DEVICE REFERENCE FROM B1:", sheet_device_reference)
    print("CONNECTOR ROOT:", CONNECTOR_ROOT_NAME)
    print("SHIELD SYMBOL:", SHIELD_SYMBOL_NAME)
    print("TWIST SYMBOL:", TWIST_SYMBOL_NAME)
    print("LEFT TWIST OFFSET J2:K2:", "YES" if twist_offset_settings["LEFT"] else "NO")
    print("RIGHT TWIST OFFSET R2:S2:", "YES" if twist_offset_settings["RIGHT"] else "NO")
    print("PLACEMENT DELAY:", PLACEMENT_DELAY_SECONDS)
    print("TWIST STRETCHING: DISABLED")
    print("SHIELD MODE: REAL Shield_Demo -> Place -> immediate SetDisplayLength")
    print("DATA ROWS:", DATA_FIRST_ROW, "TO", last_row)

    left_rows = read_side_rows(ws, "LEFT", last_row)
    right_rows = read_side_rows(ws, "RIGHT", last_row)

    left_sections = split_into_sections(left_rows)
    right_sections = split_into_sections(right_rows)

    sections = left_sections + right_sections

    packages = []
    warnings = []

    # Twists and inner shields are connector-specific.
    for section_rows in sections:
        jobs, section_warnings = build_section_jobs(section_rows)

        if jobs:
            packages.append({"rows": section_rows, "jobs": jobs})

        warnings.extend(section_warnings)

    # Build separate outer shields for each contiguous marked connector run.
    outer_jobs = (
        build_outer_shield_jobs(left_rows) +
        build_outer_shield_jobs(right_rows)
    )

    print("")
    print("CONNECTOR SECTIONS WITH TWIST / INNER-SHIELD SYMBOLS:", len(packages))
    print("CONNECTOR-SPECIFIC OUTER SHIELD JOBS:", len(outer_jobs))

    if warnings:
        print("")
        print("============================================================")
        print("EXCEL WARNINGS")
        print("============================================================")

        for warning in warnings:
            print("WARNING:", warning)

    app = win32.gencache.EnsureDispatch("CT.Application")
    prj = app.CreateJobObject()
    dev = prj.CreateDeviceObject()
    pin = prj.CreatePinObject()
    sym = prj.CreateSymbolObject()

    app.PutMessage("Starting Cable Shield / Twist Overlay Script")

    all_devices = get_all_devices(prj, dev)

    print("")
    print("E3 BASE DEVICE ID COUNT:", len(all_devices))

    pin_map_cache = {}
    placed_ids = []

    # Place separate per-connector outer shields FIRST, preserving working order.
    for job in outer_jobs:
        try:
            print("")
            print("============================================================")
            print("PROCESSING CONNECTOR OUTER SHIELD")
            print("============================================================")
            print("SIDE:", job["side"])
            print("CONNECTORS:", job["connectors"])
            print("EXCEL ROWS:", job["excel_rows"][0], "TO", job["excel_rows"][-1])

            placement = calculate_outer_shield_placement(
                job,
                all_devices,
                dev,
                pin,
                pin_map_cache,
                twist_offset_settings
            )

            placed_id = place_job(prj, sym, job, placement)

            if placed_id is not None:
                placed_ids.append(placed_id)

        except Exception as error:
            print("ERROR:", str(error))
            app.PutMessage(f"Cable overlay warning: {error}")

        time.sleep(PLACEMENT_DELAY_SECONDS)


    for package in packages:
        section_rows = package["rows"]
        jobs = package["jobs"]

        side = section_rows[0]["side"]
        connector = section_rows[0]["connector"]

        required_pins = list(dict.fromkeys(row["pin"] for row in section_rows))

        print("")
        print("============================================================")
        print("PROCESSING CONNECTOR SECTION")
        print("============================================================")
        print("SIDE:", side)
        print("TWIST OFFSET ENABLED:", "YES" if twist_offset_settings[side] else "NO")
        print("EXCEL CONNECTOR:", connector)
        print("EXPECTED E3 CONNECTOR:", connector_base_name(connector))
        print("EXCEL ROWS:", section_rows[0]["excel_row"], "TO", section_rows[-1]["excel_row"])
        print("REQUIRED PINS:", required_pins)

        device_record, pin_map, sheet_id = resolve_connector_schematic_view(connector, required_pins, all_devices, dev, pin, pin_map_cache)

        print_section_pin_locations(required_pins, pin_map, sheet_id)

        # Inner shields are placed before twists, matching the earlier working dynamic-placement script.
        jobs = sorted(jobs, key=job_priority)

        for job in jobs:
            try:
                placement = calculate_placement(job, pin_map, sheet_id, twist_offset_settings)
                placed_id = place_job(prj, sym, job, placement)

                if placed_id is not None:
                    placed_ids.append(placed_id)

            except Exception as error:
                print("ERROR:", str(error))
                app.PutMessage(f"Cable overlay warning: {error}")

            time.sleep(PLACEMENT_DELAY_SECONDS)

    print("")
    print("============================================================")
    print("OVERLAY COMPLETE")
    print("============================================================")
    print("PLACED SYMBOL COUNT:", len(placed_ids))
    print("PLACED SYMBOL IDS:", placed_ids)
    print("============================================================")

    app.PutMessage(f"Cable Shield / Twist Overlay complete. Placed {len(placed_ids)} symbols.")

    workbook.close()

    del sym
    del pin
    del dev
    del prj
    del app


# ================================================
# PARSE EXCEL
# ================================================
START_MODE = choose_start_mode()

if START_MODE is None:
    print("Cancelled by user.")
    sys.exit(0)

if START_MODE == "FORMATTER_ONLY":
    print("")
    print("============================================================")
    print("MODE: SIMPLE FORMATTER RUN")
    print("============================================================")

    # ------------------------------------------------
    # FORMATTER-ONLY DISCOVERY
    #
    # The normal build/place path creates these globals while it
    # creates the cable:
    #
    #   CONNECTORS
    #   new_dev_ids
    #   NEW_DEV_CONNECTORS
    #   PINN_LOCS
    #
    # Formatter-only mode skips that entire build path, so identify
    # the same objects from the cable that is already open in E3.
    #
    # Nothing outside formatter-only mode is changed.
    # ------------------------------------------------
    formatter_app = win32.gencache.EnsureDispatch("CT.Application")
    formatter_prj = formatter_app.CreateJobObject()
    formatter_dev = formatter_prj.CreateDeviceObject()
    formatter_sym = formatter_prj.CreateSymbolObject()

    CONNECTORS = {}
    new_dev_ids = []
    NEW_DEV_CONNECTORS = {}
    PINN_LOCS = []

    device_info = formatter_prj.GetAllDeviceIds(None)
    formatter_device_ids = [
        x for x in device_info[1]
        if x is not None
    ]

    for formatter_device_id in formatter_device_ids:
        formatter_dev.SetId(formatter_device_id)

        try:
            formatter_name = str(formatter_dev.GetName()).strip()
        except Exception:
            continue

        if not formatter_name.upper().startswith(
            f"{CONNECTOR_ROOT_NAME.upper()}."
        ):
            continue

        connector_designation = connector_designation_from_device_name(
            formatter_name
        )

        if connector_designation == "":
            continue

        # A base connector owns its schematic view IDs.
        try:
            view_info = formatter_dev.GetViewIds(None)
            formatter_view_ids = extract_e3_ids(view_info)
        except Exception:
            formatter_view_ids = []

        if not formatter_view_ids:
            continue

        CONNECTORS[formatter_name] = formatter_device_id

        for formatter_view_id in formatter_view_ids:
            if formatter_view_id in NEW_DEV_CONNECTORS:
                continue

            formatter_dev.SetId(formatter_view_id)

            # Only use actual placed schematic views.
            try:
                symbol_info = formatter_dev.GetSymbolIds(None)
                formatter_symbol_ids = [
                    x for x in symbol_info[1]
                    if x is not None
                ]
            except Exception:
                formatter_symbol_ids = []

            if not formatter_symbol_ids:
                continue

            symbol_x_values = []

            for formatter_symbol_id in formatter_symbol_ids:
                formatter_sym.SetId(formatter_symbol_id)

                xloc = [0.0]
                yloc = [0.0]
                grid = [""]
                column = [""]
                row = [""]

                try:
                    location = formatter_sym.GetSchemaLocation(
                        xloc,
                        yloc,
                        grid,
                        column,
                        row
                    )

                    if isinstance(location, tuple) and len(location) >= 3:
                        symbol_x_values.append(float(location[1]))
                except Exception:
                    pass

            if not symbol_x_values:
                continue

            new_dev_ids.append(formatter_view_id)
            NEW_DEV_CONNECTORS[formatter_view_id] = formatter_name

            # run_ct_code() only needs PINN_LOCS here to recover the
            # main left/right schematic X columns for centering the
            # single Additional Part ID.
            formatter_view_x = statistics.median(symbol_x_values)

            PINN_LOCS.append([
                connector_designation,
                formatter_view_x,
                0.0,
                False
            ])

            print(
                "FORMATTER FOUND VIEW:",
                formatter_view_id,
                "| CONNECTOR:",
                formatter_name,
                "| X:",
                formatter_view_x
            )

    del formatter_sym
    del formatter_dev
    del formatter_prj
    del formatter_app

    print("FORMATTER BASE CONNECTORS:", CONNECTORS)
    print("FORMATTER SCHEMATIC VIEWS:", new_dev_ids)

    if not new_dev_ids:
        raise Exception(
            "Formatter-only mode could not find any placed W1.<designation> "
            "schematic connector views in the open E3 job."
        )

    create_gui()

    print("Formatter-only run complete.")
    sys.exit(0)

output_path = ask_save_path()
wb = load_workbook(output_path, data_only=True)
ws = wb.active

NO_WIRES = int(ws["B15"].value)
VERSION = ws["B2"].value
NAME = ws["B1"].value
SPACING_MODE, PIN_SPACING = read_pin_spacing_setting(ws)
print(f"PIN SPACING D15: {SPACING_MODE} ({PIN_SPACING:.2f} per pin)")


def placer_connector_designation(value):
    """
    Return the actual connector layout designation as a string.

    Accepts 9, 9B, P9B, W1.9B, or W1.9B.  The canonical result is 9B.
    """
    text = normalize_connector_token(value)
    return text if text != "" else None


# ------------------------------------------------
# CONNECTOR PART / TYPE ASSIGNMENT
#
# B3:B14 contains connector part numbers/types.
# Populated layout cells consume those parts in this exact order:
#   W4:W11 top-to-bottom, then Z4:Z11 top-to-bottom.
#
# The layout value is the actual connector designation.  It does NOT have
# to be 1-12.  Examples: 1, 9, 9B, 12A.
# ------------------------------------------------
CONNECTOR_PARTS = [
    ws[f"B{r}"].value
    for r in range(3, 15)
    if ws[f"B{r}"].value is not None
    and str(ws[f"B{r}"].value).strip() != ""
]

LEFT_CONNECTOR_LAYOUT = [
    placer_connector_designation(ws[f"W{r}"].value)
    for r in range(4, 12)
]
RIGHT_CONNECTOR_LAYOUT = [
    placer_connector_designation(ws[f"Z{r}"].value)
    for r in range(4, 12)
]

LEFT_CONNECTOR_LAYOUT = [
    connector for connector in LEFT_CONNECTOR_LAYOUT
    if connector is not None
]
RIGHT_CONNECTOR_LAYOUT = [
    connector for connector in RIGHT_CONNECTOR_LAYOUT
    if connector is not None
]

CONNECTOR_LAYOUT = LEFT_CONNECTOR_LAYOUT + RIGHT_CONNECTOR_LAYOUT

if len(CONNECTOR_LAYOUT) > 12:
    raise Exception(
        f"Cable placer supports up to 12 connectors. Found {len(CONNECTOR_LAYOUT)}."
    )

if len(LEFT_CONNECTOR_LAYOUT) > 8 or len(RIGHT_CONNECTOR_LAYOUT) > 8:
    raise Exception("No more than 8 connectors may be placed on either side.")

if len(CONNECTOR_LAYOUT) != len(CONNECTOR_PARTS):
    raise Exception(
        f"Connector layout count ({len(CONNECTOR_LAYOUT)}) does not match "
        f"connector part/type count ({len(CONNECTOR_PARTS)}). "
        "Check B3:B14 and W4:W11 / Z4:Z11."
    )

if len(set(CONNECTOR_LAYOUT)) != len(CONNECTOR_LAYOUT):
    raise Exception(
        f"Duplicate connector designation found in layout: {CONNECTOR_LAYOUT}"
    )

CONNECTOR_PART_MAP = dict(zip(CONNECTOR_LAYOUT, CONNECTOR_PARTS))

LAYOUT = [
    LEFT_CONNECTOR_LAYOUT,
    RIGHT_CONNECTOR_LAYOUT
]

print("LEFT CONNECTOR LAYOUT:", LEFT_CONNECTOR_LAYOUT)
print("RIGHT CONNECTOR LAYOUT:", RIGHT_CONNECTOR_LAYOUT)
print("CONNECTOR ASSIGNMENT ORDER (B3:B14 -> W4:W11 then Z4:Z11):")
for connector_designation in CONNECTOR_LAYOUT:
    print(
        f"  {connector_designation} -> "
        f"{CONNECTOR_PART_MAP[connector_designation]}"
    )


# ------------------------------------------------
# NEW SHEET CABLE MAPPING
#
# LEFT connector  = L
# LEFT pin        = N
# RIGHT pin       = O
# RIGHT connector = Q
#
# L/Q may contain 9, P9, W1.P9, 9B, P9B, W1.9B, etc.
# They resolve back to the actual layout designation.
# ------------------------------------------------
pinouts = []

current_connector1 = None
current_connector2 = None

for r in range(5, 5 + NO_WIRES):
    connector1_value = ws[f"L{r}"].value
    pin1 = ws[f"N{r}"].value

    pin2 = ws[f"O{r}"].value
    connector2_value = ws[f"Q{r}"].value

    # New sheets may only show the connector once at the top of its block.
    if clean_excel_value(connector1_value) != "":
        current_connector1 = placer_connector_designation(connector1_value)

    if clean_excel_value(connector2_value) != "":
        current_connector2 = placer_connector_designation(connector2_value)

    # Preserve the old spare-pin behavior: if a pin is blank, that endpoint
    # is still None even though the connector block itself is known.
    connector1 = current_connector1 if pin1 is not None else None
    connector2 = current_connector2 if pin2 is not None else None

    pinouts.append([connector1, pin1, connector2, pin2])


# Fail early if the cable table references a designation that does not exist
# in the W/Z connector layout.
for c1, _, c2, _ in pinouts:
    for connector_designation in (c1, c2):
        if (
            connector_designation is not None
            and connector_designation not in CONNECTOR_PART_MAP
        ):
            raise Exception(
                f"Cable table references connector {connector_designation}, "
                "but that designation is not present in W4:W11 / Z4:Z11."
            )


# ------------------------------------------------
# DETECT WHETHER THE SYMBOL STAGE IS NEEDED
#
# New symbol columns:
#   H = left outer shield
#   I = left inner shield
#   J/K = left twists
#   R/S = right twists
#   T = right inner shield
#   U = right outer shield
# ------------------------------------------------
SYMBOL_DEFINITION_COLUMNS = ["H", "I", "J", "K", "R", "S", "T", "U"]

HAS_SYMBOLS = any(
    cell_has_bar(ws[f"{column}{row}"].value)
    for row in range(5, 5 + NO_WIRES)
    for column in SYMBOL_DEFINITION_COLUMNS
)

print("NEW SHEET LAYOUT:", LAYOUT)
print("SHIELD / TWIST DEFINITIONS PRESENT:", HAS_SYMBOLS)

wb.close()


# ------------------------------------------------
# SYMBOLS-ONLY MODE
#
# Use the cable already open in E3.  Do not connect to the cable
# database workflow, do not create/load the cable component, and do
# not place connector blocks/pin views.
# ------------------------------------------------
if START_MODE == "SYMBOLS_ONLY":
    print("")
    print("============================================================")
    print("MODE: ADD SYMBOLS TO OPEN CABLE")
    print("============================================================")

    if HAS_SYMBOLS:
        run_v9_symbol_overlay(output_path)
    else:
        print("No shield / twist definitions found in the selected workbook.")
        messagebox.showinfo(
            "No Symbols Defined",
            "No shield or twist definitions were found in the selected workbook."
        )

    print("Symbols-only run complete.")
    sys.exit(0)


PINORDER = defaultdict(list)
for c1, p1, c2, p2 in pinouts:
    if c1 is not None and c2 is not None:
        PINORDER[c1].append(p1)
        PINORDER[c2].append(p2)

# ================================================
# CONNECT TO DB
# ================================================
CONNECTION_STRING = (
    "DRIVER={SQL Server};"
    "SERVER=sql-zuken-prd;"
    "DATABASE=Zuken_E3Library;"
    "Trusted_Connection=yes;"
)

conn = pyodbc.connect(CONNECTION_STRING, autocommit=True)
cur = conn.cursor()

# set objects
#====================================================================================================================================
app = win32.gencache.EnsureDispatch('CT.Application')
prj = app.CreateJobObject()
text = prj.CreateTextObject()
comp = prj.CreateComponentObject()
dev = prj.CreateDeviceObject()
sym = prj.CreateSymbolObject()
pin = prj.CreatePinObject()
attr = prj.CreateAttributeObject()
#====================================================================================================================================
# more setup
#====================================================================================================================================
app.PutMessage("Starting Pin Ordering Scrpit")

sht = prj.CreateSheetObject()
sheet_info = prj.GetAllSheetIds(None)
sheet_count = sheet_info[0]
sheet_ids = sheet_info[1]
sheet_ids = [x for x in sheet_ids if x is not None]

for id in sheet_ids:
    sht.SetId(id)
    if sht.GetName() != "1":
        sht.Delete()
    else:
        sheet_1_id = id

sheet1 = sht.SetId(sheet_1_id)


#====================================================================================================================================
# step 1 - get the component from the database
#====================================================================================================================================
name = NAME
ver = VERSION
res = app.GetDatabaseTableSelectedComponents(name, ver)
if(res[0] != 0):
    app.PutMessage(f"step 1 did not work, Result: {res}\n")
    raise Exception(f"Step 1 failed. No device found in database with name: {name}.\n")
else:
    app.PutMessage(f"Step 1 success. Device found in database. Result: {res}")

#====================================================================================================================================
# step 2 - place the component from the library into the device tree
#====================================================================================================================================
res = dev.Create("", "", "", name, ver, 0)
if(res == 0):
    app.PutMessage(f"step 2 did not work, Result: {res}\n")
    raise Exception("Step 2 failed. Device failed to be added to device tree.\n")
else:
    app.PutMessage(f"Step 2 success. Device added to device tree. Result: {res}")


#====================================================================================================================================
# step 3 - get the device, and pin info from tree and order it
#====================================================================================================================================
devinfo = prj.GetAllDeviceIds(None)
devcount = devinfo[0]
devids   = devinfo[1]
devids   = [x for x in devids if x is not None]
dev_total = len(devids)

if(dev_total == 0):
    app.PutMessage("Step 3 error, no devices found.\n")
    raise Exception("Step 3 failed. No devices found.\n")
else:
    app.PutMessage("Getting pin info now.")


# Build the connector device map directly from the populated layout.
# Example designation 9B -> expected E3 device W1.9B.
CONNECTORS = {
    f"W1.{designation}": None
    for designation in CONNECTOR_LAYOUT
}

# Match devices by their normalized layout designation instead of requiring
# one exact historical spelling.  New cable-maker output is W1.3 / W1.9B,
# while older cables may still contain W1.P3 / W1.P9B.
for id in devids:
    dev.SetId(id)
    name = str(dev.GetName()).strip()
    print(name)

    if not name.upper().startswith(f"{CONNECTOR_ROOT_NAME.upper()}."):
        continue

    designation = connector_designation_from_device_name(name)
    connector_key = f"W1.{designation}"

    if connector_key in CONNECTORS:
        CONNECTORS[connector_key] = id
        print(
            "MATCHED CONNECTOR DEVICE:",
            name,
            "->", connector_key,
            "DEVICE ID:", id
        )


PINS = []
for key, value in CONNECTORS.items():
    if value is not None:
        # IMPORTANT: the base assembly connector owns the physical pins.
        # GetPinIds is the correct first lookup here; GetAllPinIds can return
        # zero pins on otherwise valid assembly connector devices.
        pinids = get_connector_pin_ids(
            dev,
            value,
            connector_name=key,
            prefer_all=False
        )

        if not pinids:
            dev.SetId(value)
            try:
                component_name = dev.GetComponentName()
            except Exception:
                component_name = "UNKNOWN"

            raise Exception(
                f"No pins found for connector {key} "
                f"(device ID {value}, component {component_name})."
            )

        pindict = {key: value}
        for pin_id in pinids:
            pin.SetId(pin_id)
            pin_name = str(pin.GetName()).strip()
            pindict[pin_name] = pin_id

        print("CONNECTOR PINS:", key, pindict)
        PINS.append(pindict)

MAIN_CONN_LOCS, PINN_LOCS, Y_START = generate_placement_locations(
    PINS, LAYOUT, pin_spacing=PIN_SPACING
)
SPARES = get_spare_pins(PINS, pinouts)
print("spare pins: ", SPARES)
print('main_conn: ', MAIN_CONN_LOCS)
print('pin: ', PINN_LOCS)

PIN_Y = generate_aligned_pin_y(
    pinouts,
    y_start=Y_START,
    pin_spacing=PIN_SPACING,
    connector_gap=0.5
)

SPARE_X = 8.0
SPARE_Y = Y_START
SPARE_PIN_SPACING = PIN_SPACING
SPARE_CONN_GAP = 0.5

# print("ALIGNED PIN Y:")
# for key, value in PIN_Y.items():
#     print(key, value)

# BIG POPULATION LOOP==================================
new_dev_ids = []
NEW_DEV_CONNECTORS = {}
j = 1
for i, (connector, conn_id) in enumerate(CONNECTORS.items()):
    if conn_id is None:
        continue

    if j == 1:
        for _, (connector_loop, conn_id_loop) in enumerate(CONNECTORS.items()):
            if conn_id_loop == None:
                continue

            ids = []
            for pindict in PINS:
                if connector_loop in pindict:
                    for key, value in pindict.items():
                        if key != connector_loop:
                            ids.append(value)
                    break

            symname = "MilSymbolReceptacle¶FULL"
            p = connector_designation_from_device_name(connector_loop)
            for c in MAIN_CONN_LOCS:
                if str(c[0]) == p:
                    x = c[1]
                    y = c[2]
                    flip = c[3]
            if flip == 1:
                flip = "/1"
            else:
                flip = 0

            print("bulk: ", connector_loop, " conn_id: ", conn_id_loop, " pins: ", ids, " x: ", x, " y: ", y, " flip: ", flip, "\n")
            res = sym.PlacePins(ids, symname, ver, sheet_1_id, x, y, flip, 1.0)
            time.sleep(1)

            if res == 0:
                app.PutMessage(f"Step 3 not working. Result: {res}\n")
                raise Exception("Step 3 failed. Place failed.\n")
            else:
                app.PutMessage(f"Step 3 success. Pins Placed. Result: {res}\n")


    # step 4 - create the pin # view
    # Use the connector ID we already resolved and verified above.
    # Do not rescan all project devices here; if a later name lookup misses,
    # the old code can accidentally reuse the previous connector's device_id.
    device_id = conn_id
    dev.SetId(device_id)

    print(
        "CREATE VIEW TARGET:",
        connector,
        "DEVICE ID:", device_id,
        "NAME:", dev.GetName()
    )

    # E3 requires the view number to be unique within a connector.
    # Most connectors accept view 1, but some connector parts can already
    # have that view number occupied.  Try the next view number instead of
    # aborting the entire cable placement.
    res = 0
    used_view_number = None

    for candidate_view_number in range(1, 11):
        dev.SetId(device_id)
        res = dev.CreateView(device_id, candidate_view_number, 0, 0)

        print(
            "CREATE VIEW ATTEMPT:",
            connector,
            "DEVICE ID:", device_id,
            "VIEW NUMBER:", candidate_view_number,
            "RESULT:", res
        )

        if res != 0:
            used_view_number = candidate_view_number
            break

    if res == 0:
        app.PutMessage(f"Step 4 not working. Result: {res}\n")
        raise Exception(
            f"Step 4 failed. No new device view created for {connector} "
            f"(device ID {device_id}) using view numbers 1-10.\n"
        )
    else:
        app.PutMessage(
            f"Step 4 success. New device view placed. "
            f"Connector: {connector}, View number: {used_view_number}, Result: {res}"
        )

    new_device_view_id = res
    new_dev_ids.append(new_device_view_id)
    NEW_DEV_CONNECTORS[new_device_view_id] = connector
    print("new_device_view_id ", res)
    # The created schematic view normally exposes its view pins through
    # GetAllPinIds.  Fall back to GetPinIds if this E3 object exposes them
    # directly instead.
    newpinids = get_connector_pin_ids(
        dev,
        new_device_view_id,
        connector_name=f"{connector} VIEW",
        prefer_all=True
    )

    if not newpinids:
        raise Exception(
            f"No pins found in created schematic view for {connector} "
            f"(view ID {new_device_view_id})."
        )

    newpindict = {}
    for pin_id in newpinids:
        pin.SetId(pin_id)
        pin_name = str(pin.GetName()).strip()
        newpindict[pin_name] = pin_id

    print("SCHEMATIC VIEW PINS:", connector, newpindict)

    # PINS ARE GETTING ORDERED HERE
    p = connector_designation_from_device_name(connector)
    ordered_pin_ids = generate_ordered_pin_ids(newpindict, PINORDER[p])

    for c in PINN_LOCS:
        if str(c[0]) == p:
            xp = c[1]
            yp = c[2]
            pflip = c[3]
    if pflip == 1:
        pflip = "/1"
    else:
        pflip = 0

    symname = "MilSymbolReceptacle¶END_SPLIT"
    for key, value in ordered_pin_ids.items():
        pin_key = (str(p), str(key))
        if pin_key not in PIN_Y:
            app.PutMessage(f"WARNING: No aligned Y position found forConnector {p} Pin {key}")
            continue
        pin_y = PIN_Y[pin_key]
        res = sym.PlacePins(value, symname, ver,  sheet_1_id, xp, pin_y, pflip, 1.0)
        app.PutMessage(f"placing pin: {key} id: {value} symbol: {symname} y: {pin_y} x: {xp}, rot: {pflip} conn: {p}")
        symname = "MilSymbolReceptacle¶SPLIT_BOTTOM"
        time.sleep(0.5)

    connector_spares = SPARES.get(connector, {})
    if connector_spares:
        symname = "MilSymbolReceptacle¶END_SPLIT"
        app.PutMessage(f"placing {len(connector_spares)} spare pins for connector {connector}")
        for spare_pin_name in connector_spares.keys():
            # IMPORTANT: Use the pin ID from the NEW DEVICE VIEW, not the original connector pin ID.
            spare_pin_id = newpindict.get(str(spare_pin_name))
            if spare_pin_id is None:
                app.PutMessage(f"WARNING: Could not find spare pin {spare_pin_name} in new view for {connector}")
                continue
            res = sym.PlacePins(spare_pin_id, symname, ver, sheet_1_id, SPARE_X, SPARE_Y, 0, 1.0)
            app.PutMessage(f"placing spare pin: {spare_pin_name} id: {spare_pin_id} symbol: {symname} y: {SPARE_Y} x: {SPARE_X}, rot: {0} conn: {connector}")
            if res == 0:
                app.PutMessage(f"WARNING: Spare pin placement failed for {connector} pin {spare_pin_name} id {spare_pin_id}")
            SPARE_Y -= SPARE_PIN_SPACING
            symname = "MilSymbolReceptacle¶SPLIT_BOTTOM"
            time.sleep(0.55)
            app.PutMessage(f"Result: {res}")
        SPARE_Y -= SPARE_CONN_GAP



    dev.SetId(new_device_view_id)
    symbol_info = dev.GetSymbolIds(None)
    symbol_ids = [x for x in symbol_info[1] if x is not None]
    for id in symbol_ids:
        sym.SetId(id)
        texinfo = sym.GetTextIds(None)
        text_ids = [x for x in texinfo[1] if x is not None]
        for t_id in text_ids:
            text.SetId(t_id)
            if text.GetType() == 5:
                text.SetVisibility(0)
            if text.GetType() == 12:
                text.SetStyle(1) # bold
            

    dev.SetId(dev.GetAssemblyId())
    assydevinfo = dev.GetDeviceIds(None)
    assydevids = [x for x in assydevinfo[1] if x is not None]
    for assydev_id in assydevids:
        dev.SetId(assydev_id)
        if(dev.GetName() == connector): # if it is W1.<designation>
            assydevsymbolinfo = dev.GetSymbolIds(None)
            assydevsymbolids = [x for x in assydevsymbolinfo[1] if x is not None]
            for assydevsymbol_id in assydevsymbolids:
                sym.SetId(assydevsymbol_id)
                assydevsymboltextinfo = sym.GetTextIds(None)
                assydevsymboltextids = [x for x in assydevsymboltextinfo[1] if x is not None]
                for assydevsymboltext_id in assydevsymboltextids:
                    text.SetId(assydevsymboltext_id)
                    if text.GetType() == 5:
                        text.SetVisibility(0)
                    if text.GetType() == 12:
                        text.SetStyle(1)


    j += 1
    time.sleep(2.0)

del attr
del text
del sym
del pin
del comp
del dev
del prj
del app

# Existing equipment-identification / formatter checkpoint.
create_gui()


# Only require manual connection lines when V9 actually has something
# to place.  No symbol definitions = no extra window and no V9 run.
if HAS_SYMBOLS:
    if wait_for_connection_lines():
        print("")
        print("============================================================")

        print("============================================================")

        run_v9_symbol_overlay(output_path)
    else:
        print("Shield / twist placement cancelled by user.")
else:
    print("")
    print("No shield / twist definitions found.")
    print("Skipping connection-line GUI and symbol placement.")

print("Cable placer complete.")
