main.py:
# Block maker placement update, 2026-11-18:
# - Top-level connector origin at block edge, not inset 0.3
# - Secondary pin-view origin at block edge, right side, flip 0
# - One view pin ID per PlacePins call, passed as a one-element list
# - Choose secondary internal plug/receptacle symbol family from actual top
# - Keep E3's automatic MIL top-level symbol and its split characteristics
# - Text formatter in utils.py / test.py remains unchanged
# Run via existing launcher.py in a COPY of your E3 project.

from openpyxl import load_workbook
import time
import pyodbc
import win32com.client as win32
import tkinter as tk
import gc
import json
import os


# ================================================
# HELPER FXN
# ================================================
def ask_save_path(default_full_path=""):
    import os
    import tkinter as tk
    from tkinter import filedialog

    default_folder = os.path.dirname(default_full_path)

    # Create Tk root
    root = tk.Tk()
    root.withdraw()
    root.attributes("-topmost", True)
    root.update()

    try:
        # Let user PICK an existing Excel file
        selected_path = filedialog.askopenfilename(
            initialdir=default_folder,
            filetypes=[("Excel Files", "*.xlsm"), ("All Files", "*.*")]
        )
    except Exception:
        return None

    # Cancel or X
    if not selected_path:
        root.destroy()
        return None
    root.destroy()
    # Return ONLY the file name (not full path)
    return os.path.abspath(selected_path)

def generate_master_schematic_layout(PIN_SIGNAL_INFO,x_block,main_block_width):
    SHEET_DICT = {1:sheet_1_id}

    # PAGE BOUNDS
    TOP_OF_PAGE = 32.9
    BOTTOM_OF_PAGE = 0.0
    LEFT_OF_PAGE = 0.0
    RIGHT_OF_PAGE = 42.9

    # PAGE MARGINS
    TOP_MARGIN = 1.0
    BOTTOM_MARGIN = 1.0
    LEFT_MARGIN = 1.0
    RIGHT_MARGIN = 1.0

    # BLOCK SPACING
    BLOCK_X_SPACING = 1.0
    BLOCK_Y_SPACING = 1.0
    SCHEMATIC_BLOCK_WIDTH = 3.0
    PIN_SPACING = 0.2
    PIN_START_OFFSET = 2.25

    # USABLE PAGE LIMITS
    PAGE_TOP = TOP_OF_PAGE - TOP_MARGIN
    PAGE_BOTTOM = BOTTOM_OF_PAGE + BOTTOM_MARGIN
    PAGE_LEFT = LEFT_OF_PAGE + LEFT_MARGIN
    PAGE_RIGHT = RIGHT_OF_PAGE - RIGHT_MARGIN
    USABLE_PAGE_HEIGHT = PAGE_TOP - PAGE_BOTTOM

    # OUTPUT
    SPACING_DICT = []
    sheet = 1
    # On sheet 1, start to the RIGHT of the main block
    current_x = (x_block + main_block_width + BLOCK_X_SPACING)

    # Current Y is the TOP edge available for placement
    current_top_y = PAGE_TOP
    # Tracks widest block in current column
    current_column_width = 0.0

    # ================================================
    # BUILD LAYOUT
    # ================================================
    for i in range(len(PIN_SIGNAL_INFO)):
        connector_name = (PIN_SIGNAL_INFO[i][0]["connector name"])
        number_of_pins = (PIN_SIGNAL_INFO[i][0]["number of pins"])
        width = SCHEMATIC_BLOCK_WIDTH
        height = (PIN_START_OFFSET + (PIN_SPACING * number_of_pins))

        # --------------------------------------------
        # SAFETY CHECK:
        # Block cannot physically fit on ANY page
        # --------------------------------------------
        if height > USABLE_PAGE_HEIGHT:
            raise Exception(f"Schematic block for {connector_name} is too tall for a page. Height = {height}, usable page height = {USABLE_PAGE_HEIGHT}")

        # CALCULATE BOTTOM Y IF PLACED HERE
        candidate_y = current_top_y - height

        # DOES IT FIT VERTICALLY?
        if candidate_y < PAGE_BOTTOM:
            next_x = (current_x + current_column_width + BLOCK_X_SPACING)

            # DOES NEXT COLUMN FIT ON THIS PAGE?
            if next_x + width <= PAGE_RIGHT:
                current_x = next_x
                current_top_y = PAGE_TOP
                current_column_width = 0.0

            else:
                # PAGE IS FULL -> NEW PAGE
                sheet += 1
                sheet_symbol = "ANSI Size E - BAE"
                res = sht.Create(0, str(sheet), sheet_symbol, 0, 0)
                if res == 0:
                    app.PutMessage("failed to create new sheet")
                    raise Exception("failed to create new sheet")
                SHEET_DICT[sheet] = res
                current_x = PAGE_LEFT
                current_top_y = PAGE_TOP
                current_column_width = 0.0

            # Recalculate Y at new column/page
            candidate_y = current_top_y - height

        # CHECK HORIZONTAL FIT
        if current_x + width > PAGE_RIGHT:
            sheet += 1
            res = sht.Create(0, sheet, "DINA3", 0)
            if res == 0:
                app.PutMessage("failed to create new sheet")
                raise Exception("failed to create new sheet")
            SHEET_DICT[sheet] = res
            current_x = PAGE_LEFT
            current_top_y = PAGE_TOP
            current_column_width = 0.0
            candidate_y = current_top_y - height

        # SAVE PLACEMENT
        x_start = current_x
        y_start = candidate_y
        SPACING_DICT.append({"connector name": connector_name, "connector index": i, "sheet": sheet, "x start": x_start, "y start": y_start, "x end": x_start + width, "y end": y_start + height, "height": height, "width": width, "number of pins": number_of_pins})
        # UPDATE CURSOR FOR NEXT BLOCK
        current_top_y = (y_start - BLOCK_Y_SPACING)
        current_column_width = max(current_column_width, width)

    return SPACING_DICT, SHEET_DICT



def generate_schematic_pin_map(connector_info,newpindict,layout,pin_spacing=0.2,pin_start_offset=2.0):
    # Verified in the standalone E3 placement test: connector insertion origin
    # must sit ON the block boundary, not 0.3 units inside it.
    x=layout["x start"]+layout["width"]
    y_start=layout["y start"]+layout["height"]-pin_start_offset
    pin_map=[]
    for pos,pin_data in enumerate(connector_info[1:]):
        pin_name=str(pin_data[0]).strip()
        resolved_pin_name = pin_name

        # Normal case: the Excel pin name exactly matches the E3 view pin name.
        if resolved_pin_name not in newpindict:
            # Minimal fallback for library pins such as "1-C" / "2-S" when
            # Excel identifies those same pins as "1" / "2".  Only use the
            # fallback when there is exactly one unambiguous match.
            prefix_matches = [
                e3_pin_name
                for e3_pin_name in newpindict
                if str(e3_pin_name).strip().split("-", 1)[0] == pin_name
            ]

            if len(prefix_matches) == 1:
                resolved_pin_name = prefix_matches[0]
                print(
                    "PIN NAME FALLBACK:",
                    connector_info[0]['connector name'],
                    "EXCEL:", pin_name,
                    "E3:", resolved_pin_name
                )
            else:
                raise Exception(
                    f"Pin {pin_name} not found in new view for {connector_info[0]['connector name']}. "
                    f"Available E3 pins: {list(newpindict.keys())}"
                )

        pin_map.append({"pin name":pin_name,"pin id":newpindict[resolved_pin_name],"x":x,"y":y_start-(pos*pin_spacing),"flip":0})
    return pin_map

def normalize_connector_gender(value):
    """Read Excel column F; unrecognized/blank values use legacy behavior."""
    label = str(value).strip().upper() if value is not None else ""
    if label == "PLUG":
        return "PLUG"
    if label in ("RECEPTACLE", "RECEPTICLE"):
        return "RECEPTACLE"
    return None


def requested_mil_symbols_by_gender(gender):
    """Requested full/top symbol and secondary pin-view family.

    For an unknown gender, preserve the existing main.py behavior: request
    its previous top-level symbol and derive the secondary family from the
    actual E3 top-level symbol after PlacePins().
    """
    if gender == "PLUG":
        return "MilBlockPlugInt¶FULL", "MilBlockPlugInt"
    if gender == "RECEPTACLE":
        # This is the receptacle family already requested in the working
        # original main.py, so no new unverified library name is invented.
        return "MilBlockSymbolReceptacleInt¶FULL", "MilBlockSymbolReceptacleInt"
    return "MilBlockSymbolReceptacleInt¶FULL", None


def choose_secondary_mil_family(actual_top_symbol_name, connector_name):
    """Choose the secondary *internal block* family from E3's actual top view.

    E3 can substitute the requested library symbol during PlacePins.  Its
    GetSymbolTypeName(), NOT GetName(), reports what was really placed.  This
    intentionally does not try to force or swap the top-level symbol.
    """
    family = str(actual_top_symbol_name).split("¶", 1)[0]

    # Preserve the exact family prefix E3 selected; only request its Int
    # variant for the secondary pins. E.g. MilBlockPlug -> MilBlockPlugInt,
    # MilBlockSymbolReceptacle -> MilBlockSymbolReceptacleInt.
    if family.startswith("MilBlock") and (
        "Plug" in family or "Receptacle" in family
    ):
        return family if family.endswith("Int") else family + "Int"

    raise RuntimeError(
        f"Cannot identify an internal MIL block connector symbol family for "
        f"{connector_name}: E3 placed {actual_top_symbol_name!r}. "
        "No secondary pin symbols were placed using an unverified family."
    )


def format_top_connector_symbol(symbol_id, connector_name, text_size):
    """Show and format existing component/designation texts (no symbol swap)."""
    sym.SetId(symbol_id)
    textinfo = sym.GetTextIds(None)
    textids = [t for t in textinfo[1] if t is not None]
    for text_id in textids:
        text.SetId(text_id)
        try:
            kind = text.GetType()
            value = str(text.GetText()).strip()
        except Exception:
            continue

        # The tested MIL FULL symbol has type 5 (component code) and type 12
        # (device designation). Type 4 exists only on some library symbols.
        if kind in (4, 5, 12):
            text.SetVisibility(1)
            text.SetColour(0)
            text.SetMode(1)
            text.SetStyle(1)
            text.SetHeight(text_size)
        elif value == str(connector_name).strip():
            # Retain the original name-text styling, where such text exists.
            text.SetColour(0)
            text.SetMode(1)
            text.SetStyle(1)
            text.SetHeight(text_size)


def recreate_e3_objects():
    app = win32.gencache.EnsureDispatch('CT.Application')
    prj = app.CreateJobObject()
    text = prj.CreateTextObject()
    comp = prj.CreateComponentObject()
    dev = prj.CreateDeviceObject()
    sym = prj.CreateSymbolObject()
    pin = prj.CreatePinObject()
    attr = prj.CreateAttributeObject()
    sht = prj.CreateSheetObject()
    return app, prj, text, comp, dev, sym, pin, attr, sht

def display_main_block_attributes(device_view_id):
    dev.SetId(device_view_id)
    syminfo = dev.GetSymbolIds(None)
    symids = [x for x in syminfo[1] if x is not None]

    for sym_id in symids:
        sym.SetId(sym_id)

        res = sym.AddAttributeValue("Equipment Identifacation", ADDITIONAL_PART_ID)

        if res == -1:
            print("Equipment Identification already exists or failed on symbol:", sym_id)
            attrinfo = sym.GetAttributeIds(None)
            attrids = [x for x in attrinfo[1] if x is not None]

            for attr_id in attrids:
                attr.SetId(attr_id)

                if attr.GetName() == "Equipment Identifacation":
                    res = attr_id
                    break

        if res != -1:
            attr.SetId(res)
            display_result = attr.DisplayAttribute()
            print("DISPLAY ATTRIBUTE:", device_view_id, "SYMBOL:", sym_id, "ATTR:", res, "RESULT:", display_result)


def format_main_block_view(device_view_id, x, y, width, height, block_symbol_id=None):
    print("FORMATTING DEVICE VIEW:", device_view_id)

    x_center = x + (width / 2.0)

    # Split blocks share one logical device view.  Do NOT reformat every
    # symbol from that view for each connector's independent block layout.
    # The saved exact symbol ID ensures each split block keeps its own text.
    if block_symbol_id is not None:
        symids = [block_symbol_id]
    else:
        dev.SetId(device_view_id)
        syminfo = dev.GetSymbolIds(None)
        symids = [s for s in syminfo[1] if s is not None]

    for sym_id in symids:
        sym.SetId(sym_id)

        sym.SetBlockHatchColour(58)
        sym.SetBlockOutlineColour(0)

        #=========================================
        # NORMAL SYMBOL TEXT
        #=========================================
        textinfo = sym.GetTextIds(None)
        textids = [t for t in textinfo[1] if t is not None]

        for text_id in textids:
            text.SetId(text_id)
            txt = str(text.GetText()).strip()

            print("SYMBOL TEXT:", "VIEW:", device_view_id, "SYM:", sym_id, "TEXT ID:", text_id, "VALUE:", repr(txt))

            if txt == "BxRxUx":
                text.SetColour(0)
                text.SetMode(1)
                text.SetAlignment(2)
                text.SetStyle(1)
                text.SetHeight(TEXT_SIZE)
                text.SetSchemaLocation(x_center, y + height - 0.3)

            elif txt == str(DEVICE_DESIGNATION).strip() or str(DEVICE_DESIGNATION).strip() in txt:
                text.SetColour(0)
                text.SetMode(1)
                text.SetAlignment(2)
                text.SetStyle(1)
                text.SetHeight(TEXT_SIZE)
                text.SetSchemaLocation(x_center, y + height - 0.7)

                print("MOVED DEVICE DESIGNATION:", repr(txt), "TO:", x_center, y + height - 0.7)

            elif DEVICE_FUNCTION is not None and txt == str(DEVICE_FUNCTION).strip():
                text.SetVisibility(0)

        #=========================================
        # ATTRIBUTE TEXT
        #=========================================
        attrinfo = sym.GetAttributeIds(None)
        attrids = [a for a in attrinfo[1] if a is not None]

        for attr_id in attrids:
            attr.SetId(attr_id)
            attr_name = str(attr.GetName()).strip()

            print("ATTRIBUTE:", "VIEW:", device_view_id, "SYM:", sym_id, "ATTR ID:", attr_id, "NAME:", repr(attr_name))

            if attr_name == "Equipment Identifacation":
                attrtextinfo = attr.GetTextIds(None)
                attrtextids = [t for t in attrtextinfo[1] if t is not None]

                print("EQUIPMENT IDENTIFICATION TEXT IDS:", attrtextids)

                for text_id in attrtextids:
                    text.SetId(text_id)
                    txt = str(text.GetText()).strip()

                    print("ATTRIBUTE TEXT:", "ID:", text_id, "VALUE:", repr(txt))

                    text.SetVisibility(1)
                    text.SetColour(0)
                    text.SetMode(1)
                    text.SetAlignment(2)
                    text.SetStyle(1)
                    text.SetHeight(0.2)
                    text.SetSchemaLocation(x_center, y + height - 1.1)

                    print("MOVED EQUIPMENT IDENTIFICATION:", repr(txt), "TO:", x_center, y + height - 1.1)


# ================================================
# PARSE EXCEL
# ================================================
BLOCK_OFFSET = 5
output_path = ask_save_path()
wb = load_workbook(output_path, data_only=True)
ws = wb.active

ASSY_NAME = ws["C1"].value
VERSION = ws["C2"].value
ADDITIONAL_PART_ID = ws["C3"].value
DEVICE_DESIGNATION = ws["D3"].value
DEVICE_DESIGNATION = DEVICE_DESIGNATION.strip()
DEVICE_FUNCTION = ws["C4"].value
NUMBER_OF_CONNECTORS = ws["C5"].value

CONNECTORS = {}
CONNECTOR_GENDERS = {}
for row in ws.iter_rows(min_row=7, max_row=6 + int(NUMBER_OF_CONNECTORS)):
    connector_name = row[1].value  # B: connector designation (unchanged)
    connector_type = row[2].value  # C: component code (unchanged)
    gender_value = row[5].value    # F: newly inserted gender column
    if connector_name is not None and connector_type is not None:
        connector_name = str(connector_name).strip()
        CONNECTORS[connector_name] = connector_type
        CONNECTOR_GENDERS[connector_name] = normalize_connector_gender(gender_value)
        print("EXCEL CONNECTOR:", connector_name,
              "COMPONENT:", connector_type,
              "GENDER (F):", repr(gender_value),
              "NORMALIZED:", CONNECTOR_GENDERS[connector_name] or "UNKNOWN -> legacy symbols",
              flush=True)

# A column was inserted at F.  Previous G check cells are now H.
MODEL_CHECK = ws["H8"].value
TITLE_CHECK = ws["E2"].value  # Left of F: not shifted
CONNECTOR_MODEL_CHECK = ws["H2"].value
DUPLICATE_CHECK = ws["H4"].value
TEXT_SIZE = ws["D5"].value
if TEXT_SIZE is None or TEXT_SIZE < 0.15:
    TEXT_SIZE = 0.2
if MODEL_CHECK != "Pass":
    raise Exception("Model check is failing, fix it")
if TITLE_CHECK != "Pass":
    raise Exception("Title check is failing, fix it")
if CONNECTOR_MODEL_CHECK != "Pass":
    raise Exception("Connector model check is failing, fix it")
if DUPLICATE_CHECK != "Pass":
    raise Exception("Duplicate pin check is failing, fix it")

PIN_SIGNAL_INFO = []
for i in range(NUMBER_OF_CONNECTORS):
    # Original first pin was column I.  Inserting F shifts it to J.
    pin_col = 10 + (i * BLOCK_OFFSET)
    signal_col = pin_col + 1
    in_out_col = pin_col + 2
    number_of_pins = ws.cell(row=5, column=signal_col).value
    connector_name = ws.cell(row=6, column=signal_col).value
    if number_of_pins is None:
        number_of_pins = 0
    number_of_pins = int(number_of_pins)
    connector_type = CONNECTORS[connector_name]
    connector_gender = CONNECTOR_GENDERS.get(connector_name)
    temp = [{
        'connector name': connector_name,
        'connector type': connector_type,
        'connector gender': connector_gender,
        'number of pins': number_of_pins,
    }]
    end_row = 8 + number_of_pins - 1
    for r in range(8, end_row + 1):
        pin = ws.cell(row=r, column=pin_col).value
        signal = ws.cell(row=r, column=signal_col).value
        if signal is None:
            signal = "SPARE"
        in_out = ws.cell(row=r, column=in_out_col).value
        if in_out is None:
            in_out = "NONE"
        temp.append([pin, signal, in_out])
    PIN_SIGNAL_INFO.append(temp)

wb.close()

# front side green - 58
# back side green  - 74
sym.SetBlockHatchPattern(1, 45.0, 135.0)
sym.SetBlockHatchStyle(0)

res = sym.SetBlockOutlineColour(0)
if res != -1:
    app.PutMessage("failed to set block hatch outline color")
time.sleep(0.25)

# print('pin signal info: ', PIN_SIGNAL_INFO)
# print('\n connectors: ', CONNECTORS)
# print('n connector map: ', CONNECTOR_MAP)
CONNECTOR_DEVICE_IDS = {}
# Actual library families are recorded per connector and reused for its
# secondary view; this supports both plug and receptacle components.
SECONDARY_MIL_SYMBOL_BY_CONNECTOR = {}
#====================================================================================================================================
# LOAD AND PLACE CONNECTOR LOOP
#====================================================================================================================================
for i in range(len(PIN_SIGNAL_INFO)):
    # step 1 - get the component from the database
    conn_type = PIN_SIGNAL_INFO[i][0]['connector type']
    conn_name = PIN_SIGNAL_INFO[i][0]['connector name']
    ver = 1
    res = app.GetDatabaseTableSelectedComponents(conn_type, ver)
    if(res[0] != 0):
        app.PutMessage(f"step 1 did not work, Result: {res}\n")
        raise Exception(f"Step 1 failed. No device found in database with name: {conn_type}.\n")
    else:
        app.PutMessage(f"Step 1 success. Device found in database. Result: {res}")

    # step 2 - place the component from the library into the device tree
    # print(f'Placing connector name: {conn_name} type: {conn_type} into device tree\n')
    res = dev.Create(conn_name, "", "", conn_type, ver, 0)
    if(res == 0):
        app.PutMessage(f"step 2 did not work, Result: {res}\n")
        raise Exception("Step 2 failed. Device failed to be added to device tree.\n")
    else:
        app.PutMessage(f"Step 2 success. Device added to device tree. Result: {res}")


    # step 3 - get the device, and pin info from tree and order it
    devinfo = prj.GetAllDeviceIds(None)
    devcount = devinfo[0]
    devids   = devinfo[1]
    devids   = [x for x in devids if x is not None]
    dev_total = len(devids)

    CURRENT_CONNECTOR_ID = None
    for id in devids:
        dev.SetId(id)
        name = dev.GetName()
        if name == PIN_SIGNAL_INFO[i][0]['connector name']:
            CURRENT_CONNECTOR_ID = id
            break
    CONNECTOR_DEVICE_IDS[conn_name] = CURRENT_CONNECTOR_ID
    # assign signal name
    dev.SetId(CURRENT_CONNECTOR_ID)
    pininfo = dev.GetPinIds(None)
    pindids = [x for x in pininfo[1] if x is not None]
    PINDICT = []
    pins = []
    for pin_id in pindids:
        pin.SetId(pin_id)
        name = pin.GetName()
        pins.append(pin_id)
        current_pin_info = None
        for pin_data in PIN_SIGNAL_INFO[i][1:]:
            if str(pin_data[0]) == str(name):
                current_pin_info = pin_data
                break

        if current_pin_info is not None:
            signal = current_pin_info[1] if len(current_pin_info) > 1 else None
            in_out = current_pin_info[2] if len(current_pin_info) > 2 else None
        else:
            signal = None
            in_out = None

        if signal is not None:
            pin.SetAttributeValue("Local Signal Name", signal)

        if in_out is not None:
            pin.SetAttributeValue("Signal Types", in_out)
        attrinfo = pin.GetAttributeIds(None)
        attids = [x for x in attrinfo[1] if x is not None]
        for att_id in attids:
            attr.SetId(att_id)
            if attr.GetName() == "Local Signal Name" or attr.GetName() == "Signal Types":
                attr.DisplayAttribute()
        PINDICT.append(pin_id)

    # step 4 - create the pin # view
    devinfo = prj.GetAllDeviceIds(None)
    dev_ids = [x for x in devinfo[1] if x is not None]
    for id in dev_ids:
        dev.SetId(id)
        if dev.GetName() == PIN_SIGNAL_INFO[i][0]['connector name']:
            device_id = id
            break

    connector_gender = PIN_SIGNAL_INFO[i][0].get('connector gender')
    symname, gender_secondary_family = requested_mil_symbols_by_gender(
        connector_gender
    )

    connector_map_info = CONNECTOR_MAP[i]
    connector_position = connector_map_info['connector position']
    flip = connector_map_info['connector flip']

    # Preserve the original left/right connector grouping but attach the
    # insertion origin to the ACTUAL block edge (offset 0.0).
    # Verified in E3: RIGHT edge + flip=0. LEFT edge uses mirrored flip=/1.
    if flip == 0:
        x = x_block                     # LEFT block boundary
        y = y_start - (connector_position * PIN_VIEW_SPACING)
        flip = "/1"
    else:
        x = x_block + block_width       # RIGHT block boundary
        y = y_start - (connector_position * PIN_VIEW_SPACING)
        flip = 0

    app.PutMessage(f'placing {conn_name} at x = {x}, y = {y}, flip = {flip}')
    # A full pin-ID LIST is the E3-tested PlacePins argument type.
    # E3 is allowed to substitute the FULL top-level MIL symbol; we do NOT
    # replace that symbol, since the edge placement is what fixes mating.
    res = sym.PlacePins(PINDICT, symname, ver, sheet_1_id, x, y, flip, 1.0)
    top_symbol_id = res[0] if isinstance(res, (tuple, list)) else res
    if not isinstance(top_symbol_id, int) or top_symbol_id <= 0:
        raise RuntimeError(
            f"Top-level PlacePins failed for {conn_name}, sheet={sheet_1_id}, "
            f"x={x}, y={y}, flip={flip!r}: {res!r}"
        )

    sym.SetId(top_symbol_id)
    actual_top_symbol = str(sym.GetSymbolTypeName())
    # A recognized gender controls BOTH requested symbol families.
    # Unknown gender keeps the proven old behavior (derive secondary from
    # what E3 actually placed).  E3 can override top symbol selection;
    # report that override without attempting to swap symbols afterward.
    if gender_secondary_family is not None:
        SECONDARY_MIL_SYMBOL_BY_CONNECTOR[conn_name] = gender_secondary_family
    else:
        SECONDARY_MIL_SYMBOL_BY_CONNECTOR[conn_name] = choose_secondary_mil_family(
            actual_top_symbol, conn_name
        )

    print("TOP CONNECTOR:", conn_name,
          "EXCEL GENDER:", connector_gender or "UNKNOWN (legacy)",
          "REQUESTED:", symname,
          "ACTUAL:", actual_top_symbol,
          "SYMBOL ID:", top_symbol_id,
          "SECONDARY FAMILY:", SECONDARY_MIL_SYMBOL_BY_CONNECTOR[conn_name],
          flush=True)
    if connector_gender is not None and actual_top_symbol != symname:
        print("NOTICE: E3 selected a different top-level symbol for", conn_name,
              "(requested", repr(symname), "but got", repr(actual_top_symbol),
              "). Continuing with requested secondary gender family.", flush=True)
    format_top_connector_symbol(top_symbol_id, conn_name, TEXT_SIZE)
    time.sleep(0.5)

#=========================================
# LOAD AND PLACE SCHEMATIC VIEWS
#=========================================
MAIN_DEVICE_ID = None
devinfo = prj.GetAllDeviceIds(None)
devids = [x for x in devinfo[1] if x is not None]
for dev_id in devids:
    dev.SetId(dev_id)
    name = dev.GetName()
    if name == DEVICE_DESIGNATION:
        MAIN_DEVICE_ID = dev_id
        break

if MAIN_DEVICE_ID is None:
    raise Exception(f"Could not find main device {DEVICE_DESIGNATION}")

MASTER_SCHEMATIC_LAYOUT, SHEET_DICT = generate_master_schematic_layout(PIN_SIGNAL_INFO, x_block, block_width)

SCHEMATIC_DATA = []

#=========================================
# PHASE 1 - CREATE ONE SECONDARY VIEW,
# THEN PLACE ITS BLOCK AS SPLIT SYMBOLS.
#
# All connector blocks belong to this SAME logical main-device view.
# Each call to PlaceBlock reuses that main-device view ID. Whether E3
# treats the resulting symbols as true split blocks must be verified in E3.
# Each connector gets a new device view from its original parent connector.
#=========================================
SHARED_MAIN_VIEW_NUMBER = 1

dev.SetId(MAIN_DEVICE_ID)
SHARED_MAIN_VIEW_ID = dev.CreateView(MAIN_DEVICE_ID, SHARED_MAIN_VIEW_NUMBER, 0, "0")
if SHARED_MAIN_VIEW_ID == 0:
    raise Exception(f"Failed creating shared main schematic view {SHARED_MAIN_VIEW_NUMBER}")

print("SHARED MAIN DEVICE VIEW:", "SOURCE:", MAIN_DEVICE_ID,
      "VIEW:", SHARED_MAIN_VIEW_NUMBER, "ID:", SHARED_MAIN_VIEW_ID)

placed_split_block_ids = set()

for i in range(len(PIN_SIGNAL_INFO)):
    conn_name = PIN_SIGNAL_INFO[i][0]["connector name"]
    conn_layout_dict = MASTER_SCHEMATIC_LAYOUT[i]
    current_sheet_id = SHEET_DICT[conn_layout_dict["sheet"]]

    xp = conn_layout_dict["x start"]
    yp = conn_layout_dict["y start"]
    xwidth = conn_layout_dict["width"]
    yheight = conn_layout_dict["height"]

    # All split block symbols share main-device view 1.  Connector view
    # numbers should be aligned with that main view.  Each connector is a
    # distinct logical device, so each may independently have view 1.
    view_number = SHARED_MAIN_VIEW_NUMBER
    parent_block = SHARED_MAIN_VIEW_ID
    print("SPLIT BLOCK:", conn_name, "SHARED MAIN VIEW:", parent_block,
          "SPLIT INDEX:", i + 1)

    # Fresh symbol object ONLY for this block
    block_sym = prj.CreateSymbolObject()
    is_loaded = block_sym.Load("CABBLOCK", 1)

    if not is_loaded:
        raise Exception(f"Failed loading CABBLOCK for {conn_name}")

    actual_yp = yp
    block_symbol_id = block_sym.PlaceBlock(current_sheet_id, xp, actual_yp, xwidth, yheight, parent_block)

    if block_symbol_id == 0:
        for retry_offset in range(1, 5):
            actual_yp = yp - retry_offset

            block_symbol_id = block_sym.PlaceBlock(
                current_sheet_id,
                xp,
                actual_yp,
                xwidth,
                yheight,
                parent_block
            )

            if block_symbol_id != 0:
                break

        if block_symbol_id == 0:
            raise Exception(
                f"Failed placing split block for {conn_name} in shared view "
                f"{SHARED_MAIN_VIEW_ID}. E3 did not accept another PlaceBlock "
                "placement for the same main-device view."
            )

    # Guard against a COM result that returns an existing symbol instead of
    # generating a new split representation. Never format the wrong block.
    if block_symbol_id in placed_split_block_ids:
        raise Exception(
            f"E3 reused symbol ID {block_symbol_id} for {conn_name} "
            "instead of creating a distinct split block symbol."
        )
    placed_split_block_ids.add(block_symbol_id)

    # If this block had to move down, move every still-unplaced block below it
    # in the SAME sheet/column by the same amount.  This keeps the original
    # spacing intact instead of letting a retry move on top of the next block.
    placement_shift = yp - actual_yp
    if placement_shift > 0:
        for future_layout in MASTER_SCHEMATIC_LAYOUT[i + 1:]:
            if (
                future_layout["sheet"] == conn_layout_dict["sheet"]
                and abs(future_layout["x start"] - conn_layout_dict["x start"]) < 0.000001
            ):
                future_layout["y start"] -= placement_shift
                future_layout["y end"] -= placement_shift

        print(
            "SHIFTED REMAINING BLOCKS IN COLUMN:",
            conn_name,
            "SHIFT:", placement_shift
        )

    # Keep the layout synchronized with where the block was ACTUALLY placed.
    # The later pin map and text formatter both use this layout Y.
    conn_layout_dict["y start"] = actual_yp
    conn_layout_dict["y end"] = actual_yp + yheight

    # =========================================
    # SET COLOR
    # =========================================
    block_sym.SetId(block_symbol_id)
    block_sym.SetBlockHatchPattern(1, 45.0, 135.0)
    block_sym.SetBlockHatchStyle(0)
    block_sym.SetBlockHatchWidth(0.25)
    block_sym.SetBlockHatchDistance(1.0)
    block_sym.SetBlockHatchColour(58)

    print("BLOCK:", conn_name, "DEVICE VIEW:", parent_block, "SYMBOL:", block_symbol_id)

    # =========================================
    # ADD EQUIPMENT IDENTIFACATION DIRECTLY TO
    # THE EXACT SECONDARY BLOCK SYMBOL WE JUST PLACED
    # =========================================
    block_sym.SetId(block_symbol_id)

    equipment_attr_id = block_sym.AddAttributeValue("Equipment Identifacation", ADDITIONAL_PART_ID)

    if equipment_attr_id == -1:
        print("ADD EQUIPMENT IDENTIFACATION RETURNED -1:", "CONNECTOR:", conn_name, "VIEW:", parent_block, "SYMBOL:", block_symbol_id)
    else:
        print("ADDED EQUIPMENT IDENTIFACATION:", "CONNECTOR:", conn_name, "VIEW:", parent_block, "SYMBOL:", block_symbol_id, "ATTR ID:", equipment_attr_id, "VALUE:", repr(ADDITIONAL_PART_ID))

    # =========================================
    # VERIFY IT IS ACTUALLY ON THIS SYMBOL
    # DO NOT DISPLAY OR FORMAT IT
    # =========================================
    verify_attr_info = block_sym.GetAttributeIds(None)
    verify_attr_ids = [x for x in verify_attr_info[1] if x is not None]

    equipment_attr_found = False

    for verify_attr_id in verify_attr_ids:
        attr.SetId(verify_attr_id)

        try:
            verify_attr_name = str(attr.GetName()).strip()
        except Exception:
            continue

        if verify_attr_name == "Equipment Identifacation":
            equipment_attr_found = True
            print("VERIFIED EQUIPMENT IDENTIFACATION ON BLOCK SYMBOL:", "CONNECTOR:", conn_name, "VIEW:", parent_block, "SYMBOL:", block_symbol_id, "ATTR ID:", verify_attr_id)
            break

    if not equipment_attr_found:
        print("WARNING - EQUIPMENT IDENTIFACATION NOT FOUND AFTER ADD:", "CONNECTOR:", conn_name, "VIEW:", parent_block, "SYMBOL:", block_symbol_id)

    # IMPORTANT - release this symbol object NOW
    del block_sym
    time.sleep(0.25)

    connector_device_id = CONNECTOR_DEVICE_IDS[conn_name]

    # Create a NEW DEVICE VIEW OF THE ORIGINAL/PARENT CONNECTOR.
    # The shared CABBLOCK main device still has only ONE view; each
    # connector has its own separate view number 1 underneath it.
    # Zuken requires that block connector views have the same view number
    # as their block, so do not increment this view number per pin.
    dev.SetId(connector_device_id)
    connector_view_number = SHARED_MAIN_VIEW_NUMBER
    connector_view_id = dev.CreateView(
        connector_device_id,
        connector_view_number,
        parent_block
    )
    if not connector_view_id:
        raise Exception(
            f"Failed creating new view from parent connector {conn_name} "
            f"(source ID {connector_device_id}, view number "
            f"{connector_view_number}, parent block {parent_block})"
        )

    print("CREATED VIEW OF PARENT CONNECTOR:",
          "CONNECTOR:", conn_name,
          "ORIGINAL ID:", connector_device_id,
          "NEW VIEW ID:", connector_view_id,
          "VIEW NUMBER:", connector_view_number,
          "SHARED BLOCK:", parent_block, flush=True)

    SCHEMATIC_DATA.append({
        "index": i,
        "name": conn_name,
        "parent_block": parent_block,
        "block_symbol_id": block_symbol_id,
        "connector_device_id": connector_device_id,
        "connector_view": connector_view_id,
        "sheet_id": current_sheet_id,
        "layout": conn_layout_dict
    })

    time.sleep(0.5)

#=========================================
# SAVE EXACT SECONDARY BLOCK SYMBOL IDS
# FOR THE FRESH-PROCESS FORMATTER
#=========================================
secondary_block_file = os.environ.get("E3_SECONDARY_BLOCK_FILE")

if not secondary_block_file:
    raise RuntimeError("E3_SECONDARY_BLOCK_FILE was not provided by launcher.py")

secondary_block_data = []

for data in SCHEMATIC_DATA:
    secondary_block_data.append({
        "name": data["name"],
        "parent_block": data["parent_block"],
        "block_symbol_id": data["block_symbol_id"],
        "sheet_id": data["sheet_id"],
        "x": data["layout"]["x start"],
        "y": data["layout"]["y start"],
        "width": data["layout"]["width"],
        "height": data["layout"]["height"],
        "text_size": TEXT_SIZE
    })

with open(secondary_block_file, "w", encoding="utf-8") as secondary_block_handle:
    json.dump(secondary_block_data, secondary_block_handle, indent=2)

print("SAVED SECONDARY BLOCK METADATA:", secondary_block_file)
print("SECONDARY BLOCK COUNT:", len(secondary_block_data))

#=========================================
# COMPLETELY RELEASE E3 OBJECTS
#=========================================
del attr
del text
del sym
del pin
del comp
del dev
del sht
del prj
del app

time.sleep(1.5)

#=========================================
# RECREATE CLEAN E3 OBJECTS
#=========================================
app = win32.gencache.EnsureDispatch("CT.Application")
prj = app.CreateJobObject()
text = prj.CreateTextObject()
comp = prj.CreateComponentObject()
dev = prj.CreateDeviceObject()
sym = prj.CreateSymbolObject()
pin = prj.CreatePinObject()
attr = prj.CreateAttributeObject()
sht = prj.CreateSheetObject()

#=========================================
# PHASE 2 - PLACE ALL PINS
# RESET E3 OBJECTS FOR EVERY CONNECTOR
#=========================================
for data in SCHEMATIC_DATA:
    del attr
    del text
    del sym
    del pin
    del comp
    del dev
    del sht
    del prj
    del app

    time.sleep(1.0)

    app = win32.gencache.EnsureDispatch("CT.Application")
    prj = app.CreateJobObject()
    text = prj.CreateTextObject()
    comp = prj.CreateComponentObject()
    dev = prj.CreateDeviceObject()
    sym = prj.CreateSymbolObject()
    pin = prj.CreatePinObject()
    attr = prj.CreateAttributeObject()
    sht = prj.CreateSheetObject()

    i = data["index"]
    conn_name = data["name"]
    parent_connector_id = data["connector_device_id"]
    connector_view_id = data["connector_view"]
    current_sheet_id = data["sheet_id"]
    conn_layout_dict = data["layout"]

    print("=========================================")
    print("STARTING CONNECTOR:", conn_name, flush=True)
    print("PARENT CONNECTOR:", parent_connector_id,
          "NEW CONNECTOR VIEW:", connector_view_id,
          "BLOCK SYMBOL:", data["block_symbol_id"],
          "SHARED MAIN VIEW:", data["parent_block"], flush=True)
    print("SHEET:", current_sheet_id, flush=True)
    print("=========================================")

    # IMPORTANT: resolve PIN IDs FROM THE NEW CONNECTOR VIEW, not from
    # the logical/original connector. This is the actual E3 view hierarchy:
    #   original connector -> connector device view -> separate pin symbols.
    dev.SetId(connector_view_id)
    view_pin_result = dev.GetAllPinIds(None)
    view_pin_ids = [pid for pid in view_pin_result[1] if pid is not None]
    view_pin_map = {}
    for view_pin_id in view_pin_ids:
        pin.SetId(view_pin_id)
        view_pin_name = str(pin.GetName()).strip()
        if view_pin_name in view_pin_map:
            raise Exception(f"Duplicate pin name {view_pin_name!r} in new connector view {connector_view_id}")
        view_pin_map[view_pin_name] = view_pin_id

    print("PARENT CONNECTOR VIEW PINS:", conn_name,
          "VIEW:", connector_view_id, "PINS:", view_pin_map, flush=True)
    SCHEMATIC_PIN_MAP = generate_schematic_pin_map(
        PIN_SIGNAL_INFO[i], view_pin_map, conn_layout_dict
    )
    data["placed_pin_symbols"] = []
    placed_pin_symbol_ids = set()

    # Same internal BLOCK symbol family used by the successful standalone
    # tests. Derive plug vs. receptacle from the actual top-level symbol.
    secondary_family = SECONDARY_MIL_SYMBOL_BY_CONNECTOR[conn_name]
    for pin_index, pin_data in enumerate(SCHEMATIC_PIN_MAP, start=1):
        # The tests requested END_TOP on pin 1; E3 sometimes automatically
        # resolves it as END_SPLIT. Either result is logged, not force-swapped.
        characteristic = "END_TOP" if pin_index == 1 else "SPLIT_BOTTOM"
        symname = f"{secondary_family}¶{characteristic}"
        print("PLACING PIN FROM PARENT CONNECTOR VIEW:",
              "CONNECTOR:", conn_name,
              "PIN:", pin_data["pin name"],
              "VIEW ID:", connector_view_id,
              "VIEW PIN ID:", pin_data["pin id"],
              "X:", pin_data["x"],
              "Y:", pin_data["y"], flush=True)

        # CRITICAL: ONE view pin per PlacePins call, so each pin gets its
        # own placed symbol, rather than one combined pin-group symbol.
        res = sym.PlacePins(
            [pin_data["pin id"]], symname, 1, current_sheet_id,
            pin_data["x"], pin_data["y"], pin_data["flip"], 1.0
        )
        print("PLACEPINS RESULT:", repr(res), flush=True)
        placed_sym_id = res[0] if isinstance(res, (tuple, list)) else res
        if not placed_sym_id:
            raise Exception(
                f"Cannot place {conn_name} pin {pin_data['pin name']} "
                f"from connector view {connector_view_id}, "
                f"pin ID {pin_data['pin id']}, "
                f"block {data['block_symbol_id']}, sheet {current_sheet_id}; "
                f"E3 response {res!r}"
            )
        if placed_sym_id in placed_pin_symbol_ids:
            raise Exception(
                f"E3 reused pin symbol {placed_sym_id} for {conn_name} "
                f"pin {pin_data['pin name']}; expected a separate symbol"
            )
        placed_pin_symbol_ids.add(placed_sym_id)
        sym.SetId(placed_sym_id)
        actual_pin_symbol = str(sym.GetSymbolTypeName())
        print("SECONDARY PIN:", conn_name, pin_data["pin name"],
              "REQUESTED:", symname, "ACTUAL:", actual_pin_symbol,
              "SYMBOL ID:", placed_sym_id, flush=True)
        data["placed_pin_symbols"].append({
            "symbol_id": placed_sym_id,
            "pin_id": pin_data["pin id"],
            "pin_name": pin_data["pin name"],
            "position": pin_index,
            "connector_view_id": connector_view_id,
            "parent_connector_id": parent_connector_id,
            "requested_symbol": symname,
            "actual_symbol": actual_pin_symbol,
        })
        app.PutMessage(
            f"Placed {conn_name} pin {pin_data['pin name']} "
            f"x={pin_data['x']} y={pin_data['y']}"
        )
        time.sleep(0.25)

    print("VALIDATED DISTINCT PIN SYMBOL IDS:", conn_name,
          len(placed_pin_symbol_ids), flush=True)
    print("FINISHED CONNECTOR:", conn_name)
    time.sleep(0.5)

#=========================================
# UPDATE METADATA WITH INDEPENDENT PIN SYMBOL IDS AND THEIR PARENT VIEW.
# The formatter can access each symbol by its exact placed symbol ID.
#=========================================
for saved_block_data, data in zip(secondary_block_data, SCHEMATIC_DATA):
    saved_block_data["placed_pin_symbols"] = data.get("placed_pin_symbols", [])

with open(secondary_block_file, "w", encoding="utf-8") as secondary_block_handle:
    json.dump(secondary_block_data, secondary_block_handle, indent=2)

print("SAVED CONNECTOR-VIEW PIN SYMBOL METADATA:", secondary_block_file, flush=True)

#=========================================
# RESET BEFORE MAIN BLOCK FORMATTING
#=========================================
del attr
del text
del sym
del pin
del comp
del dev
del sht
del prj
del app

time.sleep(1.0)

app = win32.gencache.EnsureDispatch("CT.Application")
prj = app.CreateJobObject()
text = prj.CreateTextObject()
comp = prj.CreateComponentObject()
dev = prj.CreateDeviceObject()
sym = prj.CreateSymbolObject()
pin = prj.CreatePinObject()
attr = prj.CreateAttributeObject()
sht = prj.CreateSheetObject()

#=========================================
# FORMAT SECONDARY MAIN BLOCK TEXT
#=========================================
for data in SCHEMATIC_DATA:
    layout = data["layout"]
    format_main_block_view(data["parent_block"], layout["x start"], layout["y start"], layout["width"], layout["height"], block_symbol_id=data["block_symbol_id"])

#=========================================
# FINAL SECONDARY PIN COMPONENT-CODE VISIBILITY
#=========================================
# E3's default MIL pin symbols often display the component code (text type 5)
# on each individual secondary pin.  Work from the exact symbol IDs saved
# during PlacePins instead of relying on pin.GetViewIds(), which can miss
# these separately placed pin-view symbols.  Do this AFTER all pins have been
# placed so subsequent placements cannot reset the visibility.
print("\nHIDING COMPONENT CODE ON INDIVIDUAL SECONDARY PINS", flush=True)
hidden_component_codes = 0
visibility_warnings = []

for data in SCHEMATIC_DATA:
    for placed_pin in data.get("placed_pin_symbols", []):
        placed_symbol_id = int(placed_pin["symbol_id"])
        pin_name = str(placed_pin["pin_name"])
        try:
            sym.SetId(placed_symbol_id)
            if not sym.GetSymbolTypeName():
                visibility_warnings.append(
                    f"{data['name']} pin {pin_name}: symbol {placed_symbol_id} no longer resolves"
                )
                continue

            text_result = sym.GetTextIds(None)
            text_ids = [tid for tid in text_result[1] if isinstance(tid, int) and tid > 0]

            for text_id in text_ids:
                text.SetId(text_id)
                if text.GetType() != 5:  # Component code
                    continue
                text.SetVisibility(0)
                if text.GetVisibility() == 0:
                    hidden_component_codes += 1
                    print("COMPONENT CODE OFF:", data["name"], "PIN:", pin_name,
                          "SYMBOL:", placed_symbol_id, "TEXT:", text_id, flush=True)
                else:
                    visibility_warnings.append(
                        f"{data['name']} pin {pin_name}: type-5 text {text_id} is still visible"
                    )
        except Exception as exc:
            visibility_warnings.append(
                f"{data['name']} pin {pin_name}, symbol {placed_symbol_id}: {exc!r}"
            )

print("SECONDARY COMPONENT CODES HIDDEN:", hidden_component_codes, flush=True)
for warning in visibility_warnings:
    print("WARNING - SECONDARY COMPONENT CODE:", warning, flush=True)

#=========================================
# MAIN CREATION PHASE COMPLETE
#
# IMPORTANT:
# main.py does NOT launch test.py and does NOT import utils.
# launcher.py waits for this entire Python process to exit,
# then launcher.py starts test.py in a fresh Python process.
#=========================================
print("")
print("============================================================")
print("MAIN E3 CREATION COMPLETE")
print("MAIN.PY WILL NOW END")
print("============================================================")

# Close the SQL handles if possible.
# Do not run the final text formatter here.
try:
    cur.close()
except Exception:
    pass

try:
    conn.close()
except Exception:
    pass


launcher.py:
"""E3 Block Maker launcher with descriptive, copyable error popups.

Run `python launcher.py` (or the packaged launcher executable).
Runs main.py and test.py in separate processes, as in the original design.
"""

import os
import sys
import tempfile
import subprocess
import traceback
from collections import deque
from datetime import datetime
from pathlib import Path


ROLE_ENV = "BLOCK_MAKER_ROLE"
METADATA_ENV = "E3_SECONDARY_BLOCK_FILE"


def get_application_directory():
    if getattr(sys, "frozen", False):
        return Path(sys.executable).resolve().parent
    return Path(__file__).resolve().parent


def get_child_command():
    if getattr(sys, "frozen", False):
        return [sys.executable]
    return [sys.executable, str(Path(__file__).resolve())]


def run_builder_child():
    import main  # Exceptions must propagate so subprocess returns a failure code.


def run_formatter_child():
    import test  # Exceptions must propagate so subprocess returns a failure code.


class StageFailed(Exception):
    def __init__(self, stage, return_code, output):
        self.stage = stage
        self.return_code = return_code
        self.output = output
        super().__init__(f"{stage} exited with code {return_code}")


def emit(line, log_handle):
    """Stream to both console and persistent log, without waiting for the child."""
    log_handle.write(line)
    log_handle.flush()
    if sys.stdout is not None:
        try:
            sys.stdout.write(line)
            sys.stdout.flush()
        except (OSError, ValueError):
            pass


def run_stage(stage, command, environment, cwd, log_handle):
    heading = f"\n{'=' * 60}\nSTARTING {stage.upper()}\n{'=' * 60}\n"
    emit(heading, log_handle)

    # Keep a tail in memory, but persist the entire output to the log file.
    recent_lines = deque(maxlen=400)
    process = subprocess.Popen(
        command,
        cwd=str(cwd),
        env=environment,
        stdout=subprocess.PIPE,
        stderr=subprocess.STDOUT,
        text=True,
        encoding="utf-8",
        errors="replace",
        bufsize=1,
    )

    assert process.stdout is not None
    with process.stdout:
        for line in process.stdout:
            emit(line, log_handle)
            recent_lines.append(line)

    exit_code = process.wait()
    if exit_code != 0:
        raise StageFailed(stage, exit_code, "".join(recent_lines))

    emit(f"\n{stage.upper()} COMPLETE\n", log_handle)


def main():
    # Children should never create their own popups. One parent-level popup
    # handles exceptions from both stages, including those in utils.py.
    role = os.environ.get(ROLE_ENV, "").strip().lower()
    if role == "main":
        run_builder_child()
        return 0
    if role == "formatter":
        run_formatter_child()
        return 0

    from error_popup import show_error_dialog

    app_dir = get_application_directory()
    command = get_child_command()
    log_dir = Path(tempfile.gettempdir()) / "E3_Block_Maker_Logs"
    log_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    log_path = log_dir / f"block_maker_{datetime.now():%Y%m%d_%H%M%S_%f}.log"

    stage = "Launcher"
    try:
        with log_path.open("w", encoding="utf-8", errors="replace") as log:
            emit(f"Block Maker log: {log_path}\n", log)
            emit(f"Application directory: {app_dir}\n", log)
            with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory(prefix="e3_block_maker_") as temp_dir:
                metadata_path = str(Path(temp_dir) / "secondary_blocks.json")
                emit(f"Metadata path: {metadata_path}\n", log)

                # Each child receives its own environment. The original
                # builder->formatter sequencing is preserved.
                builder_env = os.environ.copy()
                builder_env[ROLE_ENV] = "main"
                builder_env[METADATA_ENV] = metadata_path
                builder_env["PYTHONUNBUFFERED"] = "1"
                stage = "Main Builder"
                run_stage(stage, command, builder_env, app_dir, log)

                if not Path(metadata_path).is_file():
                    raise RuntimeError(
                        "Main builder exited successfully but did not write "
                        "the E3_SECONDARY_BLOCK_FILE metadata JSON."
                    )

                formatter_env = os.environ.copy()
                formatter_env[ROLE_ENV] = "formatter"
                formatter_env[METADATA_ENV] = metadata_path
                formatter_env["PYTHONUNBUFFERED"] = "1"
                stage = "Formatter"
                run_stage(stage, command, formatter_env, app_dir, log)

            emit("\nBLOCK MAKER COMPLETE — BOTH STAGES SUCCEEDED\n", log)
        return 0

    except StageFailed as exc:
        # No hidden success state: a failed child stops the next stage.
        show_error_dialog(exc.stage, exc.output, str(log_path), exc.return_code)
        return exc.return_code if exc.return_code > 0 else 1

    except Exception:
        details = traceback.format_exc()
        try:
            with log_path.open("a", encoding="utf-8") as log:
                emit(f"\nLAUNCHER/STAGE FAILURE\n{details}", log)
        except OSError:
            sys.stderr.write(details)
        show_error_dialog(stage, details, str(log_path), 1)
        return 1


if __name__ == "__main__":
    sys.exit(main())

def wait_for_equipment_identification():
    root = tk.Tk()
    root.title("Equipment Identification")
    root.attributes("-topmost", True)
    root.resizable(False, False)

    window_width = 620
    window_height = 260

    screen_width = root.winfo_screenwidth()
    screen_height = root.winfo_screenheight()

    x_position = int((screen_width - window_width) / 2)
    y_position = int((screen_height - window_height) / 2)

    root.geometry(f"{window_width}x{window_height}+{x_position}+{y_position}")

    message = "Turn ON all Equipment Identifications from symbol properties for all SCH blocks.\n\nOnce every Equipment Identification is visible,\ncome back here and click DONE.\n\nThe formatter will then run again automatically."

    label = tk.Label(root, text=message, font=("Arial", 12), justify="center")
    label.pack(pady=32)

    done_button = tk.Button(root, text="DONE - RUN FORMATTER AGAIN", width=34, height=2, command=root.destroy)
    done_button.pack(pady=8)

    root.lift()
    root.focus_force()
    root.mainloop()
