            signal_text_alignment = 3
        log(f"FORMAT PIN {staged_record['pin_name']} | SIDE {side} | ROW {staged_record['final_row']} | SYMBOL {identity['symbol_id']} | SIGNAL ALIGNMENT {signal_text_alignment} | DEVICE DESIGNATION {'ON' if is_top_of_side else 'OFF'}")
        apply_text_tweaks(prj, identity["symbol_id"], side, is_top_of_side)
        staged_record["final_resolved_symbol_id"] = identity["symbol_id"]
# AUTO-DETECT SOURCE EDGE + FAST VERIFIED E3 PIN LOOKUPS
# All current pins on opposite edge from Excel final side => ONE PASS:
# transfer each pin directly to its final coordinate, top-of-stack first.
# Current pins on same edge as Excel final side => preserve proven TWO-PASS
# staging to unused edge then final. Never attempt center placement.
# Existing C4/J2 selection and safety checks are unchanged.
# Previously, EACH staged -> final pin required scanning and reading symbols
# throughout the entire E3 project. We now resolve a pin by its already-verified
# stable pin-view ID or the most recently returned placed symbol ID, validate
# exact name/designation/coordinates, and scan only when those identities fail.
# COM MOVE TIMING lines show whether Zuken itself is spending time on Delete/
# PlacePinView, which cannot be accelerated through Python-only lookup changes.
# DRY RUN DEFAULT IS UNCHANGED. No COM worker/process termination.
# No center placement probe. A GUI launcher uses TWO normally exiting Python
# processes to release COM before the user changes E3 display settings.
# No worker killing or COM Surrogate management. C4/J2 are optional; only use the unused block edge, after full
# preflight validation of every connector. On placement failure, STOP and
# recover from a project copy: the live COM API has no proven atomic rollback.

# ================================================
# PIN ORDERING SETTINGS
# ================================================

# SAFETY FIRST: first run only prints exactly what would move. It does NOT
# call Symbol.Delete() or Symbol.PlacePinView(). After reviewing this plan and
# manually proving the opposite-edge placement works in a SAVED COPY, set
# EDGE_STAGING_DRY_RUN = False to run the actual pin ordering.
EDGE_STAGING_DRY_RUN = False

# ORDER-ONLY MODE (default per the current workflow): when the existing E3
# stack ALREADY has the exact Excel top-to-bottom pin order and spacing, leave
# it alone even if it is on the opposite block edge from Excel's pin column.
# This avoids redoing an already ordered J1A stack on the RIGHT edge.
# Set False to require that finished pins be on the Excel-specified LEFT/RIGHT
# edge: an ordered-right J1A with Excel LEFT will then transfer RIGHT -> LEFT.
SKIP_IF_ALREADY_ORDERED_ON_EITHER_EDGE = True

# C4 (device designation) and J2 (sheet/page) are read from Excel below.
# Leave either blank to search without that restriction.

# Fallback ONLY when no live pins exist. Normal runs measure the real
# E3 symbol-origin offset from the existing connector, which can be 0.00.
PIN_EDGE_INSET = 0.30

STAGING_LANE_OFFSET = 0.35
STAGING_EDGE_CLEARANCE = 0.15
STAGING_TOP_BOTTOM_CLEARANCE = 0.20

LEFT_FLIP = "0"
RIGHT_FLIP = "/1"

BLOCK_BOTTOM_CLEARANCE = 0.30

DETACHED_SYMBOL_NAME = "MilSymbolReceptacle¶END_SPLIT"
DETACHED_SYMBOL_VERSION = "1"
MAINTAIN_TEXT_SIZE = 1

SYMBOL_SCAN_COORDINATE_TOLERANCE = 0.06

COM_MISSING = -2147352572



# ==============================================================
# TWO-PHASE SYMBOL FORMATTING / COM-FREE LAUNCHER
# ==============================================================
# Run this file normally. A normal Python child first runs the proven pin-order
# logic and exits, relinquishing all E3 COM references. The parent process has
# never connected to E3, and displays the manual property-change window.
# Clicking Run Symbol Formatter starts a fresh Python child to reconnect.
# This is NOT a forced process exit and does not kill dllhost.exe or Zuken.
#
# Direct --order-worker / --format-worker modes are internal to the launcher.

FORMATTER_REQUIRED_PROPERTY = "Equipment Identification"


def create_formatter_manifest(work_records, sheet_names, excel_path):
    """Capture EXPECTED finished pins after ordering, including SKIP connectors.

    Do not store transient symbol IDs: E3 may rebuild them while creating
    split-block connector stacks. The later formatter must rediscover them.
    """
    connectors = []
    for work in work_records:
        block = work['block_geometry']
        by_name = {str(p['pin_name']): p for p in work['sorted_pin_records']}
        if len(by_name) != len(work['sorted_pin_records']):
            raise RuntimeError(f"{work['connector_name']}: duplicate E3 pin name in formatter manifest")
        if work['skip_reorder']:
            # Order-only skip may be on the OPPOSITE Excel edge. Format at the
            # REAL E3 positions, not hypothetical Excel-side coordinates.
            actual = sorted(work['sorted_pin_records'], key=lambda p: -float(p['y']))
            expected_pins = [{
                'pin_name': str(p['pin_name']),
                'side': work['source_edge'], 'row': i,
                'x': float(p['x']), 'y': float(p['y']),
                'pin_view_id': int(p['pin_view_id']),
            } for i, p in enumerate(actual)]
        else:
            expected_pins = [{
                'pin_name': str(item['pin_name']),
                'side': item['side'], 'row': int(item['row']),
                'x': float(item['x']), 'y': float(item['y']),
                'pin_view_id': int(by_name[str(item['pin_name'])]['pin_view_id']),
            } for item in work['final_plan']]
        connectors.append({
            'connector_name': work['connector_name'],
            'main_device_id': int(work['main_device_id']),
            'sheet_id': int(block['sheet_id']),
            'sheet_name': sheet_names.get(int(block['sheet_id']), ''),
            'block_symbol_id': int(block['block_symbol_id']),
            'block_view_number': int(block['connector_view_number']),
            'block_bounds': [float(block[k]) for k in (
                'block_left', 'block_right', 'block_bottom', 'block_top')],
            'pins': expected_pins,
            'was_skipped': bool(work['skip_reorder']),
        })
    if not connectors:
        raise RuntimeError('No connector records exist to format.')
    return {'version': 1, 'excel_path': excel_path, 'connectors': connectors}


def write_formatter_manifest(path, data):
    """Atomically publish the manifest only after ordering succeeds."""
    directory = os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(path))
    os.makedirs(directory, exist_ok=True)
    temp = path + '.writing'
    with open(temp, 'w', encoding='utf-8') as stream:
        json.dump(data, stream, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2)
    os.replace(temp, path)


def _formatter_in_expected_block(loc, connector, tol=0.075):
    if not loc or int(loc['sheet_id']) != int(connector['sheet_id']):
        return False
    left, right, bottom, top = connector['block_bounds']
    return (left - tol <= float(loc['x']) <= right + tol and
            bottom - tol <= float(loc['y']) <= top + tol)


def _formatter_sheet_symbols(prj, sht, sheet_id, connectors):
    """Scan a SHEET once (not the entire project for EVERY pin)."""
    # In some E3 COM versions Sheet.SetId returns None even when successful.
    # Check the selected ID where that API is available; don't mistake None
    # for a failed sheet lookup.
    sht.SetId(int(sheet_id))
    selected_id = call_noarg(sht, 'GetId')
    if selected_id is not None and int(selected_id) != int(sheet_id):
        raise RuntimeError(f'Formatter selected sheet {selected_id}, expected {sheet_id}')
    ids = call_id_array(sht.GetSymbolIds)
    if not ids:
        raise RuntimeError(f'No sheet-local symbols returned on sheet {sheet_id}; no formatting done')
    log(f'FORMAT LOOKUP: sheet {sheet_id} has {len(ids)} symbol IDs; one scan for all connector pins')
    candidates = []
    probe = prj.CreateSymbolObject()
    for sym_id in ids:
        try:
            if not probe.SetId(int(sym_id)):
                continue
            type_name = str(probe.GetSymbolTypeName() or '')
            # Ignore the thousands of unrelated schematic symbols. The
            # observed working family was MilBlockSymbolReceptacleIInt.
            if 'Mil' not in type_name or '¶' not in type_name:
                continue
            location = get_symbol_schema_location(probe)
            if not any(_formatter_in_expected_block(location, c)
                       for c in connectors):
                continue
            identity = read_symbol_identity(prj, int(sym_id))
            if identity and identity['pin_number'] not in (None, ''):
                candidates.append(identity)
        except Exception:
            # Some COM symbols disappear as split blocks regenerate; leave
            # resolution to the complete-inventory preflight below.
            continue
    log(f'FORMAT LOOKUP: {len(candidates)} connector pin candidates inside the requested block bounds')
    return candidates


def resolve_formatter_targets(prj, sht, payload):
    """READ-ONLY: all pins must match name, designation, block and location.

    Resolve all targets BEFORE making any text edits. A mismatch or an
    ambiguous candidate never results in formatting an arbitrary symbol.
    """
    connectors = payload['connectors']
    expected_count = sum(len(c['pins']) for c in connectors)
    if expected_count == 0:
        raise RuntimeError('Formatter manifest contains no pins')
    by_sheet = {}
    for c in connectors:
        by_sheet.setdefault(int(c['sheet_id']), []).append(c)
        block = prj.CreateSymbolObject()
        if not block.SetId(int(c['block_symbol_id'])):
            raise RuntimeError(f"{c['connector_name']}: saved CABBLOCK no longer exists; no formatting done")
        block_type = str(block.GetSymbolTypeName() or '')
        block_loc = get_symbol_schema_location(block)
        if 'CABBLOCK' not in block_type.upper() or not block_loc or int(block_loc['sheet_id']) != int(c['sheet_id']):
            raise RuntimeError(f"{c['connector_name']}: CABBLOCK identity/page changed; no formatting done")
        area = get_symbol_placed_area(block)
        if area is None:
            raise RuntimeError(f"{c['connector_name']}: CABBLOCK bounds unavailable; no formatting done")
        actual = [float(area[k]) for k in ('left', 'right', 'bottom', 'top')]
        if any(abs(x - y) > 0.075 for x, y in zip(actual, c['block_bounds'])):
            raise RuntimeError(f"{c['connector_name']}: CABBLOCK moved after ordering; no formatting done")
    sheet_pool = {}
    for sid, cs in by_sheet.items():
        sheet_pool[sid] = _formatter_sheet_symbols(prj, sht, sid, cs)
    resolved = []
    used = set()
    for c in connectors:
        connector = c['connector_name']
        for target in c['pins']:
            matches = []
            for cand in sheet_pool[int(c['sheet_id'])]:
                loc = cand.get('location')
                if (cand.get('pin_number') == target['pin_name']
                    and match_connector_designation(cand.get('device_text'), connector)
                    and _formatter_in_expected_block(loc, c)
                    and coordinates_close(loc, target['x'], target['y'])):
                    matches.append(cand)
            if len(matches) != 1:
                raise RuntimeError(
                    f"FORMAT PREFLIGHT FAILED {connector} PIN {target['pin_name']} "
                    f"at ({target['x']:.4f},{target['y']:.4f}) on sheet {c['sheet_name']!r}: "
                    f"found {len(matches)} matching symbols (need exactly one). "
                    'Check the E3 property and that the pins/blocks were not moved. '
                    'NO formatting changes made.'
                )
            symbol_id = int(matches[0]['symbol_id'])
            if symbol_id in used:
                raise RuntimeError(f'Formatter symbol {symbol_id} assigned twice; no formatting done')
            used.add(symbol_id)
            resolved.append((c, target, symbol_id))
    log(f'FORMAT PREFLIGHT PASS: uniquely matched all {len(resolved)} / {expected_count} pins')
    return resolved


def format_worker_run(manifest_path):
    """Fresh COM session to apply ORIGINAL text tweaks after manual E3 setting."""
    with open(manifest_path, encoding='utf-8') as stream:
        payload = json.load(stream)
    if payload.get('version') != 1:
        raise RuntimeError('Unknown formatter manifest version')
    app_fmt = prj_fmt = sht_fmt = None
    try:
        app_fmt = win32.gencache.EnsureDispatch('CT.Application')
        prj_fmt = app_fmt.CreateJobObject()
        sht_fmt = prj_fmt.CreateSheetObject()
        section('SECOND PHASE - CONNECT TO E3 SYMBOL FORMATTER')
        log('Please ensure the required Equipment Identification property is enabled in Zuken.')
        targets = resolve_formatter_targets(prj_fmt, sht_fmt, payload)
        section('APPLY ORIGINAL PIN TEXT FORMATTING')
        for connector, target, symbol_id in targets:
            side = target['side']
            top = int(target['row']) == 0
            log(f"FORMAT {connector['connector_name']} PIN {target['pin_name']} "
                f"| {side} ROW {target['row']} | SYMBOL {symbol_id} "
                f"| EQUIPMENT IDENTIFICATION {'ON' if top else 'OFF'}")
            # The proven original formatting routine: text types 5,12,617,203;
            # signal alignment, visibility, font, colour and hatch treatment.
            apply_text_tweaks(prj_fmt, symbol_id, side, top)
        section('SYMBOL FORMATTING COMPLETE')
        log(f'Formatted {len(targets)} pins in {len(payload["connectors"])} connector(s).')
    finally:
        sht_fmt = None
        prj_fmt = None
        app_fmt = None
        gc.collect()
        log('Formatter E3 COM proxies released.')


def build_worker_command(mode, manifest_path):
    """Start an isolated worker from .py or a PyInstaller --onefile exe.

    In frozen builds sys.executable IS the bundled .exe; adding __file__
    would pass the bundled script as an unexpected command-line argument,
    preventing both --order-worker and --format-worker from ever starting.
    """
    if mode not in ('--order-worker', '--format-worker'):
        raise ValueError(f'Unsupported worker mode: {mode!r}')
    manifest_path = os.path.abspath(manifest_path)
    if getattr(sys, 'frozen', False):
        # PyInstaller -F: re-launch our own executable, NOT main.py.
        return [sys.executable, mode, manifest_path]
    # Normal Python: run the same .py file as a child interpreter.
    return [sys.executable, os.path.abspath(__file__), mode, manifest_path]


def run_worker_process(mode, manifest_path):
    command = build_worker_command(mode, manifest_path)
    log(f'WORKER LAUNCH: mode={mode} | frozen={bool(getattr(sys, "frozen", False))}')
    log(f'WORKER COMMAND: {command!r}')
    # No hard exit or COM Surrogate termination. The child exits normally.
    result = subprocess.run(command, check=False)
    log(f'WORKER EXIT: mode={mode} | exit_code={result.returncode}')
    return result


def gui_two_phase_launcher():
    """This PARENT never connects to E3, so E3 can accept manual settings."""
    import tkinter as tk
    from tkinter import messagebox
    session_dir = tempfile.mkdtemp(prefix='zuken_pin_order_')
    manifest = os.path.join(session_dir, 'formatter_manifest.json')
    section('PHASE 1: ORDER PINS (SEPARATE COM SESSION)')
    proc = run_worker_process('--order-worker', manifest)
    if proc.returncode != 0:
        messagebox.showerror('Zuken Pin Ordering',
                             f'Pin ordering worker exited with code {proc.returncode}.\n'
                             'Formatter has NOT been started.\n\n'
                             'Run the executable from Command Prompt to see the '
                             'actual worker error.')
        return 1
    if not os.path.isfile(manifest):
        messagebox.showinfo('Zuken Pin Ordering - Dry Run',
                            'Pin ordering finished without changes (dry run). '                            'Set EDGE_STAGING_DRY_RUN = False to order and format.')
        return 0
    with open(manifest, encoding='utf-8') as stream:
        connectors = json.load(stream)['connectors']
    targets = ', '.join(c['connector_name'] for c in connectors)
    root = tk.Tk()
    root.title('Zuken - Final Symbol Formatter')
    root.geometry('580x300')
    root.resizable(False, False)
    root.attributes('-topmost', True)
    tk.Label(root, text='PIN ORDERING COMPLETED', font=('Segoe UI', 14, 'bold')).pack(pady=(22, 10))
    tk.Label(root, text=f'Connectors: {targets}', wraplength=520).pack(pady=(0, 15))
    instructions = (
        'The pin-ordering Python COM process has exited normally.\n\n'
        'In Zuken, enable the required display property:\n'
        f'  {FORMATTER_REQUIRED_PROPERTY}\n'
        'Save or refresh the active drawing if needed.\n\n'
        'Then return here and click RUN SYMBOL FORMATTER.'
    )
    tk.Label(root, text=instructions, justify='left', wraplength=530).pack()
    buttons = tk.Frame(root)
    buttons.pack(pady=15)

    def run_formatter():
        run_button.config(state='disabled')
        root.update_idletasks()
        result = run_worker_process('--format-worker', manifest)
        if result.returncode == 0:
            messagebox.showinfo('Zuken Symbol Formatter',
                                'Symbol formatting completed. See terminal for details.', parent=root)
            root.destroy()
        else:
            messagebox.showerror('Zuken Symbol Formatter',
                                 'Symbol formatting failed. No pin movement was retried. '                                 'Review terminal output, adjust E3 settings, and click '                                 'Run Symbol Formatter again.', parent=root)
            run_button.config(state='normal')

    run_button = tk.Button(buttons, text='RUN SYMBOL FORMATTER', width=25,
                           height=2, command=run_formatter)
    run_button.pack(side='left', padx=8)
    tk.Button(buttons, text='CLOSE (NO FORMATTING)', width=22,
              height=2, command=root.destroy).pack(side='left', padx=8)
    root.mainloop()
    return 0


# BEFORE parsing Excel or attaching to E3, route each normal run through a
# COM-free launcher. Worker mode bypasses this block and runs one phase only.
if __name__ == '__main__':
    if len(sys.argv) == 1:
        sys.exit(gui_two_phase_launcher())
    if len(sys.argv) >= 3 and sys.argv[1] == '--format-worker':
        try:
            format_worker_run(sys.argv[2])
        except Exception as exc:
            import traceback
            traceback.print_exc()
            sys.exit(1)
        sys.exit(0)
    if not (len(sys.argv) >= 3 and sys.argv[1] == '--order-worker'):
        raise SystemExit('Usage: python script.py  (launch normal 2-phase workflow)')

# ================================================
# PARSE EXCEL
# ================================================

BLOCK_OFFSET = 4

output_path = ask_save_path()

if output_path is None:
    raise Exception("No Excel file selected")

wb = load_workbook(output_path, data_only=True)
ws = wb.active

# Optional restrictions: blank cells behave like unrestricted project search.
REQUESTED_DEVICE_DESIGNATION = normalize_optional_text(ws["C4"].value)
REQUESTED_PAGE = normalize_optional_text(ws["J2"].value)
# Optional center-to-center vertical pin spacing. None means original behavior.
REQUESTED_VERTICAL_SPACING = parse_optional_vertical_spacing(ws["J1"].value)

NUMBER_OF_CONNECTORS = ws["C5"].value

if NUMBER_OF_CONNECTORS is None:
    raise Exception("Number of connectors is blank")

NUMBER_OF_CONNECTORS = int(NUMBER_OF_CONNECTORS)

# The old model check has been removed. B2 now contains duplicate-pin check.
DUPLICATE_PIN_CHECK = ws["B2"].value

if str(DUPLICATE_PIN_CHECK).strip().casefold() != "pass":
    raise Exception("Duplicate pin check (B2) is failing; fix duplicate pins in Excel")

CONNECTOR_PIN_INFO = []

for i in range(NUMBER_OF_CONNECTORS):

    left_pin_col = 9 + (i * BLOCK_OFFSET)
    right_pin_col = left_pin_col + 1

    declared_pin_rows = ws.cell(row=5, column=right_pin_col).value
    connector_name = ws.cell(row=6, column=right_pin_col).value

    # The header defines how many pin rows to READ, not how many must be
    # filled in. A connector with 50 physical pin rows can list only 8 pins.
    if declared_pin_rows is None:
        declared_pin_rows = 0
    declared_pin_rows = int(declared_pin_rows)
    if declared_pin_rows < 0:
        raise ValueError(f'Connector {i + 1}: pin row count cannot be negative')
    connector_name = str(connector_name).strip() if connector_name is not None else ''

    temp = [{
        "connector name": connector_name,
        "number of pins": 0,  # populated with the ACTUAL provided Excel pin count
        "declared pin rows": declared_pin_rows,
        "left pins": [],
        "right pins": [],
    }]

    end_row = 8 + declared_pin_rows - 1

    left = []
    right = []

    for r in range(8, end_row + 1):

        left_pin = normalize_pin_value(ws.cell(row=r, column=left_pin_col).value)
        right_pin = normalize_pin_value(ws.cell(row=r, column=right_pin_col).value)

        if left_pin not in (None, ""):
            left.append(left_pin)

        if right_pin not in (None, ""):
            right.append(right_pin)

    temp[0]["left pins"].extend(left)
    temp[0]["right pins"].extend(right)

    provided_pin_count = len(left) + len(right)
    temp[0]['number of pins'] = provided_pin_count
    log(f'Excel connector {connector_name!r}: {provided_pin_count} provided pin(s) '
        f'within {declared_pin_rows} scanned pin row(s).')

    # Connectors with no requested pins need no changes. They can be omitted
    # without triggering a false model-completeness requirement.
    if provided_pin_count == 0:
        log(f'Excel connector {connector_name!r}: zero requested pins; skipping.')
        continue
    if not connector_name:
        raise RuntimeError(f'Connector {i + 1}: Excel connector name is blank.')

    CONNECTOR_PIN_INFO.append(temp)

wb.close()

if not CONNECTOR_PIN_INFO:
    raise RuntimeError('No pins were specified in the Excel sheet; nothing to arrange.')

print(CONNECTOR_PIN_INFO)


# ================================================
# CONNECT TO DB
# ================================================

# set objects
#====================================================================================================================================

try:
    app = win32.gencache.EnsureDispatch("CT.Application")
    prj = app.CreateJobObject()
    text = prj.CreateTextObject()
    comp = prj.CreateComponentObject()
    dev = prj.CreateDeviceObject()
    sym = prj.CreateSymbolObject()
    pin = prj.CreatePinObject()
    attr = prj.CreateAttributeObject()

    #====================================================================================================================================
    # more setup
    #====================================================================================================================================

    app.PutMessage("\n Starting Block Pin Ordering Script")

    sht = prj.CreateSheetObject()
    SHEETS = get_sheet_metadata(prj, sht)
    SHEET_NAME_BY_ID = {sheet["sheet_id"]: sheet["name"] for sheet in SHEETS}
    REQUESTED_SHEET_ID = resolve_optional_sheet_id(SHEETS, REQUESTED_PAGE)

    ALL_DEVICE_IDS = get_device_ids(prj)
    if REQUESTED_DEVICE_DESIGNATION is not None:
        MAIN_DEVICE_IDS = find_device_ids(prj, dev, REQUESTED_DEVICE_DESIGNATION, ALL_DEVICE_IDS)
        if not MAIN_DEVICE_IDS:
            raise RuntimeError(
                f"C4 device designation {REQUESTED_DEVICE_DESIGNATION!r} was not found in E3."
            )
    else:
        # Without C4, find all placed CABBLOCKs and let the connector's
        # view number + actual pin locations narrow the correct main device.
        MAIN_DEVICE_IDS = ALL_DEVICE_IDS

    section("OPTIONAL PROJECT TARGET FILTERS")
    log(f"C4 device designation: {REQUESTED_DEVICE_DESIGNATION or '<blank - automatic>'}")
    log(f"J2 sheet/page: {REQUESTED_PAGE or '<blank - any sheet>'}")
    log(f"J1 vertical pin spacing: {REQUESTED_VERTICAL_SPACING if REQUESTED_VERTICAL_SPACING is not None else '<blank - automatic original pitch>'}")
    if REQUESTED_SHEET_ID is not None:
        log(f"Resolved sheet: {SHEET_NAME_BY_ID[REQUESTED_SHEET_ID]!r} (E3 ID {REQUESTED_SHEET_ID})")

    # This read-only scan is completed before pin movement begins.
    BLOCK_CANDIDATES = collect_candidate_cabblocks(
        prj, dev, MAIN_DEVICE_IDS, REQUESTED_SHEET_ID
    )
    if not BLOCK_CANDIDATES:
        raise RuntimeError(
            "No placed CABBLOCKs match the optional C4/J2 restrictions. "
            "Check that the device and sheet identify the intended cable drawing."
        )
    log(f"Placed CABBLOCK candidates found: {len(BLOCK_CANDIDATES)}")

    # Diagnostic data is printed unconditionally whether C4/J2 are blank or filled.
    print_candidate_block_details(
        prj, dev, sht, BLOCK_CANDIDATES, SHEET_NAME_BY_ID,
        [group[0]["connector name"] for group in CONNECTOR_PIN_INFO],
    )

    # The detached MilSymbolReceptacle symbol is an optional backup. Do not
    # block MilBlockSymbolReceptacle projects if that old family cannot load:
    # choose_compatible_pin_symbol() validates the actual per-connector family.
    try:
        verify_detached_symbol_load(prj)
    except RuntimeError as exc:
        log(f'LEGACY SYMBOL OPTIONAL: {exc}; will try placed pin symbol family.')


    # generate pin location maps, get block dimensions and find the block centers

    CONNECTOR_WORK_INFO = []

    section("GENERATE PIN MAPS AND BLOCK GEOMETRY")

    for connector_group in CONNECTOR_PIN_INFO:

        connector_excel_record = connector_group[0]
        connector_name = connector_excel_record["connector name"]

        # Match the printed pin-symbol connector designation, e.g. J1A#1.
        # E3 dev.GetName() may be PNL2 (the parent) or A2J1A (a different device).
        selected = select_labeled_block(
            prj, sht, connector_excel_record, BLOCK_CANDIDATES, SHEET_NAME_BY_ID
        )
        main_device_id = selected['main_device_id']
        block_geometry = selected['block_geometry']
        bindings = locate_logical_pin_bindings(prj, dev, pin, selected, ALL_DEVICE_IDS)
        initial_snapshot = snapshot_selected_pin_bindings(prj, bindings, selected)
        sorted_pin_records = get_sorted_live_pin_records(initial_snapshot)
        connector_device_id = main_device_id  # informational only; pin IDs are per-view

        validate_connector_snapshot_location(connector_name, sorted_pin_records, block_geometry)
        validate_excel_against_e3(connector_excel_record, sorted_pin_records)
        complete_partial_pin_order(connector_excel_record, sorted_pin_records)

        pin_geometry = derive_pin_geometry(
            block_geometry, sorted_pin_records, connector_excel_record,
            vertical_spacing=REQUESTED_VERTICAL_SPACING)
        final_plan = build_final_plan(connector_excel_record, pin_geometry)
        global_final_sequence = build_global_final_sequence(final_plan)
        decision = decide_edge_ordering_mode(
            connector_name, sorted_pin_records, final_plan,
            connector_excel_record, pin_geometry,
            skip_ordered_either_edge=SKIP_IF_ALREADY_ORDERED_ON_EITHER_EDGE)
        existing_edge = decision['source_edge']
        final_edge = decision['final_edge']
        staging_mode = decision['mode']
        already_matches = staging_mode == 'SKIP'
        skip_reason = decision['skip_reason']

        if decision['order_match'] and not decision['exact_match']:
            log(f'{connector_name}: Excel pin order and vertical positions MATCH, '
                f'but source edge {existing_edge} differs from requested '
                f'Excel edge {final_edge}.')
        if staging_mode == 'SKIP':
            log(f'{connector_name}: SKIP REASON: {skip_reason}.')
        if staging_mode == 'TWO_PASS':
            staging_map = build_edge_staging_map(
                global_final_sequence, pin_geometry,
                connector_excel_record, sorted_pin_records)
            edge_staging_name = next(iter(staging_map.values()))['lane']
        else:
            staging_map = {}
            edge_staging_name = None
        # When the source is already on the opposite edge we MUST peel from
        # the ORIGINAL top downward. Do not peel in an arbitrary Excel order.
        if staging_mode == 'DIRECT':
            global_final_sequence = order_final_moves_by_existing_stack(
                final_plan, sorted_pin_records)

        placement_symbol_name = (
            choose_compatible_pin_symbol(prj, sorted_pin_records, connector_name)
            if not already_matches else None
        )
        log(f'{connector_name}: SOURCE={existing_edge}, FINAL={final_edge}, '
            f'MODE={staging_mode}; '
            + ('zero pin moves' if staging_mode == 'SKIP' else
               'one direct pass (NO staging pass)' if staging_mode == 'DIRECT' else
               'stage to opposite edge then return (two passes)'))

        work_record = {
            "placement_symbol_name": placement_symbol_name,
            "connector_excel_record": connector_excel_record,
            "connector_name": connector_name,
            "connector_device_id": connector_device_id,
            "main_device_id": main_device_id,
            "initial_snapshot": initial_snapshot,
            "sorted_pin_records": sorted_pin_records,
            "block_geometry": block_geometry,
            "pin_geometry": pin_geometry,
            "final_plan": final_plan,
            "global_final_sequence": global_final_sequence,
            "staging_map": staging_map,
            "edge_staging_name": edge_staging_name,
            "source_edge": existing_edge,
            "final_edge": final_edge,
            "staging_mode": staging_mode,
            "staging_method": None,
            "staged_records": [],
            "skip_reorder": already_matches,
            "skip_reason": skip_reason,
        }

        # Detect duplicate input groups resolving to the SAME physical connector/block
        # before any modification begins.
        for previous in CONNECTOR_WORK_INFO:
            if previous["block_geometry"]["block_symbol_id"] == block_geometry["block_symbol_id"]:
                raise RuntimeError(
                    f"Excel connectors {previous['connector_name']!r} and {connector_name!r} "
                    "resolve to the same E3 block; aborting before pin changes."
                )
        CONNECTOR_WORK_INFO.append(work_record)

        log()
        log(f"CONNECTOR: {connector_name}")
        log(f"OWNING MAIN DEVICE ID: {connector_device_id}")
        log(f"MATCHED E3 CONNECTOR LABELS: {selected['labels']}")
        log(f"EXCEL REQUESTED PINS: {connector_excel_record['number of pins']} | "
            f"AUTO-INCLUDED PINS: {len(connector_excel_record['auto-added pins'])} | "
            f"TOTAL E3 PINS: {connector_excel_record['total E3 pins']}")
        log(f"SELECTED MAIN DEVICE ID: {main_device_id}")
        log(f"SELECTED SHEET: {SHEET_NAME_BY_ID[block_geometry['sheet_id']]!r} (E3 ID {block_geometry['sheet_id']})")
        log(f"CONNECTOR VIEW NUMBER: {block_geometry['connector_view_number']}")
        log(f"MAIN VIEW ID: {block_geometry['main_view_id']}")
        log(f"BLOCK SYMBOL ID: {block_geometry['block_symbol_id']}")
        log(f"BLOCK LEFT: {block_geometry['block_left']}")
        log(f"BLOCK RIGHT: {block_geometry['block_right']}")
        log(f"BLOCK BOTTOM: {block_geometry['block_bottom']}")
        log(f"BLOCK TOP: {block_geometry['block_top']}")
        log(f"BLOCK WIDTH: {block_geometry['block_width']}")
        log(f"BLOCK HEIGHT: {block_geometry['block_height']}")
        log(f"BLOCK CENTER X: {block_geometry['block_center_x']}")
        log(f"BLOCK CENTER Y: {block_geometry['block_center_y']}")
        log(f"LEFT PIN X: {pin_geometry['left_x']}")
        log(f"RIGHT PIN X: {pin_geometry['right_x']}")
        log(f"PIN PITCH: {pin_geometry['pin_pitch']}")
        if pin_geometry['custom_vertical_spacing']:
            log(f"MEASURED SOURCE PIN PITCH (STAGING): {pin_geometry['measured_pin_pitch']}")
            log(f"J1 OVERRIDE (FINAL VERTICAL PIN PITCH): {pin_geometry['pin_pitch']}")
        log(f"FINAL TOP Y: {pin_geometry['final_top_y']}")

        if already_matches:
            log("ORDER CHECK:")
            log(f"    {skip_reason}.")
            if existing_edge != final_edge:
                log(f"    NOTE: pins remain on {existing_edge}; Excel column requests {final_edge}.")
                log("    Set SKIP_IF_ALREADY_ORDERED_ON_EITHER_EDGE = False "
                    "to force movement onto the Excel edge.")
            log("    THIS CONNECTOR WILL BE SKIPPED (ZERO PIN MOVES).")
        else:
            log("GLOBAL FINAL PLACEMENT SEQUENCE:")
            if staging_mode == 'DIRECT':
                log('DIRECT TRANSFER: move existing edge top-to-bottom to the '
                    'opposite Excel edge; no separate staging pass.')
                for item in global_final_sequence:
                    log(f"    STEP {item['sequence_index'] + 1} -> PIN {item['pin_name']} "
                        f"-> FINAL {item['side']} ROW {item['row']} "
                        f"at ({item['x']}, {item['y']})")
            elif staging_mode == 'TWO_PASS':
                log('TWO-PASS: first stage on the unused block edge, then place in Excel order.')
                for item in global_final_sequence:
                    stage = staging_map[item["pin_name"]]
                    log(f"    STEP {item['sequence_index'] + 1} -> PIN {item['pin_name']} "
                        f"-> TEMP {stage['lane']} ROW {stage['row']} "
                        f"at ({stage['x']}, {stage['y']}); "
                        f"FINAL {item['side']} ROW {item['row']} "
                        f"at ({item['x']}, {item['y']})")
            else:
                raise RuntimeError(f'Unexpected staging mode {staging_mode!r}')


    # Every connector is selected and preflighted BEFORE this checkpoint.
    # Default behavior is read-only; no COM write call is made in dry-run mode.
    section("SAFE EDGE STAGING PREVIEW")
    for work_record in CONNECTOR_WORK_INFO:
        if work_record["skip_reorder"]:
            log(f"{work_record['connector_name']}: SKIPPED — {work_record['skip_reason']}.")
        else:
            log(f"{work_record['connector_name']}: block {work_record['block_geometry']['block_symbol_id']} "
                f"on sheet {SHEET_NAME_BY_ID[work_record['block_geometry']['sheet_id']]}; "
                f"{work_record['source_edge']} -> {work_record['final_edge']}; "
                f"mode={work_record['staging_mode']}; "
                f"{len(work_record['sorted_pin_records'])} pins.")

    if EDGE_STAGING_DRY_RUN:
        log("DRY RUN IS ON: No pins were deleted, moved, or formatted.")
        log("Only after you manually confirm one pin can be moved to the unused "
            "block edge in a disposable E3 project copy should you set "
            "EDGE_STAGING_DRY_RUN = False in this script.")
    else:
        section("STAGE PINS ON UNUSED BLOCK EDGE (NO CENTER PROBE)")
        for work_record in CONNECTOR_WORK_INFO:
            if work_record["skip_reorder"]:
                log(f"SKIPPING {work_record['connector_name']}: already ordered")
                continue
            if work_record['staging_mode'] == 'DIRECT':
                log(f"DIRECT TRANSFER {work_record['connector_name']}: "
                    'already on the opposite edge; bypassing all staging moves.')
                work_record['staged_records'] = existing_edge_as_stage_records(
                    work_record['sorted_pin_records'], work_record['connector_name'],
                    work_record['placement_symbol_name'])
                work_record['staging_method'] = 'DIRECT'
            else:
                log(f"EDGE STAGING {work_record['connector_name']} ")
                work_record['staged_records'] = peel_all_pins_to_staging(
                    prj, dev, pin, sht, work_record['connector_device_id'],
                    work_record['connector_name'], work_record['block_geometry'],
                    work_record['sorted_pin_records'], work_record['staging_map'],
                    work_record['pin_geometry'], work_record['placement_symbol_name'])
                work_record['staging_method'] = 'EDGE'


    # based on pin maps and block geom generated -> start populating pins based on excel parsed data

    if not EDGE_STAGING_DRY_RUN:
        section("POPULATE PINS FROM EXCEL ORDER")

        for work_record in CONNECTOR_WORK_INFO:

            log()

            if work_record["skip_reorder"]:
                log(f"SKIPPING ORDERING {work_record['connector_name']} - CURRENT ORDER ALREADY MATCHES EXCEL")
                continue

            log(f"ORDERING {work_record['connector_name']}")

            move_center_stacks_to_final(prj, sht, work_record["connector_name"], work_record["staged_records"], work_record["global_final_sequence"])

            time.sleep(0.40)

            # Formatting occurs after a NORMAL COM disconnect + manual property toggle.
            # Never do the 77s-per-pin formatting lookup inside this worker.
            log(f"PIN ORDERED {work_record['connector_name']} - deferred symbol formatting")

            log(f"{work_record['connector_name']} COMPLETE")


    # Publish the formatter manifest ONLY after all pin movement succeeded.
    # SKIP connectors are included: their symbols may still need formatting.
    if not EDGE_STAGING_DRY_RUN:
        manifest_path = sys.argv[2]
        completed = create_formatter_manifest(CONNECTOR_WORK_INFO,
                                              SHEET_NAME_BY_ID, output_path)
        write_formatter_manifest(manifest_path, completed)
        section('PIN ORDERING FINISHED - E3 COM WORKER WILL NOW EXIT')
        log(f"Saved second-pass formatter map for {len(completed['connectors'])} connector(s).")
        log('Enable Equipment Identification in Zuken after this worker exits.')
        log('The COM-free launcher will provide a Run Symbol Formatter button.')

    # Normal and exceptional paths both release Python-side COM references.

finally:
    # Never os._exit(), terminate the COM Surrogate, or kill Zuken.
    # Python COM proxies are relinquished as the operation returns.
    import gc
    for _name in ('work_record', 'connector_group', 'selected', 'bindings',
                  'initial_snapshot', 'local_sym', 'pin', 'sym', 'text',
                  'attr', 'comp', 'dev', 'sht', 'prj', 'app'):
        if _name in globals():
            globals()[_name] = None
    gc.collect()
    print('Python-side Zuken COM references released (no process killed).')




block pin formatter:
"""E3.series - spreadsheet-driven, formatting-only connector pin repair.

Reads the SAME Excel layout as the existing Pin Arranger:
    B2 = duplicate-pin check (PASS)
    C4 = optional owning device name, e.g. PNL2
    C5 = connector count
    J2 = optional schematic page/sheet
    Connector #n: left column I+4*n, right column J+4*n (n starts at 0)
                  pin row count at right-column row 5
                  connector designation at right-column row 6
                  pin names in row 8 onward
    J1 (pin pitch) is intentionally ignored: NO pins are moved.

For each Excel connector, the script finds matching E3 pin symbols across ALL
its physical CABBLOCKs, including a split-off lower block. The TOP pin of
EACH side of EACH physical block shows device designation text type 12,
bold, height 0.20; every other pin on that same side hides type 12.
Local signal names (type 617) are made visible, black, bold, height 0.15,
and aligned by the ACTUAL E3 block side. Pin numbers (type 203) are made
visible and bold, matching the earlier arranger's formatter.

No symbol placement, resizing, removal, rebuilding, or pin-order changes.

Requires: Windows, Python, pywin32, openpyxl, running E3.series project.
For visible equipment identification, the E3 display property
"Equipment Identification" must be enabled in E3.

Usage:
    python E3_Split_Block_Pin_Formatter.py              # choose Excel with dialog
    python E3_Split_Block_Pin_Formatter.py pins.xlsm    # format immediately
    python E3_Split_Block_Pin_Formatter.py --dry-run pins.xlsm
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import math
import os
import sys
import traceback
from collections import defaultdict
from pathlib import Path

try:
    from openpyxl import load_workbook
except ImportError:
    raise SystemExit("Missing openpyxl. Install with: pip install openpyxl pywin32")

# Import win32com only when connecting so --help / offline tests work.

# ------------------------ Formatting settings ----------------------------
SIGNAL_NAME_TYPE = 617
PIN_NUMBER_TYPE = 203
DEVICE_DESIGNATION_TYPE = 12
BOLD_STYLE = 1                 # Same Zuken SetStyle(1) used in Pin Arranger
SIGNAL_NAME_HEIGHT = 0.15       # Same as the original formatter
TOP_DESIGNATION_HEIGHT = 0.20
BOLD_PIN_NUMBERS = True         # Preserve the old formatter's pin-number bolding
SIGNAL_BLACK = True             # Preserve black signal labels
SIGNAL_ALIGNMENT_BY_SIDE = True # LEFT -> 1; RIGHT -> 3, as in Pin Arranger
MAX_PIN_EDGE_OFFSET = 0.65     # Max allowed pin origin distance from block edge
BLOCK_LOCATION_TOLERANCE = 0.10
TOP_Y_TOLERANCE = 1e-4
COM_MISSING = -2147352572

LOG_STREAM = None


def log(message=""):
    line = str(message)
    print(line, flush=True)
    if LOG_STREAM is not None:
        LOG_STREAM.write(line + "\n")
        LOG_STREAM.flush()


def normalize_pin(value):
    if value is None:
        return None
    if isinstance(value, float) and math.isfinite(value) and value.is_integer():
        return str(int(value))
    val = str(value).strip()
    return val or None


def parse_id_result(value):
    """Support E3's (count, ID-array) COM return and normal ID arrays."""
    if value is None:
        return []
    if isinstance(value, (tuple, list)):
        if (len(value) >= 2 and isinstance(value[1], (tuple, list))
                and isinstance(value[0], (int, float))):
            ids = value[1]
            try:
                expected_count = int(value[0])
                ids = list(ids)
                if expected_count < len(ids):
                    # COM wrappers can prepend output/status values.
                    ids = ids[-expected_count:] if expected_count > 0 else []
            except (ValueError, TypeError):
                pass
        else:
            ids = value
    else:
        ids = [value]
    result = []
    for v in ids:
        try:
            i = int(v)
            if i > 0 and i != COM_MISSING:
                result.append(i)
        except (TypeError, ValueError):
            pass
    return result


def get_ids(method, *arguments):
    # E3's GetSymbolIds(None, 0) uses an output array BEFORE a mode argument;
    # GetViewIds(None), GetAllSheetIds(None) and some wrapper versions differ.
    attempts = ((None, *arguments), arguments, (*arguments, None))
    for args in attempts:
        try:
            value = parse_id_result(method(*args))
            if value:
                return value
        except Exception:
            continue
    return []


def select_id(obj, object_id):
    """Do not mistake E3 versions returning None from SetId for failure."""
    try:
        result = obj.SetId(int(object_id))
        if isinstance(result, (int, float)) and result == 0:
            return False
        if hasattr(obj, "GetId"):
            current = obj.GetId()
            if current is not None and int(current) != int(object_id):
                return False
        return True
    except Exception:
        return False


def optional_call(obj, name):
    try:
        return getattr(obj, name)()
    except Exception:
        return None


def read_sheet(prj, sht):
    rows = []
    ids = get_ids(prj.GetAllSheetIds)
    for sid in ids:
        if not select_id(sht, sid):
            continue
        name = str(sht.GetName()).strip()
        aliases = {name.casefold()}
        for method in ("GetSheetNumber", "GetNumber"):
            val = optional_call(sht, method)
            if val is not None:
                aliases.add(str(normalize_pin(val)).casefold())
        rows.append({"id": sid, "name": name, "aliases": aliases})
    if not rows:
        raise RuntimeError("E3 returned no sheets in the open project.")
    return rows


def same_sheet_label(label, expected):
    a, b = str(label).strip().casefold(), str(expected).strip().casefold()
    if a == b:
        return True
    return a.isdecimal() and b.isdecimal() and int(a) == int(b)


def read_excel(excel_path):
    wb = load_workbook(str(excel_path), read_only=True, data_only=True)
    try:
        ws = wb.active
        dup = ws["B2"].value
        if str(dup).strip().casefold() != "pass":
            raise ValueError(
                "Excel B2 duplicate-pin check is not PASS. As in Pin Arranger, "
                "recalculate/save the workbook in Excel and resolve any duplicate-pin errors."
            )
        num_raw = ws["C5"].value
        if num_raw is None or isinstance(num_raw, bool):
            raise ValueError("Excel C5 connector count is blank or invalid.")
        count = int(num_raw)
        if count < 0 or count > 2000:
            raise ValueError(f"Excel C5 connector count {count} is invalid.")
        owner = normalize_pin(ws["C4"].value)
        requested_page = normalize_pin(ws["J2"].value)
        records = []
        for idx in range(count):
            left_col = 9 + 4 * idx
            right_col = left_col + 1
            pin_rows_raw = ws.cell(row=5, column=right_col).value
            count_rows = int(pin_rows_raw) if pin_rows_raw is not None else 0
            if count_rows < 0 or count_rows > 10000:
                raise ValueError(f"Connector {idx + 1}: invalid pin row count {count_rows}.")
            conn_name = normalize_pin(ws.cell(row=6, column=right_col).value)
            pins_left, pins_right = [], []
            for r in range(8, 8 + count_rows):
                lp = normalize_pin(ws.cell(row=r, column=left_col).value)
                rp = normalize_pin(ws.cell(row=r, column=right_col).value)
                if lp is not None:
                    pins_left.append(lp)
                if rp is not None:
                    pins_right.append(rp)
            all_pins = pins_left + pins_right
            if not all_pins:
                continue
            if not conn_name:
                raise ValueError(f"Excel connector {idx + 1}: designation in row 6 is blank.")
            if len(all_pins) != len(set(all_pins)):
                raise ValueError(f"Excel connector {conn_name}: duplicate pin names in its columns.")
            records.append({
                "name": conn_name, "expected_pins": set(all_pins),
                "excel_count": len(all_pins), "left": pins_left, "right": pins_right
            })
        if not records:
            raise ValueError("No connector pins were specified in the spreadsheet.")
        names = [r["name"].casefold() for r in records]
        if len(set(names)) != len(names):
            raise ValueError("A connector designation appears more than once in Excel; "
                             "combine its listed pins into one connector group.")
        return owner, requested_page, records
    finally:
        wb.close()


def get_symbol_location(sym):
    try:
        raw = sym.GetSchemaLocation(None, None, None, None, None)
        if not isinstance(raw, (tuple, list)) or len(raw) < 3:
            return None
        return {"sheet_id": int(raw[0]), "x": float(raw[1]), "y": float(raw[2])}
    except Exception:
        return None


def get_symbol_area(sym):
    try:
        raw = sym.GetPlacedArea(None, None, None, None)
        if not isinstance(raw, (tuple, list)) or len(raw) < 5 or int(raw[0]) <= 0:
            return None
        l, b, r, t = map(float, raw[1:5])
        if not (r > l and t > b):
            return None
        return {"left": l, "bottom": b, "right": r, "top": t}
    except Exception:
        return None


def read_device_views(dev, device_id):
    if not select_id(dev, device_id):
        return []
    return get_ids(dev.GetViewIds)


def read_device_symbols(dev, view_id):
    if not select_id(dev, view_id):
        return []
    return get_ids(dev.GetSymbolIds, 0)


def find_cabblocks(prj, dev, owner_name, requested_page, sheets):
    allowed_pages = None
    if requested_page:
        eligible = [s for s in sheets if any(same_sheet_label(a, requested_page)
                                            for a in s["aliases"])]
        if len(eligible) != 1:
            choices = ", ".join(s["name"] for s in sheets)
            raise RuntimeError(f"J2 page {requested_page!r} matched {len(eligible)} E3 "
                               f"sheets. Available sheets: {choices}")
        allowed_pages = {eligible[0]["id"]}
    device_ids = get_ids(prj.GetAllDeviceIds)
    if owner_name:
        filtered = []
        for did in device_ids:
            if select_id(dev, did) and str(dev.GetName()).strip() == owner_name:
                filtered.append(did)
        if not filtered:
            raise RuntimeError(f"C4 device {owner_name!r} not found by E3 GetName().")
        device_ids = filtered
    blocks = {}
    sym = prj.CreateSymbolObject()
    for did in device_ids:
        for view_id in read_device_views(dev, did):
            if not select_id(dev, view_id):
                continue
            try:
                view_number = int(dev.GetViewNumber())
            except Exception:
                view_number = None
            for sid in read_device_symbols(dev, view_id):
                if sid in blocks or not select_id(sym, sid):
                    continue
                try:
                    kind = str(sym.GetSymbolTypeName() or "")
                except Exception:
                    continue
                if "CABBLOCK" not in kind.upper():
                    continue
                loc, area = get_symbol_location(sym), get_symbol_area(sym)
                if not loc or not area:
                    continue
                if allowed_pages is not None and loc["sheet_id"] not in allowed_pages:
                    continue
                blocks[sid] = {
                    "id": sid, "owner": did, "view_id": view_id,
                    "view_number": view_number,
                    "sheet_id": loc["sheet_id"], "kind": kind,
                    **area,
                }
    if not blocks:
        raise RuntimeError("No placed CABBLOCKs matched Excel's C4/J2 filters.")
    return list(blocks.values())


def inside_block(loc, block):
    tol = BLOCK_LOCATION_TOLERANCE
    return (loc is not None and loc["sheet_id"] == block["sheet_id"]
            and block["left"] - tol <= loc["x"] <= block["right"] + tol
            and block["bottom"] - tol <= loc["y"] <= block["top"] + tol)


def match_designation(actual, requested):
    """Actual J1A or J1A#1 may match Excel J1A, not J1A2."""
    if not actual:
        return False
    a, b = str(actual).strip().casefold(), str(requested).strip().casefold()
    return bool(b) and (a == b or
                        (a.startswith(b + "#") and a[len(b) + 1:].isdecimal()))


def scan_sheet_symbols(prj, sht, sheet_id):
    if not select_id(sht, sheet_id):
        raise RuntimeError(f"Could not select E3 sheet ID {sheet_id}.")
    ids = get_ids(sht.GetSymbolIds)
    if ids:
        return ids
    log(f"  Sheet {sheet_id} has no sheet-local symbol ID array; scanning project IDs instead.")
    return get_ids(prj.GetSymbolIds)


def read_pin_identity(prj, symbol_id, expected_connectors):
    sym = prj.CreateSymbolObject()
    if not select_id(sym, symbol_id):
        return None
    kind = str(optional_call(sym, "GetSymbolTypeName") or "")
    # The pin arranger uses MilSymbolReceptacle and MilBlockSymbolReceptacle.
    if "RECEPTACLE" not in kind.upper() or "¶" not in kind:
        return None
    loc = get_symbol_location(sym)
    if loc is None:
        return None
    txt = prj.CreateTextObject()
    label, pin_number, type_ids = None, None, defaultdict(list)
    for tid in get_ids(sym.GetTextIds):
        if not select_id(txt, tid):
            continue
        try:
            typ = int(txt.GetType())
        except Exception:
            continue
        type_ids[typ].append(tid)
        if typ in (DEVICE_DESIGNATION_TYPE, PIN_NUMBER_TYPE):
            raw_text = optional_call(txt, "GetText")
            if typ == DEVICE_DESIGNATION_TYPE and raw_text is not None:
                label = str(raw_text).strip()
            elif typ == PIN_NUMBER_TYPE:
                pin_number = normalize_pin(raw_text)
    if not pin_number or not label:
        return None
    if not any(match_designation(label, name) for name in expected_connectors):
        return None
    return {"symbol_id": symbol_id, "kind": kind, "location": loc,
            "pin_name": pin_number, "designation": label,
            "texts": dict(type_ids)}


def discover_pins(prj, sht, blocks, connectors):
    """Read ALL pins on ALL candidate blocks; no Excel pin completeness per block."""
    blocks_by_sheet = defaultdict(list)
    for b in blocks:
        blocks_by_sheet[b["sheet_id"]].append(b)
    expected_names = [x["name"] for x in connectors]
    pins = []
    for sheet_id, sheet_blocks in blocks_by_sheet.items():
        ids = scan_sheet_symbols(prj, sht, sheet_id)
        log(f"Scanning E3 sheet {sheet_id}: {len(ids)} symbols, "
            f"{len(sheet_blocks)} candidate CABBLOCKs")
        block_ids = {b["id"] for b in sheet_blocks}
        for symbol_id in set(ids):
            if symbol_id in block_ids:
                continue
            ident = read_pin_identity(prj, symbol_id, expected_names)
            if ident is None:
                continue
            matched_blocks = [b for b in sheet_blocks
                              if inside_block(ident["location"], b)]
            if not matched_blocks:
                continue
            # An E3 symbol in two CABBLOCK rectangles is ambiguous. Do not
            # arbitrarily assign its top designation to the wrong block.
            if len(matched_blocks) > 1:
                detail = [(b["id"], b["owner"]) for b in matched_blocks]
                raise RuntimeError(
                    f"Pin symbol {symbol_id} {ident['designation']} "
                    f"pin {ident['pin_name']} intersects multiple CABBLOCKs "
                    f"{detail}. No formatting applied."
                )
            pins.append({**ident, "block": matched_blocks[0]})
    return pins


def determine_side(pin):
    x = pin["location"]["x"]
    block = pin["block"]
    ld, rd = abs(x - block["left"]), abs(x - block["right"])
    if abs(ld - rd) < 0.05 or min(ld, rd) > MAX_PIN_EDGE_OFFSET:
        raise RuntimeError(
            f"Cannot determine LEFT/RIGHT for {pin['designation']} "
            f"pin {pin['pin_name']} (symbol {pin['symbol_id']}) in block {block['id']}; "
            f"X={x:.4f}, left={block['left']:.4f}, right={block['right']:.4f}. "
            "No formatting applied."
        )
    return "LEFT" if ld < rd else "RIGHT"


def make_plan(pins, connectors):
    """Require one unambiguous device owner for EACH Excel connector."""
    planned = []
    used_symbols = set()
    for conn in connectors:
        matching = [p for p in pins if match_designation(p["designation"], conn["name"])]
        by_owner = defaultdict(list)
        for p in matching:
            by_owner[p["block"]["owner"]].append(p)
        matching_owners = []
        for owner, owner_pins in by_owner.items():
            names = {p["pin_name"] for p in owner_pins}
            if conn["expected_pins"].issubset(names):
                matching_owners.append(owner)
        if len(matching_owners) != 1:
            diagnostics = [f"owner {owner}: {len(ps)} pins; "
                           f"Excel missing {sorted(conn['expected_pins'] - {p['pin_name'] for p in ps})[:12]}"
                           for owner, ps in by_owner.items()]
            raise RuntimeError(
                f"Connector {conn['name']}: expected one E3 device owner containing all "
                f"{conn['excel_count']} Excel pin(s), found {len(matching_owners)}. "
                f"Candidates: {' | '.join(diagnostics) or 'none'}. "
                "Set C4 (device) and/or J2 (page) in Excel if needed. "
                "NO formatting changes applied."
            )
        owner = matching_owners[0]
        selected = by_owner[owner]
        by_block_and_name = defaultdict(list)
        for p in selected:
            by_block_and_name[(p["block"]["id"], p["pin_name"])].append(p)
            if DEVICE_DESIGNATION_TYPE not in p["texts"]:
                raise RuntimeError(
                    f"Connector {conn['name']} pin {p['pin_name']} symbol {p['symbol_id']} "
                    "has no device-designation text (type 12). "
                    "Enable E3 'Equipment Identification' display property "
                    "and re-run. NO formatting changes applied."
                )
        duplicated = [k for k, group in by_block_and_name.items() if len(group) > 1]
        if duplicated:
            raise RuntimeError(f"Connector {conn['name']}: duplicated pin numbers "
                               f"inside physical block(s): {duplicated}. No changes applied.")
        by_side = defaultdict(list)
        for p in selected:
            p["side"] = determine_side(p)
            key = (p["block"]["id"], p["side"])
            by_side[key].append(p)
        for (block_id, side), group in by_side.items():
            group.sort(key=lambda p: (-p["location"]["y"], p["pin_name"]))
            if (len(group) > 1 and
                abs(group[0]["location"]["y"] - group[1]["location"]["y"]) <= TOP_Y_TOLERANCE):
                raise RuntimeError(f"Connector {conn['name']} block {block_id} {side}: "
                                   "more than one pin shares the highest Y position; "
                                   "cannot choose one top pin safely. No changes applied.")
            for row, p in enumerate(group):
                if p["symbol_id"] in used_symbols:
                    raise RuntimeError(f"Symbol {p['symbol_id']} was selected twice. No changes applied.")
                used_symbols.add(p["symbol_id"])
                planned.append({**p, "connector": conn["name"],
                                "top": row == 0, "row": row})
        log(f"  {conn['name']}: owner {owner}, {len(selected)} E3 pins "
            f"({conn['excel_count']} named in Excel), "
            f"{len({p['block']['id'] for p in selected})} physical block(s)")
    return sorted(planned, key=lambda p: (p["connector"], p["block"]["sheet_id"],
                                           p["block"]["id"], p["side"], p["row"]))


def apply_plan(prj, plan):
    """Set only font/visibility/alignment/colour; never move symbols or pins."""
    text = prj.CreateTextObject()
    completed = 0
    for p in plan:
        label = f"{p['connector']} pin {p['pin_name']} (symbol {p['symbol_id']})"
        type_ids = p["texts"]
        # GetTextIds identified these exact text objects during preflight.
        # Rebind each before use, so COM's current selection cannot drift.
        for text_id in type_ids.get(DEVICE_DESIGNATION_TYPE, []):
            if not select_id(text, text_id):
                raise RuntimeError(f"{label}: cannot select type-12 text ID {text_id}")
            if p["top"]:
                text.SetVisibility(1)
                text.SetStyle(BOLD_STYLE)
                text.SetHeight(TOP_DESIGNATION_HEIGHT)
            else:
                text.SetVisibility(0)
        for text_id in type_ids.get(SIGNAL_NAME_TYPE, []):
            if not select_id(text, text_id):
                raise RuntimeError(f"{label}: cannot select type-617 text ID {text_id}")
            text.SetVisibility(1)
            text.SetStyle(BOLD_STYLE)
            text.SetHeight(SIGNAL_NAME_HEIGHT)
            if SIGNAL_BLACK:
                text.SetColour(0)
            if SIGNAL_ALIGNMENT_BY_SIDE:
                text.SetAlignment(1 if p["side"] == "LEFT" else 3)
        if BOLD_PIN_NUMBERS:
            for text_id in type_ids.get(PIN_NUMBER_TYPE, []):
                if not select_id(text, text_id):
                    raise RuntimeError(f"{label}: cannot select type-203 text ID {text_id}")
                text.SetVisibility(1)
                text.SetStyle(BOLD_STYLE)
        completed += 1
        log(f"  FORMATTED {label} | block {p['block']['id']} "
            f"| {p['side']} row {p['row'] + 1} | "
            f"designation {'ON, 0.20 BOLD' if p['top'] else 'OFF'} "
            f"| signal labels {len(type_ids.get(SIGNAL_NAME_TYPE, []))}")
    return completed


def file_dialog():
    import tkinter as tk
    from tkinter import filedialog
    root = tk.Tk()
    root.withdraw()
    root.attributes("-topmost", True)
    path = filedialog.askopenfilename(
        title="Select the SAME Pin Arranger Excel workbook",
        filetypes=[("Excel macro workbook", "*.xlsm"),
                   ("Excel workbook", "*.xlsx"), ("All files", "*.*")],
    )
    root.destroy()
    return path


def message_box(title, message, error=False):
    try:
        import tkinter as tk
        from tkinter import messagebox
        root = tk.Tk()
        root.withdraw()
        root.attributes("-topmost", True)
        if error:
            messagebox.showerror(title, message, parent=root)
        else:
            messagebox.showinfo(title, message, parent=root)
        root.destroy()
    except Exception:
        pass


def run(excel_path, dry_run=False):
    global LOG_STREAM
    excel_path = Path(excel_path).resolve()
    if not excel_path.is_file():
        raise FileNotFoundError(f"Excel file does not exist: {excel_path}")
    log_path = excel_path.with_name("E3_Pin_Formatter_Log.txt")
    try:
        LOG_STREAM = open(log_path, "w", encoding="utf-8")
    except OSError:
        log_path = None
        LOG_STREAM = None
    try:
        log("=" * 72)
        log("E3 SPLIT-BLOCK PIN FORMATTER - FORMATTING ONLY, NO MOVEMENT")
        log(f"Workbook: {excel_path}")
        log("=" * 72)
        owner, page, connectors = read_excel(excel_path)
        log(f"Excel C4 device: {owner or '<any>'}")
        log(f"Excel J2 page: {page or '<any>'}")
        log(f"Excel connectors: {[c['name'] for c in connectors]}")
        log("Enable E3 View Properties > Equipment Identification before running if "
            "designation labels are not available/visible.")
        log("Excel side/row order is NOT used to move pins or select 'top'.")
        log("'Top' is determined by the highest placed pin on each physical block side.")
        try:
            import win32com.client as win32
        except ImportError as exc:
            raise RuntimeError("pywin32 is required. Install: pip install pywin32") from exc
        app = prj = dev = sht = None
        try:
            app = win32.gencache.EnsureDispatch("CT.Application")
            prj = app.CreateJobObject()
            dev = prj.CreateDeviceObject()
            sht = prj.CreateSheetObject()
            log("Connected to E3.series; all targeting is read-only until preflight passes.")
            sheets = read_sheet(prj, sht)
            blocks = find_cabblocks(prj, dev, owner, page, sheets)
            log(f"Found {len(blocks)} placed CABBLOCK(s) within C4/J2 restrictions.")
            pins = discover_pins(prj, sht, blocks, connectors)
            plan = make_plan(pins, connectors)
            log("\nPREFLIGHT PASSED. Target per physical CABBLOCK side:")
            by_side = defaultdict(list)
            for p in plan:
                by_side[(p["connector"], p["block"]["id"], p["side"])].append(p)
            for (connector, block_id, side), group in sorted(by_side.items()):
                top_pin = next(p for p in group if p["top"])
                log(f"  {connector} | block {block_id} | {side}: "
                    f"{len(group)} pins, top = {top_pin['pin_name']} "
                    f"at Y={top_pin['location']['y']:.3f}")
            log(f"Total symbols to format: {len(plan)}")
            if dry_run:
                log("DRY RUN: no changes were made to E3.")
            else:
                log("\nAPPLYING FORMATTING (no delete/move/resize operations)")
                try:
                    app.PutMessage("\nStarting split-block pin text formatter")
                except Exception:
                    pass
                completed = apply_plan(prj, plan)
                log(f"SUCCESS: formatted {completed} pin symbols. Save the project in E3.")
            summary = (f"{'Preview complete (no changes).' if dry_run else 'Pin formatting complete.'}\n\n"
                       f"{len(plan)} pins, {len(by_side)} physical block sides, "
                       f"{len(connectors)} connector(s).\n"
                       f"No pins were moved or recreated.\n\n"
                       "Ensure E3's 'Equipment Identification' display property "
                       "is enabled so device designations appear.\n"
                       "Save your E3 drawing.\n\n"
                       f"Log: {log_path or 'console only'}")
            message_box("E3 Pin Formatter", summary)
            return 0
        finally:
            sht = dev = prj = app = None
    except Exception:
        log("\nERROR: " + traceback.format_exc())
        raise
    finally:
        if LOG_STREAM is not None:
            LOG_STREAM.close()
        LOG_STREAM = None


def main(argv=None):
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description="Format pins across split E3 CABBLOCKs, without moving pins")
    parser.add_argument("excel", nargs="?", help="Pin Arranger .xlsm / .xlsx (file picker if omitted)")
    parser.add_argument("--dry-run", action="store_true", help="Resolve and preview all pin targets; do not write to E3")
    args = parser.parse_args(argv)
    path = args.excel or file_dialog()
    if not path:
        log("No Excel workbook selected.")
        return 1
    try:
        return run(path, dry_run=args.dry_run)
    except Exception as exc:
        log(f"FAILED: {exc}")
        message_box("E3 Pin Formatter - Error", str(exc) + "\n\nSee E3_Pin_Formatter_Log.txt next to the workbook.", error=True)
        return 1


if __name__ == "__main__":
    sys.exit(main())





block signal adder:
"""Zuken E3.series: Excel J2 local-signal importer.

Version 2.6: Display missing pin-owned Local Signal Name attributes before making their placed text visible; retain v2.4 completion/error windows.
Looks up device designation text on connector-owned symbols in addition
 to the pin; prints before/after text style; no log file.
Run from E3 or Python, choose one Excel workbook, and immediately apply.
Diagnostics are captured in memory; on success only a small completion window appears.
On error, detailed diagnostics are displayed with a Copy button. No log file.

Excel worksheet: any name; uses the matching data tab (or active tab).
E3 drawing sheet 'J2' is used only to locate the block.

Worksheet cells:
    B2    = duplicate-pin status/check (if present)
    C4    = block device designation
    C5    = connector count N
    B7:B(6+N) = connector names
    C7:C(6+N) = connector types (additional identity information)
    I:J   = connector 1: pin, local signal
    M:N   = connector 2: pin, local signal
    Q:R   = connector 3, etc., all starting on row 7
    K:L, O:P, ... are intentionally unused spacer columns

Only rows with BOTH a pin value and a nonblank local signal are imported.
The header row (Pin Number / Local Signal Name) is ignored automatically.
Blank signal cells do not clear E3 data. Duplicate pin names within a
connector are the only spreadsheet/model integrity check. E3 object ownership
is additionally checked before writes to avoid modifying the wrong object.

IMPORTANT: This version NEVER calls Pin.SetSignalName() or writes type-7
reference signal text because those can change the electrical net and
propagate into other connectors. It writes only to an explicitly existing
pin-owned attribute named Local Signal Name (or listed direct alias).
When no such field exists, it performs READ-ONLY diagnostics and prints
what E3 exposes, without changing signal connectivity.
It calls Attribute.DisplayAttribute on hidden pin-owned Local Signal Name
attributes, then formats actual placed pin-view text type 617 after matching
its logical pin and connector. Type-12 designation is enabled and bolded on the unique
top placed pin identified by split-position marker type 213 (1/N).
Unlike E3 electrical net signal, these changes are only text styling.

Test in a copy of the E3 project. COM support cannot be run on non-Windows hosts.
Requires: py -m pip install pywin32 openpyxl
Optional: Zuken's version-matched 'e3series' Python package.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from collections import Counter
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from datetime import datetime
from contextlib import redirect_stdout, redirect_stderr
import io
import sys
import warnings
from pathlib import Path
import math
from statistics import median
import re
import traceback
import time

SHEET_NAME = 'J2'  # E3 drawing-page identifier, NOT a required Excel tab name
VERSION = '2.6-show-pin-attributes'
FIRST_ROW = 7
FIRST_PIN_COL = 9  # I
CONNECTOR_COL_STEP = 4  # I:J, M:N, Q:R, U:V, ...
LOCAL_SIGNAL_TEXT_TYPE = 617  # Displayed Local Signal Name on placed pin view
DEVICE_DESIGNATION_TEXT_TYPE = 12
TEXT_HEIGHT_IN = 0.15
BOLD_STYLE = 1
NO_VALUE = -2147352572  # COM's DISP_E_PARAMNOTFOUND


class ConsoleLines(list):
    """Pass diagnostics through the host interpreter with plain print()."""
    def __init__(self, lines=()):
        super().__init__()
        self.extend(lines)

    def append(self, line):
        line = str(line)
        super().append(line)
        print(line, flush=True)

    def extend(self, lines):
        for line in lines:
            self.append(line)

    def __iadd__(self, lines):
        self.extend(lines)
        return self


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class PinEntry:
    connector_index: int
    connector_name: str
    connector_type: str
    pin_name: str
    signal_name: str
    cell: str


@dataclass
class ImportSheet:
    workbook_path: Path
    block_name: str
    connector_count: int
    entries: list[PinEntry]
    check_b2: object
    duplicate_problems: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    worksheet_name: str = ''


@dataclass
class ResolvedPin:
    entry: PinEntry
    connector_id: int
    pin_id: int


def as_text(value):
    if value is None:
        return ''
    if isinstance(value, float) and value.is_integer():
        return str(int(value))
    return str(value).strip()


def device_key(value):
    """Treat a leading E3 '-' designation marker as optional, not arbitrary substrings."""
    return as_text(value).lstrip('-').casefold()


def connector_keys(value):
    """Recognize a connector with or without its block prefix ('-A1.J1')."""
    v = device_key(value)
    return {v, v.rsplit('.', 1)[-1]}


def duplicate_flag_b2(value):
    """Check commonly used B2 duplicate-pin outcomes, not unrelated statuses."""
    if isinstance(value, bool):
        return bool(value)
    if isinstance(value, (int, float)):
        return value > 0
    s = as_text(value).lower().replace('_', ' ').strip()
    if not s or s in ('ok', 'pass', 'passed', 'false', 'none', 'clear', 'no', '0'):
        return False
    if any(x in s for x in ('no duplicate', '0 duplicate', 'without duplicate', 'duplicate-free')):
        return False
    return s in ('error', 'fail', 'failed', 'true') or 'duplicate' in s


def select_data_sheet(wb):
    """Choose the Excel data tab; J2 refers to the *E3 page*, not Excel.

    Prefer a populated J2 tab if present, then the active Excel tab,
    otherwise the first tab with a designation in C4 and connector count C5.
    """
    def has_inputs(sh):
        if not as_text(sh['C4'].value):
            return False
        try:
            n = float(sh['C5'].value)
            return n >= 1 and n.is_integer()
        except (TypeError, ValueError):
            return False

    matches = [sh for sh in wb.worksheets if has_inputs(sh)]
    if not matches:
        tabs = ', '.join(repr(sh.title) for sh in wb.worksheets)
        raise ValueError(
            'Could not find an Excel worksheet with the device designation '
            'in C4 and connector count in C5. Excel tab names found: '
            + (tabs or '(none)')
        )
    for sh in matches:
        if sh.title.casefold() == SHEET_NAME.casefold():
            return sh
    if wb.active in matches:
        return wb.active
    return matches[0]


def parse_workbook(filename: Path) -> ImportSheet:
    """Read Excel values without modifying the workbook or recalculating it."""
    from openpyxl import load_workbook
    wb = load_workbook(str(filename), read_only=False, data_only=True)
    try:
        sh = select_data_sheet(wb)
        block = as_text(sh['C4'].value)
        if not block:
            raise ValueError(f'{sh.title}!C4 (device designation) is empty.')
        raw_count = sh['C5'].value
        try:
            connector_count = int(raw_count)
            if connector_count < 1 or float(raw_count) != connector_count:
                raise ValueError()
        except (ValueError, TypeError):
            raise ValueError(f'{sh.title}!C5 must contain the number of connectors.') from None

        entries = []
        problems = []
        for ci in range(connector_count):
            connector_name = as_text(sh.cell(FIRST_ROW + ci, 2).value)
            connector_type = as_text(sh.cell(FIRST_ROW + ci, 3).value)
            # Pin/signal boxes are separated by two blank columns: I:J, M:N, Q:R, ...
            pin_col = FIRST_PIN_COL + CONNECTOR_COL_STEP * ci
            signal_col = pin_col + 1
            pin_col_letter = sh.cell(FIRST_ROW, pin_col).column_letter
            signal_col_letter = sh.cell(FIRST_ROW, signal_col).column_letter
            if not connector_name:
                # A missing connector name is a matching problem, not a model check.
                problems.append(f'Connector {ci + 1}: blank name at B{FIRST_ROW + ci}.')
            seen_pins = {}
            # Respect data even if some pin rows are intentionally left blank.
            # max_row is set by the actual workbook, not by the declared pin count.
            for row in range(FIRST_ROW, sh.max_row + 1):
                pin = as_text(sh.cell(row, pin_col).value)
                signal = as_text(sh.cell(row, signal_col).value)
                # Templates commonly place headings directly in I7/J7, M7/N7, ...
                # Neither the headings nor their formatting should become an E3 pin.
                if pin.casefold().replace(' ', '') in ('pinnumber', 'pinname', 'pin#') \
                        and signal.casefold().replace(' ', '') in ('localsignalname', 'localsignal', 'signalname', 'signal'):
                    continue
                if pin:
                    # Check duplicates even where a local signal is blank;
                    # this is the one requested model/spreadsheet check.
                    if pin.casefold() in seen_pins:
                        problems.append(f'DUPLICATE pin {pin!r} in connector {connector_name!r} '
                                        f'at {pin_col_letter}{seen_pins[pin.casefold()]} '
                                        f'and {pin_col_letter}{row}.')
                        continue
                    seen_pins[pin.casefold()] = row
                if not signal:
                    continue  # never clear or reformat an unspecified E3 pin
                if not pin:
                    problems.append(f'{signal_col_letter}{row}: signal without a pin number.')
                    continue
                entries.append(PinEntry(ci, connector_name, connector_type,
                                        pin, signal, f'{signal_col_letter}{row}'))

        sheet_data = ImportSheet(Path(filename), block, connector_count,
                                 entries, sh['B2'].value, problems, sh.title)
        # A B2 formula may have no cached value when Excel has not calculated.
        # The actual duplicate scan above remains authoritative in that case.
        if duplicate_flag_b2(sheet_data.check_b2):
            sheet_data.duplicate_problems.insert(
                0, f'{sh.title}!B2 reports duplicate pins: ' + repr(sheet_data.check_b2))
        return sheet_data
    finally:
        wb.close()


def require_no_duplicate_pins(data):
    dup = [p for p in data.duplicate_problems if 'DUPLICATE' in p.upper()]
    if dup:
        raise ValueError('Duplicate pin check failed:\n' + '\n'.join(dup))


def flatten_positive_ids(obj):
    if isinstance(obj, (list, tuple)):
        vals = []
        for x in obj:
            vals.extend(flatten_positive_ids(x))
        return vals
    if type(obj) is int and 0 < obj < 2147483647:
        return [obj]
    return []


def split_com_result(result):
    # E3 methods generally return (number_of_ids, ID-array).
    if isinstance(result, (tuple, list)) and result and type(result[0]) is int:
        return result[0], flatten_positive_ids(result[1:])
    return (result if type(result) is int else None), []


def com_ids(obj, method_name, text_type=None):
    """Read E3 arrays using both official-wrapper and direct pywin32 conventions."""
    method = getattr(obj, method_name, None)
    if method is None:
        return []
    variants = [(), (None,)] if text_type is None else [(text_type,), (None, text_type), ([], text_type)]
    for args in variants:
        try:
            raw = method(*args)
            count, ids = split_com_result(raw)
            if ids:
                return list(dict.fromkeys(ids[:count] if count and count > 0 else ids))
            if isinstance(raw, (tuple, list)) and len(raw) > 1 and all(type(i) is int and i > 0 for i in raw):
                return list(dict.fromkeys(raw))
        except Exception:
            pass
    # The Python COM interface sometimes requires an explicit output array.
    try:
        import pythoncom
        import win32com.client as wc
    except ImportError:
        return []
    for variant_type, seed in (
        (pythoncom.VT_BYREF | pythoncom.VT_VARIANT, None),
        (pythoncom.VT_BYREF | pythoncom.VT_ARRAY | pythoncom.VT_I4, []),
    ):
        try:
            arr = wc.VARIANT(variant_type, seed)
            result = method(arr) if text_type is None else method(arr, text_type)
            _, returned_ids = split_com_result(result)
            ids = returned_ids or flatten_positive_ids(arr.value)
            if ids:
                return list(dict.fromkeys(ids))
        except Exception:
            pass
    return []


def related_ids(obj, candidates, text_type=None):
    for method in candidates:
        ids = com_ids(obj, method, text_type=text_type)
        if ids:
            return ids
    return []


def get_scalar(obj, methods):
    for name in methods:
        method = getattr(obj, name, None)
        if method is None:
            continue
        try:
            result = method()
        except Exception:
            continue
